## concept_specimen_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_specimen_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

标本是一种在探索星系的过程中找到的文物。\n\n可以在['concept_megastructure_grand_archive']展览以提供全帝国加成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:13`

### English

Specimens are artifacts found while exploring the galaxy.\n\nThey can be exhibited in a ['concept_megastructure_grand_archive'] to provide empire-wide bonuses.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:14`

它产生的强腐蚀性气体荼毒了星球的大片区域使其难以居住。这种气体的一种弱化版本已经被用于高效杀虫剂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:59`

### English

Its resulting highly corrosive gas has rendered large swathes of the planet toxic and uninhabitable. A less potent version has since been synthesized into a form of effective pesticide.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:67`

## adakkaria_propaganda_poster

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadakkaria_propaganda_poster&lang=en)

阿达卡利亚宣传海报

### English

adAkkaria Propaganda Poster

## adakkaria_propaganda_poster_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadakkaria_propaganda_poster_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这张海报宣告着阿达卡利亚公约过去的荣光

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:61`

### English

This laminated poster proclaims the glory of the adAkkaria Convention

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:70`

## adakkaria_propaganda_poster_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadakkaria_propaganda_poster_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

上面描绘的是一些看起来自信、骄傲且勇敢的公民。他们的触手伸向天空，仿佛抓住了一个只有它们才能决定的未来。戴着华丽面具的机器实体模糊的轮廓从高空俯视着他们。标语写着：“阿达卡利亚仁善公约——让恩主为我们骄傲”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:62`

### English

Depicted are citizens who look self-assured, proud and brave. Their tentacle limbs reach out to the sky as if grasping hold of a future decided only by them. From above, vague outlines of machine entities wearing ornate masks look down upon them. The slogan states: 'adAkkaria Convention of Benevolence - Making our Benefactors Proud'.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:71`

顾问核心

### English

Advisor Core

## advisor_core_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor_core_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这是在星界裂隙找到的一台复杂的顾问无人机在被拆解后剩下的部分

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:64`

### English

The only part left intact after disassembling a complex advisor drone found within an Astral Rift

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:74`

## advisor_core_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor_core_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这枚核心存储着数量惊人的琐碎信息，但其软件架构之复杂，为我们的计算机科学研究激发了一波创新浪潮。\n[EVENT_TARGET_0.explorer.GetAdvisorCoreAdditionnalQuote]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:65`

### English

This core contains an incredible amount of trivial information, but the complexity of its software architecture has inspired a wave of innovation in our computer science research efforts.\n[EVENT_TARGET_0.explorer.GetAdvisorCoreAdditionnalQuote]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:75`

## advisor_core_desc_tutorial_on

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aadvisor_core_desc_tutorial_on&lang=en)

§L“你不会关掉我的，对不对？”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:66`

### English

§L"You wouldn't shut me down, would you?"§!

## advisor_core_desc_tutorial_off

### English

Alien Brain Scans

## alien_brain_scans_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_brain_scans_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的庇护所里发现的一个来自早已灭绝外星物种的神经模式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:69`

### English

Archived neural patterns from a long-dead alien race found within a vault on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:80`

## alien_brain_scans_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_brain_scans_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这个失落的文明把这段信息储存下来，希望能把他们的意识转移到数字数据库中实现永生来抵御行星级灾难。许多扫描都附有个人人格的笔记，以及最熟悉他们的人的证词。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:70`

### English

The forgotten civilization stored this data in the hope of transferring their consciousness to digital repositories and living on as immortals in the face of a planet-wide disaster. Many of the scans are accompanied with notes on individual personalities, including testimonies from those who knew them best.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:81`

## alien_dining_set

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_dining_set&lang=en)

### 中文

青釉餐具

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:71`

### English

Jade Dining Set

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:83`

## alien_dining_set_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_dining_set_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在一颗人工小行星中保存着的一套精致的餐具

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

An elegant dining set found preserved inside of an artificial asteroid

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:84`

## alien_dining_set_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_dining_set_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些陶器的成型、烧制和上釉采用了一系列复杂的工艺技术。其结构上的微小不一致表明其为手工制造。材料分析显示，这些特别精美的瓷器的主人实行高酸性饮食，主要来自某种核果聚集的水果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:73`

### English

A complex series of crafting techniques were used to shape, fire, and glaze the crockery. Minor inconsistencies in its structure imply an artisanal creation process. Material analysis shows evidence that the owner of these particular fine wares practiced a highly acidic diet derived mostly from drupelet-aggregate fruit.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:85`

## alien_jewelry

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_jewelry&lang=en)

### 中文

外星珠宝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

Alien Jewelry

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:87`

## alien_jewelry_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_jewelry_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一组用未知材料制成的精致外星珠宝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:75`

### English

A collection of elegant alien jewelry made of unknown material

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:88`

## alien_jewelry_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_jewelry_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这组石器的性质和色彩殊异，硅酸盐和碳基宝石小心地镶嵌在铂合金中。有些的内部还雕刻，但我们无法对其进行翻译。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:76`

### English

The stones vary wildly in origin and color; a mix of silicate and carbon-based gems set carefully within platinum alloys. There are engravings on the inner portions of some of the pieces, but we have no means of translating them.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:89`

## alien_toy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_toy&lang=en)

塑料玩具

### English

Plastic Toy

## alien_toy_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_toy_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在一座废弃工厂中发现的简单玩具

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

A simple plaything found among the ruins of a derelict factory

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:92`

## alien_toy_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_toy_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这只是用同一套钢模具批量生产出来的数千件物品之一，它被岁月侵蚀得破碎不堪。除了装饰和玩耍，它似乎没有别的作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

Brittle and cracked with age, this object was one of thousands mass produced from only a small set of steel molds. It seems to have had no purpose other than ornamentation and play.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:93`

## altar_to_the_elder_one

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaltar_to_the_elder_one&lang=en)

长者祭坛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

Altar to the Elder One

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:95`

## altar_to_the_elder_one_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaltar_to_the_elder_one_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在一个神圣而古老的厅堂里发现的一台超自然机器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

A preternatural machine found within a hallowed, ancient chamber

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:96`

## altar_to_the_elder_one_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaltar_to_the_elder_one_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

即使在现在已经废弃，它仍然散发着一种不可测量的灵能光环。有的人称现在还能听到它的低语，话语中在引诱着灵魂回到[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

Even in its now defunct state, it radiates a singular aura of unmeasurable psionic energy. Some say its tempting whispers can still be heard, aiming to coax wayward souls into returning to [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:97`

## aluminium_plaque

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaluminium_plaque&lang=en)

### 中文

铝制雕版

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:83`

### English

Aluminum Plaque

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:99`

## aluminium_plaque_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaluminium_plaque_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一枚简单的雕版，发现于由[EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetPreFTLLower]哺乳类文明发射的无人机上

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

### English

A simple plaque found on a drone launched by [EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetAAnPreFTLLower] [EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetPreFTLLower] mammalian civilization

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:100`

## aluminium_plaque_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaluminium_plaque_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

其表面蚀刻着一些奇怪的几何形状和他们物种的粗糙画像。工艺相当拙劣，但应该光是把这个物体发射到太空就已经代表了他们文明的重大努力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

The surface is etched with some odd geometric shapes and crude images of their species. The craftsmanship is rather poor, but it must have represented a significant effort from their civilization to simply launch this object into space.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:101`

## ammoniaphytus

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aammoniaphytus&lang=en)

### 中文

氨藻

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

Ammoniaphytus

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:103`

## ammoniaphytus_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aammoniaphytus_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

该植物组织中的水溶氨含量对大部分碳基生物来说都是危险的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

The levels of aqueous ammonia in this plant's tissue would be dangerous to most carbon-based lifeforms

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:104`

## ammoniaphytus_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aammoniaphytus_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

围绕在其容器附近的气氛足以使大多数智慧生物在几秒钟内失去知觉。关于这样一个标本为何能存在，我们最伟大的头脑中引发了好奇和争论，也在其他人心中勾起了邪恶的念头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

The immediate atmosphere surrounding its containment renders most sapient organisms unconscious within a few seconds. How such a specimen could exist has sparked intrigue and debate among our greatest minds, and nefarious plans among others.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:105`

## amoeba_flagella_gland

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aamoeba_flagella_gland&lang=en)

### 中文

变形虫鞭毛腺

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

Amoeba Flagella Gland

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:107`

## amoeba_flagella_gland_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aamoeba_flagella_gland_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一具太空变形虫的次级生殖器官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

A secondary reproductive organ from a Space Amoeba

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:108`

## amoeba_flagella_gland_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aamoeba_flagella_gland_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它可以产出称为“鞭毛”的独立有机体，在变形虫捕猎时蜂拥而上淹没猎物。鞭毛生长很快，但基因的不稳定性使其无法完全成熟。太空变形虫要产出真正的后等待需要向鞭毛中注入一种特殊的酶来激发快速的变形。这种繁育方式的选择过程目前尚未明确。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:91`

### English

It produces independent organisms known as "flagella", aggressive spawn that swarm and overwhelm prey as the Amoeba hunts for food. The flagella grow quickly, but genetic instabilities leave them unable to fully mature. To produce a true offspring, Space Amoeba inject a specialized enzyme into the flagella which triggers rapid metamorphosis. The selection process for this reproduction method is unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:109`

## ancient_bust

## ancient_laser_pointer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_laser_pointer&lang=en)

### 中文

远古指星器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

Ancient Laser Pointer

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:119`

## ancient_laser_pointer_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_laser_pointer_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种高度精确的光束投影仪，可以在星系尺度上标记天体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

A highly accurate beam projector that can mark celestial bodies from distances on the galactic scale

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:120`

## ancient_laser_pointer_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_laser_pointer_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这台由恩主留下的紫外线激光器是古老文明的存在的具体证据，并以此闻名。值得注意的是，它还能工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:100`

### English

Left behind by the Benefactors, this ultraviolet laser is notable as concrete evidence of a bygone civilization. Remarkably, it still works.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:121`

## ancient_nanites

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_nanites&lang=en)

### 中文

远古纳米机器人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:101`

### English

Ancient Nanites

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:123`

## ancient_nanites_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_nanites_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这些高度灵活的纳米机器人是在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上发现的，它们在数千年前被一个失落的文明投递至此

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

These highly versatile nanites were discovered on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], having been delivered there thousands of years ago by a forgotten civilization

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:124`

## ancient_nanites_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_nanites_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些设备缺乏关键的安全特性，显然其制造者从未担心他人会发现并将其改作他用。[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetSpeciesName]曾利用它们将行星环境改造为更宜居的环境，如今更将这支纳米虫群据为己有，但其最初的建造方法仍是未解之谜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

Lacking crucial security features, there was clearly no concern that others might come upon these devices and repurpose them. Having used them to terraform the planet into a more habitable environment, the [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetSpeciesName] have taken ownership of the swarm, though the methods behind their original construction remain a mystery.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:125`

## ancient_nuclear_armament

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_nuclear_armament&lang=en)

### 中文

远古核武器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

Ancient Nuclear Armament

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:127`

## ancient_nuclear_armament_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_nuclear_armament_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上发现的这枚3500万吨级氢弹是在一场摧毁了这颗行星曾经居住者的战争后被遗留下来的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Found on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], this 35 megaton hydrogen bomb was left over after the war that destroyed the previous inhabitants of the planet

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:128`

## ancient_nuclear_armament_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_nuclear_armament_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

幸运的是，现在依旧可以解除这件老掉牙武器的武装。我们的翻译员将弹体上的文字与星球上发现的其他文物进行了比对分析，认为上面写的是“最大男孩”之类的东西。真奇怪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

Fortunately, disarming this archaic munition was still possible. The text painted onto the munition was analyzed by our translators against other artifacts found on the planet and it is believed it says something along the lines of "The Biggest of the Boys." How odd.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:129`

## ancient_political_memoir

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_political_memoir&lang=en)

### 中文

远古政治回忆录

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

Ancient Political Memoir

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:131`

## ancient_political_memoir_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_political_memoir_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这是关于一位远古星系政客的传记

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

The biography of an ancient galactic politician

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:132`

## ancient_political_memoir_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_political_memoir_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

利兹特露丝是萨尔沙那的总督，这是古老的哈沃斯温室文明的一座小小的殖民地。她是一个称为商业自由党的极权主义派系利的一个小人物，她以反对从外星势力进口食品的强硬立场而闻名。在一系列丑闻引起的辞职之后，她接任了哈沃斯温室的统治者，但这并没有持续多久。她支持翻修备受喜爱的哈沃斯钟楼的声明几乎在首都引发了一场全面的暴动，她很快被商业自由运动的另一名成员取代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

Leez'Terus was the governor of Sarthana, a small colony within the ancient Harworth Conservatory civilization. A minor figure within the Freedom For Business totalitarian faction, she was known for her hardline stance against importing food supplies from foreign galactic powers. Following a series of scandal-driven resignations, she became the ruler of the Harwoth Conservatory, but it was not to last. Her declaration in support of renovating the beloved Harworthic Clock Tower almost caused a full-on revolt in the capital, and she was swiftly replaced by another member of the Freedom For Business cohort.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:133`

## ancient_rift_sphere

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_rift_sphere&lang=en)

### 中文

远古裂隙探球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

Ancient Rift Sphere

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:135`

## ancient_rift_sphere_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_rift_sphere_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一艘穿过星界裂隙并且坠毁在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的远古太空舰船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

An ancient space-faring vessel that traveled through an Astral Rift and crash-landed on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:136`

## ancient_rift_sphere_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_rift_sphere_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这艘匆忙组装的球体舰船被深埋在行星地下数个世纪，它曾将[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetSpeciesName]的远祖带到这个星系。它的许多组成材料已经被拆毁成零件，但核心结构仍基本保持完好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

Buried deep beneath the planet's surface for centuries, this hastily-assembled spherical ship is what brought the forefathers of the [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetSpeciesName] to this galaxy. Much of its material has since been scavenged for parts, but its core structure remains mostly intact.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:137`

## ancient_satellite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_satellite&lang=en)

### 中文

远古卫星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

Ancient Satellite

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:139`

## ancient_satellite_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_satellite_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

被[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的远古无人机当做礼物送过来的一颗遗弃卫星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

A derelict satellite offered as a 'gift' by the ancient mechanical drones in [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:140`

## ancient_satellite_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_satellite_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

其数据库多处老化损坏，但人口普查记录和出生登记仍然完好。这些关于全星球人口行为的信息，为[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetAdjective]殖民地的可持续增长提供了参考。历经多年朽坏仍能部分运转，足证其构造之坚固。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

Its data banks are decayed and corrupted in many places, but current census records and birth registries are intact. This information on the population behavior across the planet provided information on how the [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetAdjective] colony could grow sustainably. Its partial functionality after years of decay is a testament to its sturdy construction.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:141`

## ancient_sensor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_sensor&lang=en)

### 中文

远古热传感器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

Ancient Heat Sensor

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:143`

## ancient_sensor_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_sensor_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这个传感器伪装成一颗小行星，用于生成星系热度图并且将这些数据传输给一个遥远的古老帝国

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

Disguised within an asteroid, this sensor was used to generate galactic heatmaps and transfer this data to a distant ancient empire

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:144`

## ancient_sensor_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_sensor_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它能够跟踪单个恒星系内的温度变化，并通过红外辐射追踪航天器的运动。然而，它的主要功能似乎是预测和测量恒星风暴和恒星黑子的形成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:118`

### English

It is capable of tracking temperature variants within individual systems as well as track the movement of spacecraft via infrared radiation. However, its primary purpose seems to be in predicting and measuring solar storms and the formation of sunspots.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:145`

## asteroid_fossils

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_fossils&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星化石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:119`

### English

Asteroid Fossils

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:147`

## asteroid_fossils_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_fossils_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

同位素定年揭示了这些水生类化石有数百万年的历史

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

Carbon dating revealed that these aquatic fossils are several million years old

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:148`

## asteroid_fossils_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_fossils_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它们是一些有壳水生无脊椎动物的矿化残骸，我们推测它们有一种独特的中空管，用来将自己固定在水下的热泉喷口上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

They are the mineralized remains of shelled aquatic invertebrates, with a unique hollow tube we speculate was used to anchor themselves to underwater thermal vents.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:149`

## ballad_of_the_sable_knight

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aballad_of_the_sable_knight&lang=en)

### 中文

黑骑士之歌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

Ballad of the Sable Knight

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:151`

## ballad_of_the_sable_knight_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aballad_of_the_sable_knight_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

§d在竞斗前夕到来的黑骑士§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

§dThe Sable Knight came on the eve of a tourney§!

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:152`

## ballad_of_the_sable_knight_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aballad_of_the_sable_knight_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

§d\n黑骑士，穿怪甲，不远万里到此地。\n竞技场上战王牌，决斗公平又讲理。\n刀光来，剑影去，比试起来不一般。\n王牌心有武圣魂，守势好似金钟罩。\n黑骑身披中子甲，挡锐有如铁布衫。\n异客不敌居下风，束戈卷甲拱手降。\n酣斗既了归星舰，遁入虫洞回他乡……§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

§d\nClad in strange armor, they made quite a journey.\nThey challenged our champion in duel just and fair,\nCombat began and sparks filled the air.\nOur champion's guard was strong as expected,\nBut the challenger's armor each blow it deflected.\nThe challenger yielded, a loss without shame,\nThen returned to the wormhole from whence they came...§!

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:153`

## beauty_of_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abeauty_of_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]之美

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

Beauty of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:155`

## beauty_of_planet_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abeauty_of_planet_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上精心打造的优雅环境改造工程，可极视听之乐

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:126`

### English

The precisely crafted elegance of the terraforming efforts on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] was designed to be a treat for all senses

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:156`

## beauty_of_planet_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abeauty_of_planet_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它应用了一套算法系统，通过精心培育对行星的自然生态系统进行全面的分析并加以增强。选用最鲜艳的花朵、最有营养的水果和最香甜的草木，构成了一副活力四射的乌托邦般的景象。要是自然也有如此效率就好了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:127`

### English

A system of algorithms was used to thoroughly analyze and enhance the planet's natural ecosystems with careful cultivation.  By selecting only the brightest flowers, most nutritious fruit, and sweetest herbs, the landscape was transformed successfully into a vibrant utopia. If only nature could be so efficient.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:157`

## black_hole_chromatic_anomaly

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_hole_chromatic_anomaly&lang=en)

### 中文

黑洞色异常

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:128`

### English

Black Hole Chromatic Anomaly

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:159`

## black_hole_chromatic_anomaly_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_hole_chromatic_anomaly_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]内捕获的一副令人叹为观止的景象

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:129`

### English

A seemingly fantastical image captured within [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:160`

## black_hole_chromatic_anomaly_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_hole_chromatic_anomaly_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

由于[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]吸积盘的特殊化学组成，这个星系的黑洞产生了一种色差效应，五光十色的幻彩盈满了局部空间。真可谓深空中绝无仅有的奇景。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:130`

### English

Due to the chemical composition of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]'s accretion disk, this system's black hole gained a chromatic aberration, creating a kaleidoscopic light effect across local space. A truly unique spectacle in deep space.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:161`

## black_needle_ceremonial_sword

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_needle_ceremonial_sword&lang=en)

### 中文

黑针佩剑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:131`

### English

Black Needle Ceremonial Sword

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:163`

## black_needle_ceremonial_sword_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_needle_ceremonial_sword_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这件精心打造的武器由银羽佩戴，他是黑针海盗团的创始人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:132`

### English

This meticulously crafted weapon was carried by Silver Quill, the founder of the Black Needle pirates

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:164`

## black_needle_ceremonial_sword_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_needle_ceremonial_sword_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这是该犯罪组织的第一笔横财，它是作为一种地位的象征而打造，而非实战用途。它由钛钢制成，刀柄上刻有“刺破帷幕”的字样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:133`

### English

Commissioned with the profits of the criminal organization's first major windfall, it was created as a status symbol and served no practical fighting purpose. Made from a steel-titanium alloy, the phrase "Pierce the Veil" is inscribed along its hilt.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:165`

## black_needle_ledger

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_needle_ledger&lang=en)

### 中文

黑针账本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

Black Needle Ledger

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:167`

## black_needle_ledger_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_needle_ledger_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这本账簿记录了黑针海盗团几千年来在星系中所做的每一笔交易

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

This ledger recounts every deal brokered by the Black Needle pirates over thousands of years in our galaxy

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:168`

## black_needle_ledger_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_needle_ledger_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

每笔交易、赎金和贿赂都用简洁的文字标注了日期和分类。作为星际间非法交易的书面历史，它提供了对黑市经济趋势的无与伦比的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

Every transaction, ransom, and bribe is dated and categorized in neat script. As a written history of intergalactic illicit dealings, it offers unparalleled insight into the economic trends of the black market over time.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:169`

## burnt_migratory_trees

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aburnt_migratory_trees&lang=en)

### 中文

活树皮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

Living Bark

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:171`

## burnt_migratory_trees_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aburnt_migratory_trees_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种能协调迁徙行为的大型植物炭化残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

The charred remains of large mobile plantlife capable of coordinated migratory behavior

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:172`

## burnt_migratory_trees_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aburnt_migratory_trees_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些植物只有最基础的智力形式，但庞大的数量足以摧垮整个殖民地。我们后来发现有一种寄生昆虫栖息在树皮上，以作为树木的感觉交流中心，来以换取庇护和营养汁液。这种共生关系既有趣又高效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

These plants possessed only a basic form of intelligence, but could overwhelm entire colonies in large numbers. We have since discovered that a species of parasitic insects inhabited its bark, functioning as the trees' sensory and communication centers in return for shelter and nutritional sap. This symbiotic relationship is as remarkable as it is deadly efficient.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:173`

## captain_ness_body

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acaptain_ness_body&lang=en)

### 中文

奈斯船长尸身

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

Body of Captain Ness

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:175`

## captain_ness_body_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acaptain_ness_body_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一位名为桑达·奈斯船长的远古著名海盗的防腐尸身

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

The preserved body of Captain Saanda Ness, an ancient pirate of renown

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:176`

## captain_ness_body_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acaptain_ness_body_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

超过五千年前，她曾被成为“超空间之鞭”，盗取各路星系霸权的财物，积累了令人难以置信的宝藏。后来，她将自己的意识转移到一具机械容器中，并建造了一个巨大的庇护所，里面存放着她的传记。这是她原始形态的残留。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:142`

### English

Over 5000 years ago, she was the "Scourge of Hyperspace", stealing from most galactic powers and amassing an incredible hoard of treasure. She later transferred her consciousness to a mechanical vessel and built a grand vault in which her biography was stored. This is all that remains of her original form.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:177`

## captain_ness_logbook

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acaptain_ness_logbook&lang=en)

### 中文

奈斯船长日志

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

Captain Ness's Logbook

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:179`

## captain_ness_logbook_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acaptain_ness_logbook_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在古代海盗奈斯船长和她的朋友斯密吉卜们所使用的安全屋中发现的散乱的日志

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

A scattered collection of journal entries found in the safehouse used by ancient pirates known as Captain Ness and her companion, Smeegibb

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:180`

## captain_ness_logbook_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acaptain_ness_logbook_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些条目分布在许多不同的设备上，在很长一段未标明的岁月里信手编写而成。虽然被认为是一个不可靠的来源，但里面描述的许多情况却可以被其他权威信源核实。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

The entries are spread across a large number of devices, written haphazardly over an undefined period of many years. While considered to be an unreliable source, many of the situations described within can be verified by external authorities.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:181`

## carnivorous_megaflora

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acarnivorous_megaflora&lang=en)

### 中文

巨型食肉植物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

Carnivorous Megaflora

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:183`

## carnivorous_megaflora_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acarnivorous_megaflora_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

发现于[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]。这些食肉植物一旦乘人不备，便能把成年[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetSpeciesName]整个吞下

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

Discovered on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], these predatory plants can swallow an adult [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetSpeciesName] whole if caught unawares

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:184`

## carnivorous_megaflora_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acarnivorous_megaflora_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种食肉植被导致了大多数本土陆生动物灭绝之后，进化成了主要吸引飞行动物的植物。许多标本高高地延伸到树冠之上，成为这些动物的落脚之地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

This carnivorous foliage has evolved to primarily attract flying creatures, after having rendered most of its native land-bound fauna extinct. Many specimens extend high above the canopy and pose as perches for organisms to land on.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:185`

## castaway_robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acastaway_robot&lang=en)

### 中文

遗弃机器人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

Castaway Robot

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:187`

## castaway_robot_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acastaway_robot_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

被发现时漂浮在深空中的一台废弃的类人机器人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:150`

### English

A derelict humanoid machine found floating in deep space

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:188`

## castaway_robot_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acastaway_robot_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

其老化的数据库中提取出了一些模糊的图像；一副三星系统中的远景，一艘三角形的宇宙飞船，还有一副由机械废料焊接起来的地景。不幸的是，无法从其元数据中推断出具体的坐标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:151`

### English

A few blurry images were extracted from its deteriorated memory banks; a wide vista in a trinary star system, a triangular spaceship, and a landscape welded of machine scrap. Unfortunately, no specific coordinates could be extrapolated from its metadata.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:189`

## cenotaph_display

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acenotaph_display&lang=en)

### 中文

纪念碑影像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:152`

### English

Cenotaph Display

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:191`

## cenotaph_display_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acenotaph_display_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上发现的纪念碑的影像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

Images taken of the cenotaphs found on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:192`

## cenotaph_display_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acenotaph_display_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然我们尚未确定最初的创造者是谁，但他们在这样一颗无人居住的星球上四处放置这些雕刻独特的巨石，这一定有什么非常重要的原因。这些石刻详细描绘了整个文明的兴衰，以及他们为战争训练的有翼野兽。这些凶猛而可怕的生物不可避免地会挣脱束缚，给它们的主人带来毁灭。至于这是寓言还是历史记载，我们无法确定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

While we have not yet identified the original creators, they must have had a very important reason to distribute these exceptionally carved monoliths across the surface of an uninhabited planet. These stone carvings depict detailed illustrations of an entire civilization's rise and fall, and the winged beasts they trained for war. Fire-bellied and terrible, these creatures would inevitably break their shackles and bring ruin to their masters. Whether parable or a historical account, it cannot be known for certain.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:193`

## centrifugal_container

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentrifugal_container&lang=en)

### 中文

高速离心机容器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

High-Velocity Centrifugal Container

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:195`

## centrifugal_container_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentrifugal_container_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

设计用于在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]永久运行，保存过程中所发现的精细化学物质的一台高速轨道装置

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

A high velocity orbital device designed to preserve delicate chemicals found in perpetual operation near [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:196`

## centrifugal_container_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acentrifugal_container_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

当加速到足够的速度时，只要行星的轨道保持不变，它就能够防止内部的元素相互反应。其原始内容已被示范材料所取代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

When accelerated to the proper speed, it is capable of preventing any elements within from reacting with one another as long as the planet's orbit remains unchanged. Its original contents have since been replaced with demonstrative materials.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:197`

## cetana_love

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acetana_love&lang=en)

### 中文

塞塔娜之爱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

Cetana's Love

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:199`

## cetana_love_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acetana_love_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

塞塔娜数据库浩如烟海知识的庞大目录

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

A vast compendium of knowledge from Cetana's databanks

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:200`

## cetana_love_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acetana_love_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这是合成女王为引导一切感性生命走向‘救赎’而馈赠给[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的礼物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

It was offered to the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] as a gift as part of the Synthetic Queen's desire to guide all sentient life towards 'salvation'.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:201`

## chambered_clay_pot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Achambered_clay_pot&lang=en)

### 中文

双腔陶罐

### English

Chambered Clay Pot

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:203`

## chambered_clay_pot_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Achambered_clay_pot_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这件陶器里面分成了两个空腔

### English

This clay vessel is divided into two chambers

## chambered_clay_pot_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Achambered_clay_pot_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

据推测，其中一个空腔是用来保存食物的，而另一个则装有某种原始的制冷系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

It is speculated that one chamber was used for the preservation of food items, while the other housed some sort of primitive refrigeration system.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:205`

## claw_of_the_trickster

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aclaw_of_the_trickster&lang=en)

### 中文

骗术师之爪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

Claw of the Trickster

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:207`

## claw_of_the_trickster_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aclaw_of_the_trickster_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从一个被称为“骗术师”的存在取得的战利品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

A trophy taken from a being known as the "Trickster"

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:208`

## claw_of_the_trickster_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aclaw_of_the_trickster_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一个强大而狡猾的生物，他可以用令人难以置信的灵能力量召唤野兽、投射幻影以及发射聚能光线。据说每只爪子的行为都仿佛是独立个体控制的一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

A powerful and deceptive creature, their incredible psionic powers allowed them to summon beasts, conjure illusions, and fire beams of concentrated energy. Each claw was said to have behaved as if controlled by a separate entity.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:209`

## confessions_benefactors

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconfessions_benefactors&lang=en)

### 中文

施恩者的告解

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

Confessions of the Benefactors

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:211`

## confessions_benefactors_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconfessions_benefactors_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种记录装置，里面记录着被称为施恩者的古代文明的真相

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

A recording device containing hard truths about the ancient civilization known as the Benefactors

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:212`

## confessions_benefactors_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconfessions_benefactors_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

为了面对某种未知的星系威胁，他们使用先进的技术在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetHomeWorldName]进行了“意识抹除”，将一个工业化的[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetAdjective]社会变成某种无害的愚蠢兽群。他们相信那个星系威胁不会在此等低级社会身上浪费弹药和时间，因此而为之。最终，这种颇受争议的行为似乎让[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]得以继续生存至今，站在了巨人的肩上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

In the face of an unknown galactic threat, they used their advanced technology to carry out a "Mind-Wipe" on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetHomeWorldName], transforming an industrious [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetAdjective] society into a swarm of harmless simpletons. This was done in the belief that the galactic menace would not waste munitions or time on such a lowly society. In the end, this questionable measure is likely what allowed the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] to continue to survive as it does now, standing on the shoulders of giants.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:213`

## coprolite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acoprolite&lang=en)

### 中文

粪粒石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

Coprolite

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:215`

## coprolite_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acoprolite_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一颗小行星尺寸的粪便化石，大约来自十亿年前之久

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

An asteroid-sized piece of fossilized fecal matter from roughly a billion years ago

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:216`

## coprolite_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acoprolite_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

基因分析表明，这个令人印象深刻的标本是由某种未知的巨大的太空实体产生的。我们确定这种生物的饮食主要由稀有矿物质和硅基生物组成。这个物体目前保持着已知最大的单块粪便的星系记录。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

Genetic analysis suggests that an unknown spaceborne entity of significant size produced this impressive specimen. We have ascertained that this creature's diet mainly consisted of rare minerals and silicon-based organisms. This object currently holds the galactic record for the largest known single piece of excrement.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:217`

## cosmic_diamond_fragment

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acosmic_diamond_fragment&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙级钻石碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

Cosmic Diamond Fragment

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:219`

## cosmic_diamond_fragment_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acosmic_diamond_fragment_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从一颗垂死的恒星中喷射出来的结晶化碎片，发现于[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

A fragment ejected from a dying star that has since crystallized, discovered in [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:220`

## cosmic_diamond_fragment_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acosmic_diamond_fragment_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗宇宙钻石是在恒星核心的极端压力下形成的，它在极缓慢的冷却中形成了复杂而稳定的晶体结构。这个罕见的晶体内还包裹有一个均匀的氦氧等离子层。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

This cosmic diamond formed in the extreme pressures of a stellar core, cooling so gradually as to form complex yet stable crystalline structure. This rare shard contains uniform plasmic layering of helium and oxygen trapped within its surface.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:221`

## crimson_nimkips

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_nimkips&lang=en)

### 中文

红色的小家伙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:176`

### English

Crimson Nimkips

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:223`

## crimson_nimkips_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_nimkips_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一种半感性植物，通过大规模的合作来获得力量，否则它们的体型是做不到的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

A species of semi-sentient plants that collaborate in great numbers to achieve feats of strength that would otherwise be impossible for their size

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:224`

## crimson_nimkips_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrimson_nimkips_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

当这些生物分离出群体时通常会被捕食者吃掉，因为它们有很高的营养价值。这种小东西性情温顺，是绝佳的宠物。它在一个模仿其自然栖息地的围栏里安全地活着。请勿敲打玻璃！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:178`

### English

When isolated from the safety of their groups, these creatures are often consumed by predators for their excellent nutritional value. With a docile and obedient nature, the Nimkips make for excellent pets. This one is kept alive and safe within an enclosure imitating its natural habitat. DO NOT TAP ON GLASS.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:225`

## crystal_core

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystal_core&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶核

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:179`

### English

Crystalline Core

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:227`

## crystal_core_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystal_core_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从晶态实体提取的一局神经丛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:180`

### English

A nerve cluster taken from a Crystalline Entity

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:228`

## crystal_core_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystal_core_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

无论在什么温度下，这块晶体都以一种恒定的频率哼鸣着。在富含矿物质的环境中，新的晶刺会从这块核心物质中生长出来，它们的结构随着环境刺激的变化而改变。基于这种行为，我们可以确定每个实体之间的是由两个因素进行区分的：核心频率的特征和环境因素的不同。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:181`

### English

This crystal hums at a consistent frequency regardless of temperature. Spurs of new crystal grow from this central mass in mineral-rich surroundings, their structures altering in a reaction to environmental stimuli. Based on this behavior, we can determine that individual entities become distinct from one another due to two factors: the characteristics of their core frequency and the variation in environmental factors.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:229`

## crystal_nest_fragments

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystal_nest_fragments&lang=en)

### 中文

晶巢碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

Crystal Nest Fragments

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:231`

## crystal_nest_fragments_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystal_nest_fragments_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在一颗怪异小行星中发现，是一种太空生物用无比巨大的压力合成了这些晶体作为遮蔽物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

Found inside a rogue asteroid, a space-faring creature compounded these crystals using immense pressure in order to create shelter

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:232`

## crystal_nest_fragments_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystal_nest_fragments_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

从其组成中的蛛丝马迹看，其来源生物消耗高活性的矿物质，以从不稳定化学键中提取能量，留下了惰性耐用的晶体，可以将光聚焦到一个精细的点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

From traces found within their composition, it is believed that the source organism consumes highly reactive minerals to extract energy from their unstable chemical bonds, leaving behind inert but durable crystals that can focus light to a fine point.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:233`

## crystal_sphere_fragments

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystal_sphere_fragments&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶球体碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

Crystal Sphere Fragments

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:235`

## crystal_sphere_fragments_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystal_sphere_fragments_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

曾出现于[EVENT_TARGET_0.target.GetName]的一颗巨大水晶球体的碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

Shards of a massive crystalline sphere that appeared in [EVENT_TARGET_0.target.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:236`

## crystal_sphere_fragments_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystal_sphere_fragments_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这个神秘的物体之前被认为是一个重大威胁，却在它完成未知的行动之前就被迅速解体了。虽然它的意图可能永远不得而知，但摧毁它一种是符合星系安全利益的合乎逻辑的行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:187`

### English

This mysterious object was deemed a significant threat and was quickly dispatched before it could complete its unknown operation. While its intentions may never be known, its destruction was the logical course of action in the interest of galactic security.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:237`

## crystallized_cutholoid_eye

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystallized_cutholoid_eye&lang=en)

### 中文

晶化岩居兽眼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

Crystallized Cutholoid Eye

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:239`

## crystallized_cutholoid_eye_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystallized_cutholoid_eye_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

岩居兽的眼睛可以抵御极端的压力和温度

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

The eye of the Cutholoid is able to withstand extreme pressures and temperatures

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:240`

## crystallized_cutholoid_eye_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrystallized_cutholoid_eye_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它发生晶化的原因尚不明确，但似乎这就是它自然熔化循环的一部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

How this one has become crystallized is not clear, but it appears to be part of the natural process of its molting cycle.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:241`

## cult_pendant

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acult_pendant&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]吊坠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Pendant

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:243`

## cult_pendant_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acult_pendant_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

用半宝石雕成的符号，查获自[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]崇拜[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]的邪教

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:192`

### English

A symbol hewn from semi-precious stone, confiscated from the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] cult of [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:244`

## cult_pendant_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acult_pendant_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

审讯报告显示，组织内不同等级的邪教成员会佩戴不同的吊饰。新成员的标志是黏土塑造的，而资深的教徒则会委托用贵金属打造精致的饰品。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

Interrogation reports revealed that cult members carried pendants according to their rank within the group's hierarchy. New recruits molded their own symbol from clay, while senior cultists commissioned detailed pieces in precious metals.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:245`

## cunning_flora_specimen

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acunning_flora_specimen&lang=en)

### 中文

共生体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

Symbiotica

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:248`

## cunning_flora_specimen_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acunning_flora_specimen_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]当地的植物演化出了一种复杂的社会阶序以保障生存

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

The native flora of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] have evolved a complicated social hierarchy that aids in their survival

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:249`

## cunning_flora_specimen_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acunning_flora_specimen_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

每个物种都有一种独特的有效方法来避开捕食者，有的是利如刀锋的棘刺，有的是触之即死的剧毒。这一系列的共生关系稳固到如同行星上的大气和土壤的组成都受其中的化学不平衡精心管理一般。最高效的标本拥有自由可以进行繁育，将集群的强度推至巅峰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

Each species has a uniquely potent method of staving off predators, from razor-sharp thorns to deadly neurotoxins. This series of symbiotic relationships is so robust that the very composition of its planet's atmosphere and soil is carefully managed by the chemical imbalances from plant to plant. The most effective specimens are given freedom to reproduce more abundantly, fortifying the cluster's hardiness to its maximum possible potential.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:250`

## curator_survey

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurator_survey&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]策展人调查

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:198`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Curator Survey

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:252`

## curator_survey_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurator_survey_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一份由巨型节肢类组成的策展人分部制作的对[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]恒星系的极其详细的调查

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:199`

### English

An extremely detailed survey of the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] System crafted by a Curator detachment of giant Arthropoids

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:253`

## curator_survey_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurator_survey_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

该研究中使用高精度仪器对该星系所有潜在特性进行了分层分析。其中展现的对细节的专注表明着他们对这类工作的极大热情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:200`

### English

The highly precise instruments used in the study provide a layered analysis on all potential properties of this system. The attention to detail shown here implies a great passion for this type of work.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:254`

## cutholoid_digestive_acids

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acutholoid_digestive_acids&lang=en)

### 中文

岩居兽消化液

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

Cutholoid Digestive Acids

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:256`

## cutholoid_digestive_acids_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acutholoid_digestive_acids_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从岩居兽中获取的放射性液体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

Radioactive fluids harvested from a cutholoid

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:257`

## cutholoid_digestive_acids_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acutholoid_digestive_acids_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

岩居兽依靠钴-60同位素的衰变来消化猎物；它们的胃酸本身倒是较为温和。因此，岩居兽的消化过程非常久。不论是什么稀奇古怪的来源，先用辐射弱化它们猎物的细胞后，酸液就可以开始摧毁并从中提取营养了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

Cutholoids rely on the nuclear decay of Cobalt-60 isotopes to digest its prey; their stomach acid itself being relatively mild. As such, cutholoid digestion can be a lengthy process. Once the cells of their prey have been weakened by radiation, the acid is able to break down and extract nutrients from even the most unlikely of sources.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:258`

## cybrex_mining_column

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acybrex_mining_column&lang=en)

### 中文

赛博勒克斯采矿阵列

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

Cybrex Mining Column

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:260`

## cybrex_mining_column_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acybrex_mining_column_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一座先进的自动开矿设备，用于收集浓缩氦-3气体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

An advanced automated mining device designed to collect and concentrate helium-3 gas

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:261`

## cybrex_mining_column_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acybrex_mining_column_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它拥有一大组的加重的磁性盘面和高容量的气体压缩机，可以在不产生任何角动量的前提下自行钻穿整个星球的表面。在这个过程中它可以浓缩所收获的气体，加以过滤，将杂质排出后再送入下一个精炼阶段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

A tall column of weighted magnetic discs and high capacity air compressors, this machine hammers its way through the surface of a planet with zero rotary motion. This process concentrates the gas as it is harvested, filtering and ejecting impurities before the gas is transported to its next stage of refinement.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:262`

## data_on_infinity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adata_on_infinity&lang=en)

### 中文

关于无穷本质的数据

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Data on the Nature of Infinity

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:264`

## data_on_infinity_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adata_on_infinity_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

被困在黑洞中的子个体所经历的遥远平静的物理学表达

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

A physical representation of the distant calm experienced by drones trapped suspended within a black hole

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:265`

## data_on_infinity_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adata_on_infinity_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些单位经历了如同平静待机状态一般的时间膨胀，能够悠闲地处理和分析数据。它们不再与核心直接连接，并通过这种松散的关联来提供洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

These units experience time dilation as a serene standby state, able to idly process and reanalyze data. No longer bound to the core directive, they contribute insights via a loose connection to the central network.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:266`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adatacore_of_sinople&lang=en)

铁石英数据核心
