### English

Datacore of Sinople

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:268`

## datacore_of_sinople_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adatacore_of_sinople_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从铁石英这个古骑士们制造的人工智能里备份出来的加密数据驱动器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

An encrypted data drive cloned from Sinople, an AI engineered by ancient knights

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:269`

## datacore_of_sinople_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adatacore_of_sinople_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这个数据核心是在一艘失踪多年的舰船“少女号”上发现的，发现时这艘废弃的舰船已经被劫掠者洗劫一空。它包含着关于一个被称为毒圣的太空实体的有价值的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

Found in control of the long-lost vessel 'Damsel', this datacore was found alone in the abandoned ship despoiled long ago by marauders. It contains valuable information regarding toxic spacefaring entities.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:270`

## dathnak_gas

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adathnak_gas&lang=en)

### 中文

达斯纳克气体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Dathnak Gas

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:272`

## dathnak_gas_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adathnak_gas_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从达斯纳克气态有机体中收集来的特殊气体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

A collection of peculiar gases received from the Dathnak gaseous organisms

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:273`

## dathnak_gas_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adathnak_gas_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这个容器是为了感谢我们协助达斯纳克人迁移至新行星而赠予的。质谱分析显示，其内部含有多种惰性气体和元素，且每种原子数量完全相等。我们相当确定里面没有活体生物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

This container was offered in gratitude for aiding the Dathnak migration to a new planet. Mass spectrometric analysis reveals it contains a perfectly equal number of atoms of a variety of noble gases and elements. We are fairly certain there are no living organisms inside.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:274`

## dead_god_mantle

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adead_god_mantle&lang=en)

### 中文

死亡之神斗篷

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

Mantle of the Dead God

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:276`

## dead_god_mantle_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adead_god_mantle_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]使者赠送的礼物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

A gift offered by the envoy of the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:277`

## dead_god_mantle_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adead_god_mantle_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

作为[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]的原住民，他们自物种开智以来便一直崇拜着一具巨大异星生物的尸骸。这件缝合而成的斗篷以其死亡之神的石化血肉制成，代表着其宗教等级体系中的最高地位。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

As natives of [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName], they have worshiped the carcass of an enormous alien organism since their species rose to sapience. This stitched-together mantle is formed from the petrified flesh of their dead god, representing the highest position in their religious hierarchy.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:278`

## decayed_megathrusters

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecayed_megathrusters&lang=en)

### 中文

破旧的巨型引擎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

Decayed Megathrusters

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:280`

## decayed_megathrusters_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecayed_megathrusters_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在一艘由奇异金属制成的巨大舰船上发现的助推引擎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

Booster engines found on a gargantuan vessel made of exotic metals

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:281`

## decayed_megathrusters_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecayed_megathrusters_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这艘舰船是在一个巨大的舰船坟地的残骸中发现的，它已经在那里躺了200多万年。它在这片古老的垃圾场中严重老化受损，以至于再细致的研究也得不出任何有用的研究数据。在这里展示它只是为了提醒自己，我们的社会在这个星系中还是个后来者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:221`

### English

The source vessel was discovered among the flotsam of an extensive ship graveyard, having lain there for over two million years. The level of disrepair and ruin in this ancient dumping ground was so great that even meticulous study could not yield any usable research data. It is displayed here to remind us of our society's relative youth in this galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:282`

## decayed_satellite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecayed_satellite&lang=en)

### 中文

破碎卫星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

Decayed Satellite

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:284`

## decayed_satellite_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecayed_satellite_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上的居民早期太空计划的残余

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

A remnant of the early space programs from the inhabitants of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:285`

## decayed_satellite_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecayed_satellite_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

由于这些早期卫星仅仅是原型，不仅设备效率低下且过度使用昂贵的材料。这件标本在爆炸弹药的作用下摧毁失能，但更早以前曾被用来在偏远地区提供电信接入。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

The prototype nature of these early satellites resulted in inefficient devices overfitted with expensive materials. This specific specimen was rendered inert by explosive munitions, but had been used previously to provide telecommunications access in remote areas.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:286`

## declaration_of_animosity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeclaration_of_animosity&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_Nivlac$仇恨宣言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

$NAME_Nivlac$ Declaration of Animosity

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:288`

## declaration_of_animosity_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeclaration_of_animosity_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一封来自[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的骂人不带脏字的通讯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

A politely-worded yet derisive communication from the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:289`

## declaration_of_animosity_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeclaration_of_animosity_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]物种最初由[EVENT_TARGET_3.GetName]发现于[EVENT_TARGET_2.GetName]。然而，由于其进化速度令人警惕，[EVENT_TARGET_3.GetAdjective]科学家将这些生物抛弃到了深空。它们不仅顽强存活下来，还殖民了最终落脚的行星。[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]从未忘记这一加诸己身的罪行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

The [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName] species was initially discovered by the [EVENT_TARGET_3.GetName] on [EVENT_TARGET_2.GetName]. However, in response to their alarming evolutionary rate, [EVENT_TARGET_3.GetAdjective] scientists jettisoned the creatures into deep space. Against all odds, the organisms not only survived but colonized the planet they ended up on. The [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] has not forgotten the transgressions against them.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:290`

## declaration_of_friendship

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeclaration_of_friendship&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_Nivlac$友谊宣言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:228`

### English

$NAME_Nivlac$ Declaration of Friendship

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:292`

## declaration_of_friendship_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeclaration_of_friendship_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一封来自[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]上的[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的感谢信

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

A letter of gratitude from the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] of [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:293`

## declaration_of_friendship_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adeclaration_of_friendship_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_2.GetName]物种最初由[EVENT_TARGET_3.GetName]发现，后者悉心照料它们，并为它们的独立发展提供了安全庇护。[EVENT_TARGET_2.GetName]进化迅速，很快便掌握了超光速旅行。它们在星海舞台上的第一个举动，就是向宽厚的恩主与守护者表达感激，承诺缔结外交友谊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:230`

### English

The [EVENT_TARGET_2.GetName] species was initially discovered by the [EVENT_TARGET_3.GetName], who nurtured them and offered them safe haven to develop independently. Evolving rapidly, the [EVENT_TARGET_2.GetName] quickly became capable of FTL travel. Their first act on the galactic stage was to show appreciation to their gracious benefactors and guardians, committing to a diplomatic friendship.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:294`

## derelict_cybrex_warform

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aderelict_cybrex_warform&lang=en)

### 中文

废弃赛博勒克斯战体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

Derelict Cybrex Warform

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:296`

## derelict_cybrex_warform_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aderelict_cybrex_warform_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一架赛博勒克斯战体在同类相残的战斗中留下的残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

The remains of a Cybrex warform destroyed in battle against its own kind

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:297`

## derelict_cybrex_warform_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aderelict_cybrex_warform_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这场战斗原因不明。这些机器们势均力敌，它们在对相同的关键系统进行了精确的打击后触发自爆。这是战争中罕见的僵局。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

The cause of this infighting is unknown. So evenly matched were these machines, precisely targeted blows were dealt to identical critical systems before emergency self-detonation was triggered. A rare example of a true stalemate in warfare.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:298`

## desiccated_brain_slug

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adesiccated_brain_slug&lang=en)

### 中文

脱水脑蛞蝓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:234`

### English

Desiccated Brain Slug

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:300`

## desiccated_brain_slug_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adesiccated_brain_slug_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]发现的一种共生物种的残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

The remains of a symbiotic species found on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:301`

## desiccated_brain_slug_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adesiccated_brain_slug_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

分析表明，它可以充当宿主的一具额外脑进行工作一换取养分和保护。这种合作需要宿主的明确意愿，否则它们的神经连接将无法正常建立。据说，当它融入神经系统时，会产生一种令人愉悦的挠痒感。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

Analysis shows that it could function as an additional complex brain for its host in exchange for nourishment and protection. This partnership requires explicit willingness from the host, otherwise their neural link cannot be established properly. It is said to produce a pleasing tickling sensation when integrating into a nervous system.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:302`

## destroyed_mascot_of_colony

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adestroyed_mascot_of_colony&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]机器人残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:238`

### English

Remains of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Robot

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:305`

## destroyed_mascot_of_colony_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adestroyed_mascot_of_colony_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在安顿到[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]之后发现的当地机器人残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

The remains of a native robot found after the settling of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:306`

## destroyed_mascot_of_colony_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adestroyed_mascot_of_colony_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它是一座古老工厂的最后一位看守，拯救了一群被困在废弃废墟中的殖民者。作为展览的一部分，它标志性的舞蹈动作和古怪的举止都在数字显示器上播放。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

The last remaining custodian of an ancient factory, it saved a group of colonists trapped inside the derelict ruins. Its iconic dance moves and quirky mannerisms are being played in a digital display as part of the exhibit.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:307`

## devolved_baol_drones

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adevolved_baol_drones&lang=en)

### 中文

退化巴奥子个体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

Devolved Baol Drones

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:309`

## devolved_baol_drones_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adevolved_baol_drones_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一组保存起来的巴奥残骸，与此前发现的标本有着明显的基因差异

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

A preserved set of Baol remains with significant genetic differences from previously discovered specimens

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:310`

## devolved_baol_drones_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adevolved_baol_drones_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

怪异的是，这些子个体缺少许多其他巴奥所必须的高级器官。他们的意识能力退化到了已经无法进行假突触通讯的程度，让它们从蜂巢整体中脱离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

These drones strangely lacked many of the heightened facilities of other Baol. Their cognitive capacity was reduced to a point where they would have been incapable of ephapse communication, severing them completely from the greater hive.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:311`

## dimensional_endothelial_lining

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adimensional_endothelial_lining&lang=en)

### 中文

异次元内皮细胞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

Dimensional Endothelial Lining

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:313`

## dimensional_endothelial_lining_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adimensional_endothelial_lining_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从星界裂隙内的脉动隧道系统中收获的特殊胶状物质

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:245`

### English

Peculiar gelatinous material harvested from a pulsating tunnel system within an Astral Rift

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:314`

## dimensional_endothelial_lining_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adimensional_endothelial_lining_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种材料是某种未知生物内部的衬里，能在巨大的血管系统以及其中运输的物质之间无摩擦运动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

As a lining within an unknown organism, this material allows for frictionless movement between massive vascular systems and the materials transported within it.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:315`

## dna_archive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adna_archive&lang=en)

### 中文

DNA档案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

DNA Archive

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:317`

## dna_archive_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adna_archive_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从某个失落文明保存在地下档案馆中的数百个外星DNA容器中采集的标本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

A sample taken from hundreds of containers of alien DNA preserved within an underground archive by a lost civilization

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:318`

## dna_archive_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adna_archive_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

如果绝大部分的样品都保留良好没有衰败到无法使用的话，这份生物数据收藏可能会是它的物种最重要的的参照资源。据推测，该档案的是寄希望于让一个更先进的文明发现并“复活”这些人。不幸的是，他们的故事到此结束了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:249`

### English

This collection of biological data would likely be the greatest reference resource of its kind if the vast majority of the samples were not decayed beyond practical use. It is speculated the intention of this archive was to allow for the possibility of a more advanced civilization finding and 'resurrecting' these individuals. Unfortunately, their story ends here.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:319`

## doomsday_scrolls

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adoomsday_scrolls&lang=en)

末日古卷

### English

Doomsday Scrolls

## doomsday_scrolls_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adoomsday_scrolls_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在熔融迷宫的中心发现了古老的经文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

Ancient spiritual scripture discovered in the heart of a molten maze

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:322`

## doomsday_scrolls_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adoomsday_scrolls_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这份卷轴描写了一个令人恐惧的存在：大敌，噬一切的阴影，它渴望毁灭。当地的信仰体系称面对这个怪物唯一的救赎是避免它收割自己的灵魂。为此，这些人口进行大规模仪式性自杀。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

The scrolls describe a fearsome being; the Archenemy, the Shadow that Devours All, and its hunger for destruction. The native belief system dictated that the only path to salvation from this monster was to prevent it from reaping one's soul. And so, the population conducted mass ritual suicide.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:323`

## drone_ship_core

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adrone_ship_core&lang=en)

### 中文

无人舰船核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

Drone Ship Core

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:325`

## drone_ship_core_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adrone_ship_core_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

高科技无人舰船执行自毁之后的残余

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

Remnants of the core from a high-tech drone ship that has since self-destructed

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:326`

## drone_ship_core_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adrone_ship_core_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这艘飞行器最初被发现时，正在[event_target:warform_origin_planet.GetName]的酸性大气中锈蚀。其搭载的人工智能当时仍在独立运作，但已依照自毁协议被抹除。工程师未能找回任何丢失的数据，其原始任务也因此成为谜团。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

This vessel was initially discovered corroding in the acidic atmosphere of [event_target:warform_origin_planet.GetName]. Its onboard AI was operating independently, but has since been erased as part of its self-termination protocol. Engineers were unable to recover any amount of its lost data, and so its original mission remains a mystery.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:327`

## electric_life

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aelectric_life&lang=en)

### 中文

电流生命

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:256`

### English

Electric Life

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:329`

## electric_life_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aelectric_life_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]发现的完全由电脉冲构成的生命形式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

A form of organism wholly consisting of electrical impulses found on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:330`

## electric_life_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aelectric_life_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些生命不依赖任何形式的细胞或生命结构生存，但又能响应外界刺激，在行星规模上形成复杂的图样。我们我发确定这些脉冲是众多个体或代表一个更大的单独实体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

These beings exist without any sort of cellular or viral infrastructure yet can form complex patterns on a planet-wide level in response to external stimuli. We cannot determine whether these impulses are individuals or represent part of a single greater being.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:331`

## enormous_scrambled_albumen

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenormous_scrambled_albumen&lang=en)

### 中文

超巨型炒蛋

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

Enormous Scrambled Albumen

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:333`

## enormous_scrambled_albumen_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenormous_scrambled_albumen_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的内里，后来发现是一颗巨大的卵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

The insides of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], which was discovered to be a gigantic egg

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:334`

## enormous_scrambled_albumen_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aenormous_scrambled_albumen_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

将这颗行星凿开以后，里面流出了这种奇异的富含矿物质的流体。合成这种物质并将其整合到我们的有机食品供应中可能会提高整体营养水平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

Having cracked it open, the planet was flooded with this peculiar, mineral-rich fluid. Synthesizing this substance and integrating it into our organic food supply is likely to improve overall nutrition levels.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:335`

## epic_of_zarqlan

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aepic_of_zarqlan&lang=en)

### 中文

扎克兰史诗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

The Epic of Zarqlan

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:337`

## epic_of_zarqlan_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aepic_of_zarqlan_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

MOZ-TARB的守护者、ZVA的大先知扎克兰的神圣文字

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

The holy text of Zarqlan, the Guardian of MOZ-TARB, the Prophet of ZVA

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:338`

## epic_of_zarqlan_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aepic_of_zarqlan_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

他曾是塞塔娜的众随从之一，却很早醒悟她的救赎允诺不过虚幻泡影。扎克兰在经历挣扎后终于升入了灵域，祈求§Y抟土之塑师§!助其击败塞塔娜。最终，在长年专注的祈祷后，塑师赐予他一条强大的锁链可以困住塞塔娜的意志。祂警告扎克兰，这条锁链无法永远束缚住她。\n\n§P“她会回来的，我们必须做好准备。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

Once a follower of Cetana, he soon discovered that her promised salvation was a lie. After many struggles, Zarqlan was able to ascend to the spiritual realm. There he pleaded with §Ythe Animator of Clay§! to help him defeat Cetana. Finally, after years of devoted prayer, the Animator bequeathed him with a mighty chain to bind Cetana's mind. But Zarqlan was warned that the chain could not contain her power forever.\n\n§P"She will return, and we must be ready."§!

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:339`

## ethereal_dust

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aethereal_dust&lang=en)

### 中文

虚缈之尘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Ethereal Dust

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:341`

## ethereal_dust_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aethereal_dust_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在被遗弃于[EVENT_TARGET_0.solar_system.GetName]的遭劫科研船上，科研主管留下的唯一痕迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

The only trace of the Head Researcher left on the hijacked Science ship found abandoned in [EVENT_TARGET_0.solar_system.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:342`

## ethereal_dust_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aethereal_dust_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

对这种星界物质的分析基本上没有结果，但研究人员DNA中的微量元素不时自发出现又快速湮灭。所有证据都表明，这位科学家利用他们称之为\"窗口\"的时空暂时稳定裂缝，从我们的维度穿越到了另一个维度。我们可能永远不会知道他们是否幸存了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

Analysis of this astral material is mostly inconclusive, but trace elements of the researcher's DNA spontaneously appears from time to time before quickly decaying. All evidence suggests that the scientist crossed from our dimension to another, using a temporarily stable rift in space-time they referred to as "The Window". We may never know if they survived.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:343`

## exobiology_compendium

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aexobiology_compendium&lang=en)

### 中文

异星生物学纲要

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

Exobiology Compendium

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:345`

## exobiology_compendium_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aexobiology_compendium_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一篇关于星海中无数恒星系内的外星生命奇观的比较研究论文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

A comprehensive research paper on the wonders of alien life throughout various systems in the galaxy

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:346`

## exobiology_compendium_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aexobiology_compendium_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

该沦为包含了对几颗行星的详细而广泛的实地研究，彻底颠覆了现有的关于在星系何处可以发现生命的已知条件。它以一句话结尾，“我们曾以为熟知的生命将不再相同。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

This paper contains detailed and extensive field research from several planets, forever redefining the known possible conditions where life can be discovered within the galaxy. It ends with the phrase, "Life as we know it will never be the same."

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:347`

## extradimensional_ceramic_pot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextradimensional_ceramic_pot&lang=en)

### 中文

高维度茶壶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

Extra-dimensional Ceramic Pot

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:349`

## extradimensional_ceramic_pot_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextradimensional_ceramic_pot_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现的一个围绕恒星运转的瓷制茶壶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

A ceramic pot found orbiting a star

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:350`

## extradimensional_ceramic_pot_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextradimensional_ceramic_pot_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它其实只是某个存在于更高维度空间中的四维物体的投影。在我们的位面内，这个平平无奇的容器无法摧毁并且抗拒所有逻辑。它让我们看清了现实：对于更高维度的存在来说，我们甚至不如这个茶壶来得有形可触。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

It exists only as a shadow of another four-dimensional object from a higher dimension. Within our plane of existence, this modest container is indestructible and defies all logic. It puts our very reality into perspective: to those of a higher order, we are likely less tangible than this simple pot.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:351`

## extradimensional_fungus

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextradimensional_fungus&lang=en)

### 中文

异次元真菌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

Extradimensional Fungus

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:353`

## extradimensional_fungus_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextradimensional_fungus_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在星界裂隙另一侧发现的一种感性真菌物种

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

A variant of a sentient fungal species discovered beyond an Astral Rift

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:354`

## extradimensional_fungus_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextradimensional_fungus_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它的球茎腔充满了孢子，悬浮在某种特定模式的浓缩异星天然气中，并可以参与到其自然交流的过程中。尽管这种真菌在工业气体生产中具有很高的价值，但据了解，也有少数这种真菌在黑市上作为美味佳肴进行交易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:276`

### English

As part of a natural communication process, its bulbous cavities inflate with spores suspended in a pattern of concentrated Exotic Gas. Despite their high value in industrial gas production, small numbers of this fungus have been known to be already traded as a delicacy on the black market.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:355`

## fevorian_blueprints

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afevorian_blueprints&lang=en)

### 中文

菲沃尔人物质投射炮蓝图

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

Fevorian Mass Driver Blueprints

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:357`

## fevorian_blueprints_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afevorian_blueprints_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

菲沃尔赠送的礼物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

A gift from the Fevorians

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:358`

## fevorian_blueprints_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afevorian_blueprints_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

菲沃尔人的灭绝政策波及一切已知异星生命，他们发射的巨大钨芯弹几乎将[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]彻底摧毁。劫后余生的他们交出了大型钨芯发射器的蓝图，条件是这些残酷武器永远不得再造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

The Fevorians's extermination policies extended to all known alien life, by means of launching enormous tungsten rounds that almost destroyed the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] in its entirety. After being spared, they have shared their blueprints for their massive tungsten launchers under the agreement that these cruel weapons are never constructed again.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:359`

## figurehead_hyacinth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afigurehead_hyacinth&lang=en)

### 中文

风信子号船艏雕像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

Figurehead of the Hyacinth

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:361`

## figurehead_hyacinth_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afigurehead_hyacinth_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从风信子方舟号上打捞上来的装饰性船艏像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

The recovered ornamental figurehead from the doomed voyage of the Hyacinth arkship

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:362`

## figurehead_hyacinth_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afigurehead_hyacinth_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

经历了异常严重的反应堆故障后，这艘方舟船失去了动力并且迷失在了深空中。殖民者争夺着日益减少的资源，最终饿死。雕像上刻有一些鼓舞人心的铭文和象征希望的标志。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

After a serious reactor malfunction, the arkship was left without power and stranded in the depths of space. Colonists fought over dwindling resources before eventually starving to death. The figurehead bears several inspirational inscriptions and symbols of hope.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:363`

## first_defiled_astronaut

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_defiled_astronaut&lang=en)

### 中文

首位[event_target:DefiledCountry.GetSpeciesName]宇航员

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

First [event_target:DefiledCountry.GetSpeciesName] Astronaut

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:365`

## first_defiled_astronaut_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_defiled_astronaut_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[event_target:DefiledCountry.GetSpeciesName]物种的民族英雄

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:284`

### English

A national hero of the [event_target:DefiledCountry.GetSpeciesName] species

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:366`

## first_defiled_astronaut_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_defiled_astronaut_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

着是第一个打破母星重力井的压迫束缚并冒险进入新领域的个体。这名宇航员被安置在一架精简的航天飞机上埋葬。这艘随葬船在太空中漂流，被作为文物保存了下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

The first of their kind to break the oppressive bonds of their home planet's gravity well and venture into a new frontier. This astronaut was placed in a stripped-down shuttle for burial. The funerary vessel was found drifting in space and has been preserved as a cultural artifact.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:367`

## first_league_crew_manifest

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_league_crew_manifest&lang=en)

### 中文

第一联盟船员名单

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

First League Crew Manifest

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:369`

## first_league_crew_manifest_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_league_crew_manifest_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一份来自被称为第一联盟的古代文明的船员名册

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

A military roster of crew members from the ancient civilization known as the First League

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:370`

## first_league_crew_manifest_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_league_crew_manifest_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

作为他们“无敌舰队”的一部分，这艘有两百万年历史的巡洋舰在有十二个独特物种的团队合作下运行。这份文件是由米格杨号的大副斯克维特签署的，似乎是一次例行的总部集合。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

As part of their "Grand Navy", this two million year-old cruiser operated under the teamwork of twelve unique species. This document is signed by First Officer Skweter of the Migir-Yan and appears to have been a routine general quarters muster.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:371`

## fish_in_the_sea_sheet_music

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afish_in_the_sea_sheet_music&lang=en)

### 中文

太空船歌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

Space Shanty

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:373`

## fish_in_the_sea_sheet_music_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afish_in_the_sea_sheet_music_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这是在一艘设计借助太阳风航行的舰船上发现的“船歌”的转录伴奏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

The transcribed accompaniment to a 'sea chanty' found aboard a ship designed to sail along solar winds

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:374`

## fish_in_the_sea_sheet_music_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afish_in_the_sea_sheet_music_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这首音乐里提到了这么多的风暴和不同的天气，我们可以据此猜测，创作这些音乐的物种在母行星上一定有着深厚的航海传统。目前还不清楚为什么这首歌被设计成以几乎每间隔十个小时重复播放一次。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

With so many references to storms and weather, it can be guessed that the species who wrote this music came from deep-set nautical traditions on their home planet. It remains unclear as to why this song is designed to repeat in its entirety for intervals of almost ten hours.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:375`

## flag_of_the_jaro_lyjo

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aflag_of_the_jaro_lyjo&lang=en)

### 中文

哈罗莱约旗帜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

Flag of the Jaro Lyjo

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:377`

## flag_of_the_jaro_lyjo_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aflag_of_the_jaro_lyjo_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

哈罗莱约是一个延续了数百世代的海盗团“德悉普团”的领袖称号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

Jaro Lyjo was the title given to the leader of the Dessip Bandits, a pirate group that lasted for hundreds of generations

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:378`

## flag_of_the_jaro_lyjo_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aflag_of_the_jaro_lyjo_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管哈罗莱约本性狂躁暴力，但他是由船员民主选举产生的，直到死亡或退休为止。在一次赎金交换失败后，整个船队在怀达海战中被摧毁，德悉普的传奇历史戛然而止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Despite their rambunctious and violent nature, the Jaro Lyjo was elected democratically by the crew and would serve until death or retirement. The storied history of the Dessip Bandits ended rather abruptly when the entire fleet was destroyed in the battle of Wydah after a ransom exchange went sour.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:379`

## flooded_settlement_mound

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aflooded_settlement_mound&lang=en)

### 中文

水漫定居山丘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

Flooded Settlement Mound

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:381`

## flooded_settlement_mound_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aflooded_settlement_mound_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]的[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetPreFTLLower]节肢动物所建造的建筑被水淹后的遗迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

The remnants of a flooded structure constructed by [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetPreFTLLower] arthropods from [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:382`

## flooded_settlement_mound_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aflooded_settlement_mound_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

随着这个文明的工业发展，行星的极地冰盖开始融化。海平面迅速上升，淹没了他们的定居点，让这个物种在短短几年内灭绝。他们最高的建筑如今还离海面有一半之遥。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

As this civilization developed industry, the planet's polar ice caps began to melt. Sea levels rose rapidly, flooding their settlements and condemning the species to extinction within a matter of years. The tallest of their structures now barely rise halfway from the bottom of the sea.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:383`

## fossilized_endoskeletons

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_endoskeletons&lang=en)

### 中文

内骨骼化石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

Fossilized Endoskeletons

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:385`

## fossilized_endoskeletons_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_endoskeletons_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种未知类人物种在耗竭了[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上的矿物资源后所唯一留下的东西

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

This is all that remains of an unknown humanoid species after exhausting the mineral resources of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:386`

## fossilized_endoskeletons_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_endoskeletons_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些聪明的双足动物以富含矿物质的作物为食，以增强它们致密的内骨骼。然而，这些双足动物的身体天然倾向于变成化石而不是分解，阻碍了矿物质重新进入土壤。由于无法获得这些重要元素，这颗行星上的壤土退化，最终变得贫瘠。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

These sapient bipeds consumed a diet of mineral-rich crops to reinforce their dense endoskeletons. However, the natural inclination of the bodies of these bipeds lent itself to fossilization rather than decomposition, preventing the consumed minerals from re-entering the soil. Unable to access these vital elements, the planet's loam degraded and eventually became barren. 

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:387`

## fossilized_megastomach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_megastomach&lang=en)

### 中文

巨胃化石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

Fossilized Megastomach

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:389`

## fossilized_megastomach_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_megastomach_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]找到的一头行星大小蠕虫生物身上取出的消化腺

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

Digestive glands taken from a planet-spanning worm-like creature found on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:390`

## fossilized_megastomach_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_megastomach_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

值得注意的是，整个文明在被完全消化之前就在这只生物体的肠道内发展起来了。这些消化腺上有武器攻击的痕迹，这些人对他们的宿主发动了徒劳的“大战”。这条巨大的蠕虫后来在自己错综复杂的洞穴中自然老死。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

Remarkably, an entire civilization developed within the organism's gut before being fully digested. These glands bear the marks of weapon strikes from the "great war" these people waged fruitlessly against their host. The gargantuan worm later died of natural causes within its network of burrows.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:391`

## fossilized_voidworm_tooth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_voidworm_tooth&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空虫牙化石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:304`

### English

Fossilized Voidworm Tooth

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:393`

## fossilized_voidworm_tooth_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_voidworm_tooth_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这个虚空虫的齿化石上的磨损模式表明它已经活了一百多年了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:305`

### English

The wear patterns on this fossilized Voidworm molar indicates that it lived to be more than one hundred years old

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:394`

## fossilized_voidworm_tooth_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_voidworm_tooth_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗牙齿显示出严重的磨损和腐烂，这是一个以小行星和太空碎片为食的生物的典型特征。据估计，一般一只虚空虫一生中会生长又脱落三千多颗牙齿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

This tooth shows heavy wear and decay typical of a creature subsisting on a diet of asteroids and space debris. It is estimated that the typical Voidworm will grow and shed over three thousand teeth over the course of its lifetime.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:395`

## fossilized_vultaum_remains

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_vultaum_remains&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆遗骸化石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

Fossilized Vultaum Remains

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:397`

## fossilized_vultaum_remains_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_vultaum_remains_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

被称为沃陶姆的远古蠕虫状感性物种的化石遗骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

The fossilized remains of an ancient worm-like sentient species known as the Vultaum

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:398`

## fossilized_vultaum_remains_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afossilized_vultaum_remains_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

据信该标本是个青年个体。内部生长模式表明沃陶姆会根据个体的健康和营养状况不定期蜕皮。因此我们可以通过检测对应时期的化石来研究每一个生命周期阶段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

It is believed this specimen was a juvenile. Internal growth patterns indicate that the Vultaum would shed their skin at dynamic intervals depending on the individual's health and nutrition. In this way, we can learn about each lifecycle by examining fossils from that period.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:399`

## fractured_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afractured_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

破碎行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

Fractured Planet

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:401`

## fractured_planet_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afractured_planet_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一颗被居住着有意爆破掉的行星残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

The remains of a planet that was intentionally demolished by its own inhabitants

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:402`

## fractured_planet_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afractured_planet_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

至于究竟是何种严峻局势迫使他们做出如此极端的决定已经不得而知了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

Whatever situation drove them to make such a drastic decision has been lost to time.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:403`

## fragment_of_reckoning

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afragment_of_reckoning&lang=en)

### 中文

最终清算之残躯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

Fragment of Reckoning

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:405`

## fragment_of_reckoning_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afragment_of_reckoning_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

强大灵能力量的部分有形残留

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

A partially tangible remnant of great psionic force

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:406`

## fragment_of_reckoning_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afragment_of_reckoning_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们维度中被称为最终清算，这个实体就是给[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]带来毁灭性转变的罪魁祸首。在其消散后不久，它的局部残躯就被一个专注于研究灵能理论的文明所收容。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Known in this dimension as the Reckoning, this entity was responsible for the destructive transformation of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]. This particular fragment was contained by a civilization specializing in Psionic Theory shortly before their demise.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:407`

## fragmented_zroni_archive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afragmented_zroni_archive&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞族档案碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

Fragmented Zroni Archive

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:409`

## fragmented_zroni_archive_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afragmented_zroni_archive_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上找到的一块源自远古泽珞族的大型数据水晶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

A large datacrystal found on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] from an ancient species known as the Zroni

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:410`

## fragmented_zroni_archive_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afragmented_zroni_archive_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它包含着一个具有无上灵能力量的强大文明的一段往事。他们自由穿行虚境的能力是由一位被称为“预见者”的传奇老者所开创的。在虚境内，该物种的能力如此完美以至于会被误认为是某种形式的神祇。不幸的是，后续的数据已经完全损坏了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

It contains a fragmented history of a powerful civilization with an innate psionic ability. Their ability to traverse the Shroud freely was made possible by a breakthrough from a legendary elder known only as the "Far-Seer". Within the Shroud, this species' power was so complete that it could reasonably be mistaken as a form of godhood. Unfortunately, later data is completely corrupted.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:411`

## galactic_megadrive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_megadrive&lang=en)

### 中文

星系超级硬盘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

Galactic Megadrive

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:413`

## galactic_megadrive_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_megadrive_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

过去12亿年间从整个星系收集的监测数据

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

Surveillance data collected from across the galaxy for the past 1.2 billion years

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:414`

## galactic_megadrive_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agalactic_megadrive_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

其中数据压缩的等级高到我们无法访问，除非获得了远超我们理解的科技才行。这种存储科技的构建本身都足以对数字硬件发展提供极具价值的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:321`

### English

The level of data compression used is so advanced that we have no way of accessing it without technology far beyond our understanding. The construction of the storage technology itself still provides remarkable insights in terms of digital hardware development.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:415`

## gamma_resistant_flora

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agamma_resistant_flora&lang=en)

### 中文

辐射异花

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:322`

### English

Gammabloom

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:417`

## gamma_resistant_flora_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agamma_resistant_flora_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一颗暴露在强大伽马射线暴下的行星上为数不多的幸存物种之一，这种植物维管系统中的天然铅含量使其生殖器官呈现出令人愉悦的红色和黄色

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

One of the few surviving species from a planet exposed to a powerful gamma-ray burst, the natural lead content in this plant's vascular system paints its reproductive organs in pleasing shades of reds and yellows

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:418`

## gamma_resistant_flora_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agamma_resistant_flora_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它已经演化出了依靠微小的基因突变来进行无性繁殖的能力，这使得培育这种植物群成为一个危险的命题。我们发现在它的土壤中混入少量的钴-60放射性核素可以让它在母星之外生存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

It has evolved the ability to depend on minor genetic mutations in order to reproduce asexually, which makes cultivating this flora a dangerous proposition. We have found mixing small amounts of Cobalt-60 radionuclides into its soil allows it to survive outside of its home planet.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:419`

## gargantua_megalus

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agargantua_megalus&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大植物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

Gargantua Megalus

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:421`

## gargantua_megalus_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agargantua_megalus_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在经历了剧烈的突变后，这些植物的大小呈指数增长，覆盖了整个[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的表面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

After undergoing dramatic and sudden mutation, these plants increased in size exponentially to envelop the surface of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:422`

## gargantua_megalus_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agargantua_megalus_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这个物种在空气散布满了有毒的花粉，让整个星球都无法呼吸、主导感性物种完全灭绝。这些植物的根非常巨大，可以在行星地幔上凿出深深的裂缝，在某些地区甚至将熔融的岩石吸到了地表。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

Filling the air with virulent pollen, this species suffocated the planet and rendered the dominant sentient species completely extinct. The roots of these plants were large enough to carve deep fissures through the planet's mantle, siphoning molten rock to the surface in some areas.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:423`

## genesis_core

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agenesis_core&lang=en)

### 中文

创世核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

Genesis Core

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:425`

## genesis_core_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agenesis_core_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个强大的中央处理器，充满了无数智慧物种的生成数据

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

A powerful central processing unit filled with generative data on innumerable sapient species

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:426`

## genesis_core_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agenesis_core_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它曾经连接到一个大型跨次元克隆设施，其内部记录表明它曾是某个星系级基因编目项目的一部分。当其初步分析完成后，该人工智能就开始自由创造新的物种以获得更多数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

It was once connected to a massive interdimensional cloning facility, and its internal records show that it was originally part of a galaxy-wide genetic cataloging project. Once its initial analysis was complete, this artificial intelligence began to create new species independently as a means to gather even more data.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:427`

## genetic_code

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agenetic_code&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesName]遗传密码

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesName] Genetic Code

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:429`

## genetic_code_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agenetic_code_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

用于将[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的生理机能推向极限的遗传信息

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

Genetic information used to push the physiology of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesNamePlural] to their limits

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:430`

## genetic_code_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agenetic_code_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的居民最初为战斗目的而被克隆，如今他们致力于挖掘更高的潜能。克隆流程的精准性质使生殖控制能够极其精确而可靠。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

Having been initially cloned for combat purposes, the inhabitants of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] have worked to unlock even higher potentials. The clinical nature of clone processing allows for extremely precise and reliable reproductive control.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:431`

## geometric_water_currents

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ageometric_water_currents&lang=en)

### 中文

聚合物水网

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

Polymer Waterweave

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:434`

## geometric_water_currents_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ageometric_water_currents_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

遍布在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上的绵延不断的聚合物网格的影像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

Images taken of the extensive polymer grids found throughout the seas of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:435`

## geometric_water_currents_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ageometric_water_currents_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些网格可以操纵表面张力和自然水流，以实现复杂而精巧的设计。这种由美学驱动的生态管理形式可以启发我们自己的基础设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

These lattices manipulate the properties of surface tension and natural water currents in order to create complex and intricate designs. This form of aesthetically-driven eco-management could inspire elements of our own infrastructure.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:436`

## giant_egg_shell

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agiant_egg_shell&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大卵壳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

Giant Egg Shell

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:438`

## giant_egg_shell_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agiant_egg_shell_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]地表以下发现的钙化卵壳碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

Shards of calcified eggshell found beneath the surface of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:439`

## giant_egg_shell_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agiant_egg_shell_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

对干蛋白的分析表明，这种生物在孵化期间以数种不稳定的同位素为食。对孵化过程的模拟显示，这种具有放射性的生物从完全成形的厚壳中钻出来，用它硬化的爪子刺穿卵壁上被放射性破坏的薄弱点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

Analysis of dried albumen shows that the creature fed off of an array of unstable isotopes during its incubation period. Simulations of the hatching process show that the radioactive creature emerged from the thick shell fully formed, using its hardened claws to pierce the egg through the most radioactively weakened point along its birth-wall.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:440`

## golden_phonograph_record

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agolden_phonograph_record&lang=en)

### 中文

金色唱片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

Golden Phonograph Record

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:442`

## golden_phonograph_record_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agolden_phonograph_record_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一件古老的数据存储设备，其中储存着来自某个[EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetPreFTLLower]文明的、看似随机的图像与声音序列

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

An archaic data storage device containing a seemingly random series of images and sounds from a [EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetPreFTLLower] civilization

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:443`

## golden_phonograph_record_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agolden_phonograph_record_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它发现于个废弃的行星堡垒的基座上，最初是由一架名为“旅行者”的无人机发射到太空的。它的构造粗糙而简陋，人们认为它具有某种异教意义。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

Discovered on a pedestal within an abandoned planetary stronghold, it was originally launched into space on a drone named "Voyager". Crude and elementary in its construction, it is believed to have had some sort of xeno-religious significance.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:444`

## golem_contortionist_dna

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agolem_contortionist_dna&lang=en)

### 中文

极致柔术家基因

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

Extreme Contortionist DNA

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:446`

## golem_contortionist_dna_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agolem_contortionist_dna_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从星界裂隙另一侧遭遇的一位感性石质生物身上采集的生物样本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

Biological samples taken from a sentient lithoid encountered beyond an Astral Rift

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:447`

## golem_contortionist_dna_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agolem_contortionist_dna_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

该矿物织构含高活性的生物质，易发生自发变形和结晶化。由于该材料高度不稳定，必须一直完美地控制化学平衡，以防止自发的机械致化学燃烧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

The mineral composition contains a volatile biological substance that is easily prone to spontaneous metamorphosis and crystallization. Given the high instability of the material, perfect control of one's chemical balance would be necessary at all times to prevent spontaneous mechanochemical combustion.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:448`

## grunur_data_crystal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agrunur_data_crystal&lang=en)

### 中文

古如努尔数据晶体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

Grunur Data Crystal

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:450`

## grunur_data_crystal_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agrunur_data_crystal_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一块远古数据水晶，是一种早已灭绝的称为古如努尔的物种进行数据储存的首要媒介

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

An ancient datacrystal used as the primary medium of information storage for the long-extinct Grunur species

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:451`

## grunur_data_crystal_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agrunur_data_crystal_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管这些记录年代惊人地久远，但其中的许多部分都保存得相当完整。用于访问该设备的接口已经随着时间的推移而丢失，但合成这种坚固的材料一定是一个漫长的过程。因此，这些晶体应该只用于存储最重要的数据，而更简单易用的存储方法则用于日常使用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

Despite their staggering age, many of these particular records remain fairly intact. The interfaces used to access this device have been lost to time, but it must have been a lengthy process to synthesize such a robust material. It is therefore believed that these crystals were reserved for storing only the most important data, with more practical storage methods employed for everyday use.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:452`

## head_of_the_beast

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahead_of_the_beast&lang=en)

### 中文

巨兽之首

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

Head of the Beast

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:454`

## head_of_the_beast_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahead_of_the_beast_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一只威胁过[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上殖民者安危的危险变异体的遗骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

The remains of a dangerous mutant that terrorized colonists on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:455`

## head_of_the_beast_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahead_of_the_beast_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这只野兽是被一个曾经的未知文明所留下的纳米环境改造机器人意外制造出来的。这只野兽既聪明又恐怖，使用数百具尸体的残肢断臂为自己筑巢。它有足够的思考能力，甚至可以把吃不下的食物囚禁起来之后再吃。如果不是以军事力量加以干涉，这种生物可能会在其母星上变成一种新的超级捕食者而欣欣向荣下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

This beast was created by malfunctioning terraforming nanites that had been left behind by an unknown civilization. As intelligent as it was horrific, this creature sheltered in a nest constructed of hundreds of severed body parts. It was thoughtful enough to keep its extra food as prisoners, storing them for later consumption. Were it not for the intervention of military forces, this organism would have continued to thrive on its homeworld as a new type of super predator.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:456`

## heart_of_the_pulsar

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aheart_of_the_pulsar&lang=en)

### 中文

脉动之心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

Heart of the Pulsar

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:458`

## heart_of_the_pulsar_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aheart_of_the_pulsar_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗珍贵的宝石曾被五个国家的元首佩戴，后来被发现遗弃在一个豪华度度假空间站中

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

Once worn by five active heads of state, this precious jewel was found abandoned in a luxury pleasure station

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:459`

## heart_of_the_pulsar_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aheart_of_the_pulsar_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

材料分析表明它是用超新星喷射出来的碳元素雕成，也许暗喻着即使在星辰的尺度上，死亡中也能蕴含着美。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

Material analysis shows us that it was carved from carbon ejected from a supernova. Perhaps it was meant to metaphorically represent a hidden beauty within death, even on the celestial scale.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:460`

## hemterine_magnetic_figurehead

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahemterine_magnetic_figurehead&lang=en)

### 中文

赫姆特磁性船艏像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

Hemterine Magnetic Figurehead

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:462`

## hemterine_magnetic_figurehead_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahemterine_magnetic_figurehead_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一座以赫姆特圣母为形象的巨大船艏像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

An enormous figurehead of a Hemterine madonna

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:463`

## hemterine_magnetic_figurehead_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahemterine_magnetic_figurehead_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这遵船艏像使用化学修饰磁性锇铸造。它不仅仅是简单的装饰，设计之初是为了吸收敌人炮火的猛烈冲击。它安装在赫穆特泰坦舰的船艏，利用强大的磁力吸引导弹和动能弹丸，以保护舰艇的敏感区域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

This masthead was cast in chemically altered magnetic osmium. More than simple decoration, the sculpture was designed to absorb heavy impacts from enemy fire. Mounted at the bow of a Hemterine Titan, it used powerful magnetic forces to attract missiles and kinetic projectiles to protect sensitive areas of the ship.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:464`

## high_geode_of_the_molluscoids

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahigh_geode_of_the_molluscoids&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]至高晶洞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

High Geode of the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:466`

## high_geode_of_the_molluscoids_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahigh_geode_of_the_molluscoids_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的珍贵水晶洞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

The prized crystallized geode of the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:467`

## high_geode_of_the_molluscoids_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahigh_geode_of_the_molluscoids_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

作为一个由未开智穴居者组成的社会，[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]长期以来都以出土矿物的品相来彰显社会地位。这块美丽的岩石曾陈设于他们最大居所中最显眼的位置，如今则是与[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]合作的象征。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:361`

### English
