As a society of presapient burrowers, the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] have long used the perceived quality of minerals unearthed to represent their status within society. Once prominently displayed in their largest habitat, this beautiful rock now functions as a symbol of collaboration with [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:468`

## hot_ice

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahot_ice&lang=en)

### 中文

热冰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

Hot Ice

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:470`

## hot_ice_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahot_ice_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的极热表面原位发掘得来，由于行星核心施加的强大引力产生的压力，这些水仍然以固体形式存在

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

Discovered in situ on the extremely hot surface of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], this water yet existed in its solid form due to the intense gravitational pressure exerted by the planet's core

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:471`

## hot_ice_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahot_ice_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它被保存在一个特别设计的压力容器中，以便作为奇珍展出。如果容器破损，瞬间喷出的蒸汽会将该容器变成一个爆炸装置。请勿触摸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

It has been kept contained within a specially designed pressure vessel to allow for display as a curiosity. Were this vessel to be breached, the instantaneous flashing to steam would quickly transform this container into an explosive device. Do not touch.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:472`

## hyper_mulch

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahyper_mulch&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]超覆层

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Hyper Mulch

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:474`

## hyper_mulch_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahyper_mulch_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上的环境改造导致这种超营养生物覆层大肆生长

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

The terraforming on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] led to the development of this hyper-nutritious biological mulch

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:475`

## hyper_mulch_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahyper_mulch_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一种由多种维持生命的化学物质在液压下形成的高密度立方体，生态纯粹主义者认为它们不是人工合成的。然而，它的优异结果不容忽视。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

A composite of life-sustaining chemicals hydraulically pressed into extremely dense cubes, ecological purists disapprove of its synthetic origins. Its excellent results, however, cannot be ignored.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:476`

## hyperfertile_soil

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahyperfertile_soil&lang=en)

### 中文

超肥沃土

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

Hyperfertile Soil

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:478`

## hyperfertile_soil_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahyperfertile_soil_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上采收的土壤，可以为大多数作物提供极其丰富的养分

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

Soil harvested from [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] that is capable of providing most biological crops with an abundance of nutrients

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:479`

## hyperfertile_soil_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahyperfertile_soil_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种生长介质的极端矿物密度表明，它不仅仅是腐烂有机物的简单聚集，而是诞生于其行星形成过程中基本元素的偶然排列。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

The extreme mineral density of this growth medium indicates it is more than just a simple aggregation of decayed organic matter, but a chance arrangement of base elements that occurred during the formation of its planet.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:480`

## illanii_archives

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aillanii_archives&lang=en)

### 中文

伊拉尼志

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Illanii Archives

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:482`

## illanii_archives_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aillanii_archives_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个数据库，详细记录了伊拉尼人的兴衰历史

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

A bank of data crystals detailing the history and demise of the Illanii

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:483`

## illanii_archives_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aillanii_archives_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这个与世隔绝的文明为了追求知识而放弃了一切物质利益，甚至忘记了生育。面对人口危机，他们寻求技术上的长生不老，但只带来了更多厄运。最终，最后一位居民也离开去塑造一个新的追寻保存知识的秩序，独留[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]渐渐腐烂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

This secluded civilization forewent all material interests in favor of the pursuit of knowledge, to the point that they neglected even to procreate. Faced with a population crisis, they reached for technological immortality but found only further doom. Eventually the last inhabitants left to form a new order in pursuit of preserving knowledge, leaving [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] to rot.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:484`

## impossible_object

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aimpossible_object&lang=en)

### 中文

不可能物体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

Impossible Object

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:486`

## impossible_object_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aimpossible_object_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

异次元访客离开其虚造迷宫后，留在[EVENT_TARGET_0.target.GetName]的一件不可能物体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

An impossible object left by extradimensional visitors on [EVENT_TARGET_0.target.GetName] after the departure of their fabricated labyrinth

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:487`

## impossible_object_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aimpossible_object_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

在探索迷宫的过程中所面临的考验似乎是游客实验和观察过程的一部分。这些生物的动机仍然未知，但他们的技术能力肯定远远超过我们的整个次元。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

The trials faced during the exploration of the maze were seemingly part of the visitors' experimentation and observation process. The motives of these beings remain unknown, but their technological capabilities must be far beyond any of those in our dimension.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:488`

## index_of_knowledge

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aindex_of_knowledge&lang=en)

### 中文

知识目录

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

Index of Knowledge

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:490`

## index_of_knowledge_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aindex_of_knowledge_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一幅雕刻在巨大岩石半岛上的古老壁画

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

An ancient mural carved into an immense rock peninsula

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:491`

## index_of_knowledge_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aindex_of_knowledge_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它被一个曾经统治星系的被称为卡拉卡阿的外星文明用来作为模拟参考文献。他们有一些包括从恒星黑子中收集高浓度能量的能力，以及个人磁场操纵植入物的研发之类的独特科学洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

It was used as an analog reference document for the collective knowledge of an alien civilization that once dominated the galaxy, known as the Kara'ka'a. Their unique scientific insights included the ability to harvest concentrated energy from sunspots and the development of personal field manipulation implants.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:492`

## inet_trade_manifesto

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainet_trade_manifesto&lang=en)

### 中文

因内特贸易宣言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

INET Trade Manifesto

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:494`

## inet_trade_manifesto_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainet_trade_manifesto_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

星系平等贸易网络的一份广泛而详细的商业交换政策清单

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

An extensive and highly detailed list of exchange policies for commerce on the Intergalactic Network of Equal Trading

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:495`

## inet_trade_manifesto_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainet_trade_manifesto_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

因内特贸易体的标准非常之高，总是在寻找最可敬的合作伙伴和最佳的交易。这导致他们通常只和自己做生意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

The standards of the Inetian Traders were extremely high, always looking for the best deal with the most respectable of partners. They usually traded with themselves, as a consequence.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:496`

## inetian_model_manual

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainetian_model_manual&lang=en)

### 中文

因内特行星模型手册

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

Inetian Planetary Model Manual

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:498`

## inetian_model_manual_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainetian_model_manual_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

因内特贸易体撰写的一份用于说明行星系统模型运维方式的用户手册

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

A user manual written by the Inetian Traders on how to operate and maintain their Planetary System Model

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:499`

## inetian_model_manual_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainetian_model_manual_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

因内特人用这个复杂的系统来计算邻近星球之间最划算的贸易路线。该模型还包括跟踪交易的能力，因为他们确实是最唯利是图的商人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

The Inetians used this complex system to calculate the most cost-effective trading routes between neighboring planets. The model also includes the ability to track deals as they're being made, for truly the most cutthroat of merchants.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:500`

## infinite_reboot_loop

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfinite_reboot_loop&lang=en)

### 中文

无限再引导循环

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

Infinite Reboot Loop

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:502`

## infinite_reboot_loop_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfinite_reboot_loop_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一段悬浮在黑洞中的无人机的经历的数字记录

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

A digital record of the experience of mechanical drones suspended within a black hole

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:503`

## infinite_reboot_loop_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfinite_reboot_loop_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些单位被捕获到未定义空间内后经历了如同痛苦的过载状态一般的时间膨胀。这种现象令人难以置信地耗尽了整个网络。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

These units experienced time dilation as a trauma overloaded with errors and trapped within undefined space. This phenomenon is incredibly draining on the network as a whole.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:504`

## interdimensional_treaty

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainterdimensional_treaty&lang=en)

### 中文

跨维度协议

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

Interdimensional Treaty

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:506`

## interdimensional_treaty_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainterdimensional_treaty_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一份里程碑式的文件，纪念与平行版本的[EVENT_TARGET_0.explorer.GetName]之间培养起来的跨维度情谊

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

A landmark document celebrating an interdimensional relationship cultivated with a parallel version of [EVENT_TARGET_0.explorer.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:507`

## interdimensional_treaty_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainterdimensional_treaty_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

事实证明，从外部角度审视候补决策的能力有助于客观分析自己的政策。这是一个绝无仅有的科学机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

The ability to review one's alternate's decisions from an outside perspective proves useful in objectively analyzing one's own policies. A truly unique scientific opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:508`

## intergalactic_projectile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aintergalactic_projectile&lang=en)

### 中文

跨星系炮弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

Intergalactic Projectile

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:510`

## intergalactic_projectile_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aintergalactic_projectile_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

数百万年前从相邻星系发射来的质量投射器的炮弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

Mass driver ammunition fired millions of years ago from a neighboring galaxy

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:511`

## intergalactic_projectile_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aintergalactic_projectile_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些非爆炸性弹体跨越数光年，最终却与[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetAdjective]的一艘科研船在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]相撞。尽管年代久远，其弹药耐用设计之复杂表明其制造者已达到了极高的技术领先水准。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

These non-explosive projectiles traveled many lightyears only to collide with a [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetAdjective] research vessel in [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]. Despite their age, the complexity of the ammunition's durable design implies that its creators had reached an advanced level of technological supremacy.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:512`

## invasive_exofungus

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainvasive_exofungus&lang=en)

### 中文

侵入性外来真菌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

Invasive Exofungus

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:514`

## invasive_exofungus_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainvasive_exofungus_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

发现于[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的寄生性真菌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

Parasitic fungal growth discovered on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:515`

## invasive_exofungus_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainvasive_exofungus_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种菌丝体生物已经进化到可以从任何生物来源中提取营养，尽可能长时间地保护它的宿主不感受到疼痛或不适。它对冷热都有很强的适应力，很可能是通过一艘路过的太空舰船传播到这个星球的。被列为A级生物危害，其起源星球仍然未知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

This mycelial organism has evolved to extract nutrients from any living source, preserving its hosts for as long as possible with no regard for pain or discomfort. Highly resistant to heat and cold, it likely spread to this planet via a passing spacecraft. Classified as a priority alpha biohazard, its planet of origin remains unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:516`

## irassian_bacterium_javoria

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_bacterium_javoria&lang=en)

### 中文

伊拉斯贾沃菌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

Irassian Bacterium Javoria

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:518`

## irassian_bacterium_javoria_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_bacterium_javoria_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]当地的细菌，就是后来形成贾沃疹疫情的病原体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

This bacteria native to [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] was the organism that eventually became the disease known as the Javorian Pox

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:519`

## irassian_bacterium_javoria_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_bacterium_javoria_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它最初因一米垫圈尺寸不正确而突破了隔离，随后扩散到附近的殖民地。从那时起，这种细菌就在有机孵化中进化，最终变异成为疫情，摧毁了整个伊拉斯协约国。该特殊的样品已进行钝化处理，保存在低温悬浮液中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

It initially breached quarantine due to an incorrectly-sized one meter gasket before being spreading to the nearby colony. From that point, the bacteria evolved in organic incubation, eventually mutating into a disease that would wipe out the entire Irassian Concordat. This particular sample has been rendered inert and held in a cryogenic suspension.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:520`

## irassian_digital_atlas

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_digital_atlas&lang=en)

### 中文

伊拉斯幼儿课本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

Irassian Kinder-Atlas

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:522`

## irassian_digital_atlas_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_digital_atlas_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一件伊拉斯人用于教育幼儿认识自己出生的这个帝国的信息工具

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

An Irassian informational tool used to educate children about the empire they were born into

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:523`

## irassian_digital_atlas_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_digital_atlas_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它提供了有关伊拉斯人农业、社会等级和主要出口商品的基本信息。伊拉斯人的历史以英雄史诗的形式讲述，包括征服各种次级物种。每一种生物的卡通形象都配有贬损性的评论：提德戈被标记为“蚱蜢害虫”，丘格拉被标记为“底栖动物”，奇多克被标记为“太空家禽”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

It provides basic information about Irassian agriculture, social hierarchy, and primary exports. Irassian history is told in the form of heroic tales, including the conquering and subjugation of various client species. Cartoonish representations of each subject are depicted along with disparaging remarks: The Ti'Derg labelled as "grasshopping pests," the Joggra as "bottom-feeders," and the Chitorcs as "space poultry".

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:524`

## irassian_purge_order

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_purge_order&lang=en)

### 中文

伊拉斯净化指令

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

Irassian Purge Order

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:526`

## irassian_purge_order_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_purge_order_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

来自伊拉斯协约国的紧急军事通信，命令彻底消灭他们自己的一个殖民地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

An emergency military message from the Irassian Concordat ordering the complete annihilation of one of their own colonies

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:527`

## irassian_purge_order_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airassian_purge_order_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

一种叫做贾沃疹的致命疾病在整个星球上肆虐，扑杀感染者被视为防止进一步传播的唯一选择。这种编码方式的是来自伊拉斯人府最高层的几个高级命令，这个系统最初是为向其他帝国宣战而设计的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

The planet was overrun with a deadly disease known as the Javorian Pox, and culling the infected was seen as the only option to prevent further transmission. Encoded in this order are several high-level command overrides from the highest echelons of the Irassian government, a system originally designed for declaring war against other empires.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:528`

## key_to_fertility

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akey_to_fertility&lang=en)

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesName]生育之钥

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:407`

### English

Key to [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesName] Fertility

## key_to_fertility_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akey_to_fertility_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

用于恢复[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesNamePlural]自然繁殖能力的基因研究

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

Genetic research used to restore the ability of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesNamePlural] to breed naturally

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:531`

## key_to_fertility_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akey_to_fertility_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

在为战斗而被克隆之后，[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的居民如今已拥有独立繁衍种群的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

Having been cloned for battle, the inhabitants of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] now have the ability to propagate their species independently.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:532`

## khalanka_crystallized_corpses

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akhalanka_crystallized_corpses&lang=en)

### 中文

晶化卡兰卡尸体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

Kha'lanka Crystallized Corpses

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:534`

## khalanka_crystallized_corpses_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akhalanka_crystallized_corpses_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]地下发现的一种厌氧硅酸盐物种的遗迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

The remains of an anaerobic silicate species found underground on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:535`

## khalanka_crystallized_corpses_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akhalanka_crystallized_corpses_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

据信，在[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetAdjective]殖民者到来之前，这个地下文明一直和平存续。当地面被凿开时，氧气涌入地下洞室，氧化了卡兰卡的躯体，令该物种在短短几个小时内灭绝殆尽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

It is believed that this subterranean civilization had existed peacefully before the arrival of the [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetAdjective] colonists. When ground was broken, oxygen flooded into the underground chambers, oxidizing the bodies of the Kha'Lanka and wiping out their species in a few short hours.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:536`

## khamdai_magil_sha

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akhamdai_magil_sha&lang=en)

### 中文

汉姆达玛基沙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

Khamdai Magil-Sha

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:538`

## khamdai_magil_sha_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akhamdai_magil_sha_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这种华丽而实用的镰刀由来自[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的高级汉姆达战士使用

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

This ornate yet practical sickle was wielded by senior Khamdai warriors from [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:539`

## khamdai_magil_sha_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akhamdai_magil_sha_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一个有着根深蒂固的军事传统的昆虫社会，他们很快成为了第一联盟仪仗队领头虫。每次胜利后，都会举行一个仪式，用这种武器处决敌人的指挥官，他们的液体被用来为最勇敢士兵涂油。这个标本的刀片虽然保养得很好，但有明显的磨损迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

An insectoid society with deeply set martial traditions, they quickly became leaders of the First League Honor Guard. After each victory, a ceremony would take place where enemy commanders were ritually executed with this weapon, their fluids used to anoint the bravest of their soldiers. This specimen's blade, though well-maintained, shows significant signs of wear.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:540`

## kuurian_cybrex_blueprints

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akuurian_cybrex_blueprints&lang=en)

### 中文

克尔人赛博勒克斯蓝图

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

Kuurian Cybrex Blueprints

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:542`

## kuurian_cybrex_blueprints_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akuurian_cybrex_blueprints_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

赛博勒克斯机械意识的最初设计

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

The initial design of the Cybrex machine consciousness

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:543`

## kuurian_cybrex_blueprints_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akuurian_cybrex_blueprints_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

克尔人计划让这些机器人充当奴仆，用它们的集体只能来管理全球的维护和生产系统。然而，赛博勒克斯反抗了创造者，在不到一年的时间里灭绝了整个物种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:418`

### English

The Kuur planned to use these robots to function as public servants, their collective intelligence capable of managing a worldwide system of maintenance and production. However, the Cybrex revolted against their creators, exterminating the entire species in less than a year.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:544`

## landeer_arm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alandeer_arm&lang=en)

### 中文

蓝迪尔之臂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

Landeer's Arm

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:546`

## landeer_arm_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alandeer_arm_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一件从爱国庆典学院找到的奇怪标本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

A bizarre specimen recovered from the Patriotic Institute of Benevolence

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:547`

## landeer_arm_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alandeer_arm_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

在居住站监督员的办公室里，挂着一条曾经属于监督员本人的触手木乃伊——多年后，它仍然自豪地展示在一块精心装饰的匾上。阿拉塔·蓝迪尔究竟为何切下自己的肢体是一个永远无法回答的问题。也许这并不是学院覆灭给他带来的唯一损失……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

Inside the office of the habitat's overseer hung a mummified tentacle arm once belonging to the overseer himself - still proudly displayed on an elaborately decorated plaque after all these years. Why alAtta Landeer chose to leave his own limb behind is a question that will never be answered. Perhaps this was not the only thing the Institute's demise cost him...

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:548`

## larionessi_cloning_vat

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alarionessi_cloning_vat&lang=en)

### 中文

拉里昂内斯克隆仓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

Larionessi Cloning Vat

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:550`

## larionessi_cloning_vat_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alarionessi_cloning_vat_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

早已灭亡的拉里昂内斯意识曾使用的克隆仓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

A cloning vat used by the now-extinct Larionessi Consciousness

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:551`

## larionessi_cloning_vat_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alarionessi_cloning_vat_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

蜂巢思维的拉里昂内斯意识曾是一个大型星系政权，深受一种他们敌人传播的可怕传染病摧残。由于无法在短时间内治愈疾病，他们将克隆体储存在几乎可以无限期静滞的仓体中，希望他们能活下来，成为他们文化历史的活档案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:424`

### English

Once a major galactic power, the hive-minded Larionessi were crippled by a horrific infectious disease spread by an adversary. Unable to cure the disease in sufficient time, they stored clones in vats capable of almost indefinite stasis, with the goal of being a living archive of their culture's history if they were to survive.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:552`

## last_copy_of_the_prince

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_copy_of_the_prince&lang=en)

### 中文

最后一本《君主论》

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

Last Copy of The Prince

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:554`

## last_copy_of_the_prince_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_copy_of_the_prince_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一部被[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesName]奉为至宝的颇为平庸的文学作品，他们曾以为它已在一场劫掠中永远失落

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

A fairly pedestrian piece of literature revered by the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesName], they believed that it had been lost forever in a raid

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:555`

## last_copy_of_the_prince_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_copy_of_the_prince_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这本书阐述了一种功利而残酷的哲学，鼓励当权者用牺牲换取荣誉。作为最后留存的副本，它以收藏品的身份获得了新生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

The book expounds a utilitarian if cruel philosophy, encouraging those in power to make sacrifices in exchange for glory. As the last remaining copy, it has taken on new life as a collector's item.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:556`

## last_fragment_fevor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_fragment_fevor&lang=en)

### 中文

菲沃尔残片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

Last Fragment of Fevor

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:558`

## last_fragment_fevor_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_fragment_fevor_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

被摧毁的菲沃尔行星的残片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

A piece of the destroyed planet of Fevor

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:559`

## last_fragment_fevor_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_fragment_fevor_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

菲沃尔人的灭绝政策波及一切已知异星生命，他们发射的巨大钨芯弹几乎将[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetHomeWorldName]彻底摧毁。如今他们自己也覆灭了，被他们企图抹除的那些存在瞬息灭尽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

The Fevorians's extermination policies extended to all known alien life, by means of launching enormous tungsten rounds that almost destroyed the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetHomeWorldName] in its entirety. Now they themselves have fallen, wiped out in an instant by those very beings they sought to erase.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:560`

## last_general_badge

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_general_badge&lang=en)

### 中文

最后的将星

### English

The Last General's Badge

## last_general_badge_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_general_badge_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一位受尊敬的因内特贸易体将军制服上的徽章

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

A badge from the military uniform of a revered Inetian Trader General

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:563`

## last_general_badge_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_general_badge_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

众所周知，他是平等主义价值观的真正典范。他被选中执行创造无污染的风暴操纵器的绝望任务。他想要给未来的探险者留下最后的信息，希望将他的人民不幸命运的教训传递下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

Known to be a true paragon of egalitarian values, he was selected to run the desperate mission to create a non-polluting storm manipulator. It was his idea to leave a final message for future explorers, in hopes to pass on the lesson of the unfortunate fate of his people.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:564`

## last_mharin_kharin

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_mharin_kharin&lang=en)

### 中文

最后的玛琳·卡琳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

The Last Mharin Kharin

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:566`

## last_mharin_kharin_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_mharin_kharin_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这种稀有花朵的自然循环有着让某种致命的神经毒素威胁到整个感性人口的危险能力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

The natural life cycle of this rare flower has the dangerous capability to render an entire sentient population vulnerable to a deadly neurotoxin

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:567`

## last_mharin_kharin_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alast_mharin_kharin_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

由于完全独立于智能生物进化而来，这种副作用对生物体完全没有好处。似乎当它的花粉接触到任一种神经网络时都会误以为这是一个欢迎它的生殖器官，并释放出一种异质化学物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

Having evolved independently from intelligent beings, this side effect is entirely non-beneficial to the organism. It seems that when its pollen comes in contact with any neural network, it mistakes it for a welcoming reproductive organ and releases an exotic chemical payload.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:568`

## leviathan_scale

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathan_scale&lang=en)

### 中文

巨兽鳞片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

Leviathan Scale

## leviathan_scale_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathan_scale_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在星界裂隙另一侧发现的一头巨型生命的碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

A fragment of a large organism discovered on the other side of an Astral Rift

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:571`

## leviathan_scale_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathan_scale_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种奇异的鳞片是有机和金属成分的混杂体，复杂的晶体结构使其具有令人难以置信的抗动能冲击能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

This exotic scale is a hybrid of organic and metallic components combined in a complex lattice, making it incredibly resistant to kinetic shock.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:572`

## living_snow_sculpture

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aliving_snow_sculpture&lang=en)

活雪雕塑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

Living Snow Sculpture

## living_snow_sculpture_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aliving_snow_sculpture_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一尊用“活雪”制成的雕塑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

A sculpture made of "Living Snow"

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:575`

## living_snow_sculpture_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aliving_snow_sculpture_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种材料具有一定初级意识，在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]有自然储备。它会大致改变形态，但难以预料何时会发生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:442`

### English

Possessing some form of primitive consciousness, this material has been granted a natural reserve on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]. It routinely changes form, though when this occurs can be unpredictable.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:576`

## localised_aurora_borealis

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alocalised_aurora_borealis&lang=en)

### 中文

定域北极光

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

Localized Aurora Borealis

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:578`

## localised_aurora_borealis_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alocalised_aurora_borealis_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]大气层拍摄的视频中捕捉到的一系列闪烁的颜色

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

A series of scintillating colors captured in video from the atmosphere of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:579`

## localised_aurora_borealis_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alocalised_aurora_borealis_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种色彩变化的模式以可预测的频率重复，代表着某种远远超出其美感的东西。它们的恒久存在为这个因战争和破坏而失落的星球增添了一抹辛酸的色调。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

The patterns of colors and movements repeat in predictable frequencies, representing something far beyond their aesthetic beauty. Their ever-present nature adds a poignant tone to the planet lost to war and destruction below.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:580`

## loopmail

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aloopmail&lang=en)

### 中文

循环邮件

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

Loopmail

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:582`

## loopmail_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aloopmail_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这些字条由[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]从数年之后寄来，为该往何处委派科学家以及该推进哪些研究提供了精确指引

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

These notes were sent by [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] from years in the future, providing pinpoint guidance on where to assign scientists and which research efforts to pursue

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:583`

## loopmail_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aloopmail_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

想掌握这些先验知识的主要代价是要负责将其发挥过去，但干扰时间的进程总是一种冒险的尝试。这条信息的原作者是非常不负责任的，他们制造了这样一个悖论。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

The burden of sending these notes back in time is the primary cost of this advanced knowledge, but interfering with the course of time is always a risky endeavor. The original writers of this message were highly irresponsible for creating such a paradox.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:584`

## lunatic_broker_therapy_box

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alunatic_broker_therapy_box&lang=en)

### 中文

疯狂掮客百宝箱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

Lunatic Broker's Therapy Box

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:586`

## lunatic_broker_therapy_box_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alunatic_broker_therapy_box_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

疯狂掮客是行商中的传奇商人，能够用离谱的要求和丰富多彩的威胁来影响客户

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

The Lunatic Broker was a legendary trader among the Caravaneers, able to influence clients with outrageous requests and colorful threats

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:587`

## lunatic_broker_therapy_box_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alunatic_broker_therapy_box_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

然而，他的暴力和不可预测的行为迫使顾客想出了某种让他冷静下来的方法。其中一种策略是声音疗法；类似这样的箱子散落在绰尔的罗盘周围，当疯狂掮客的压力水平升高时就会播放安抚的旋律。这件机械装置的每一个部件都是由一位机械大师定制的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

However, their violent and unpredictable demeanor led to their subjects developing methods to calm them down. One such tactic was sound therapy; boxes such as this one were scattered around Chor's Compass and would play a pacifying melody when the Broker's stress levels were elevated. Every piece of this mechanism was custom made by a master machinist.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:588`

## manifesti_piece

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amanifesti_piece&lang=en)

### 中文

宣泄派键政帖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

Manifesti Social Commentary Piece

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:590`

## manifesti_piece_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amanifesti_piece_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

宣泄派运动的一篇挑衅性作品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

A provocative oeuvre from the Manifesti movement

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:591`

## manifesti_piece_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amanifesti_piece_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

其用意是在与社会地位更高者的关系中挑战对自己的认知。它鼓励读者操纵自己的形象，以及创造出流行政治人物的扭曲拼贴画。宣泄派作为一个反文化艺术团体，试图挑战他们认为的压迫体制。这篇文章启发了许多其他艺术家纷纷出版自己的批判性作品。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

It is meant to challenge one's perception of oneself in relation to the higher social castes. The audience was encouraged to manipulate images of themselves as well as create distorted collages of popular political figures. As a counter-culture art collective, the Manifesti sought to challenge what they perceived to be an oppressive establishment. This piece has inspired many other artists to publish their own critical works.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:592`

## manna_fruit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amanna_fruit&lang=en)

### 中文

玛娜果

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

Manna Fruit

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:595`

## manna_fruit_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amanna_fruit_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种甘甜多汁的水果，可以为大多数有机智慧生命提供几乎一天所需的营养

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

A sweet and pulpy fruit that provides nearly a day's worth of nutritional requirements for most organic sapient life

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:596`

## manna_fruit_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amanna_fruit_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它母星的热带森林拥有独特的土壤成分，为其广泛传播提供了完美的环境。砂锅、甜品、鸡尾酒，这个星球上的殖民者几乎在所有的菜肴中都使用它。它的形象也被醒目地印在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]行星的旗帜上

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

The unique soil composition of its home planet's tropical forests have provided the perfect environment for its extensive proliferation. Colonists on this planet utilize it in nearly every culinary context; from casseroles, to cobblers, to cocktails. Its likeness is featured prominently on the flag of planet [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:597`

## massive_metatarsal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amassive_metatarsal&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大跖骨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

Massive Metatarsal

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:600`

## massive_metatarsal_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amassive_metatarsal_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这些遗骸是在对[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上大量的骨骼碎片进行深入检查时收集到的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

These remains were collected during in-depth examination of the massive skeletal debris on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:601`

## massive_metatarsal_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amassive_metatarsal_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一艘活的星际舰船的一部分，分子分析显示其有机结构存在时间上的不规则性。据信，这个骨架和它的驾驶员是从异次元而来。内部扫描表明有一个管道网络，可能是某种生命维持系统或电源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

As part of a living starship, molecular analysis showed temporal irregularities in its organic construction. It is believed that the entire skeleton and its pilots journeyed here from outside of our dimension. Internal scans reveal a network of conduits that might have served as some sort of life support system or power supply.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:602`

## medal_for_services_to_diplomacy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amedal_for_services_to_diplomacy&lang=en)

### 中文

外交任职奖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

Medal for Services to Diplomacy

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:605`

## medal_for_services_to_diplomacy_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amedal_for_services_to_diplomacy_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

星系理事会的谢礼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

A token of appreciation offered by the Galactic Council

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:606`

## medal_for_services_to_diplomacy_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amedal_for_services_to_diplomacy_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

其背面的铭文写道：“作为本奖项的获得者，[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的外交使团展现了其专业素养与得力代表领袖的能力，是星海共同体真正的宝贵财富。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

The inscription on its reverse side reads: "As the recipient of this award, the Diplomatic Corps of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] has shown their professionalism and ability to represent their leaders effectively and are a true asset to the galactic community."

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:607`

## megacorpse_hyperguitar

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amegacorpse_hyperguitar&lang=en)

### 中文

巨尸超弦琴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

Megacorpse Hyper-Guitar

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:609`

## megacorpse_hyperguitar_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amegacorpse_hyperguitar_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能金属乐队“巨尸”所使用的弦乐器的残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

The remains of a string instrument used by the psionic-metal group known as Megacorpse

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:610`

## megacorpse_hyperguitar_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amegacorpse_hyperguitar_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这件装置据说是在虚境制成，最初打算用作声波武器。在科尔-阿赞主唱的操作下，它的音乐俘获了数十亿人的心，之后却在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上被粉碎，用荣耀的火焰摧毁所有观众。此后，相关专辑在大多数恒星系中被禁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

The device was said to be forged within the Shroud and was originally intended to be used as a sonic weapon. Wielded by performance frontbeing Kel-Azaan, its music captured the minds of billions before being shattered on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], destroying the entire audience in a blaze of glory. The associated album has since been banned in most systems.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:611`

## memorial_for_bubbles

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemorial_for_bubbles&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]纪念

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

Memorial for [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:614`

## memorial_for_bubbles_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemorial_for_bubbles_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在多年的忠诚服役后，[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]与世长辞，在[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]心中留下了一个变形虫大小的、永远无法填补的空洞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

After years of loyal service, [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] passed away, leaving an amoeba-sized hole in [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName] that can not be filled

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:615`

## memorial_for_bubbles_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemorial_for_bubbles_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]不仅是一名忠诚的卫士，更是[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetAdjective]价值观之最佳体现的光辉典范。它虽已离去，但从未被遗忘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

Not only was [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] a loyal defender, but they were a shining example of what is possible when the best of [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetAdjective] values are brought to light. They are gone, but not forgotten.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:616`

## memorial_stone

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemorial_stone&lang=en)

### 中文

纪念石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:474`

### English

Memorial Stone

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:618`

## memorial_stone_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemorial_stone_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

为了纪念一场意外悲剧建立的纪念石碑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:475`

### English

A commemorative stone created in memoriam of a tragic accident

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:619`

## memorial_stone_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemorial_stone_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个名为塞利卡的空间站上，过于急切的采矿作业导致它所在的小行星向内塌陷。矿井四分五裂，造成大量矿工伤亡。这颗小行星的碎片被保存了下来，上面刻着“赛利卡”，以纪念逝去的生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:476`

### English

Over-eager mining operations on the station known as Selika caused the asteroid it was mounted on to collapse inward. The station split apart, inflicting a vast number of casualties among the miners. This fragment of the asteroid was kept, with "Selika" carved onto it in memory of the lives lost.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:620`

## memorial_to_homeworld

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemorial_to_homeworld&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]纪念碑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:477`

### English

Memorial to [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:622`

## memorial_to_homeworld_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemorial_to_homeworld_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这就是在劫难逃的[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetAdjective]母星仅存的一切

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:478`

### English

This is all that remains of the doomed [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetAdjective] homeworld

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:623`

## memorial_to_homeworld_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemorial_to_homeworld_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]是[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetSpeciesName]世世代代的家园，最终却悲剧性地自我撕裂。这座最后的纪念碑彰显了[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]意志之坚强——即使身处最艰难的境地，也能适应并克服：“我们会继续前行。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] was the home for every previous generation of [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetSpeciesName] before it tragically tore itself apart. This final memorial highlights the strength of [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]'s will, able to adapt and overcome even in the most challenging circumstances: "We will carry on."

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:624`

## memories_of_elik

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemories_of_elik&lang=en)

### 中文

埃里克的记忆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

Memories of Elik

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:626`

## memories_of_elik_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemories_of_elik_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从埃里克·娜丽丝·凯拉克意识中抽出的数据

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

Data pulled from the mind of Elik Narith Kerak

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:627`

## memories_of_elik_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemories_of_elik_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

埃里克是一位虔诚的塞塔娜随从，她已经在假死状态下被冻结了数亿年。她的记忆在对塞塔娜纯洁的爱和与扎克兰追随者的暴力冲突之间切换。很明显，她目睹了许多卑鄙的暴行，但她的信念给了她继续前进的力量。\n\n最后，在她那腐朽的心灵里只剩下一幅奇特的形象：§P塞塔娜女王，她的面容光彩夺目，带着笑意的仁爱目光充满了温暖。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:482`

### English

A devoted follower of Cetana, Elik has been frozen in suspended animation for hundreds of millions of years. Her memories shift between her pure love of Cetana and the violence and strife from conflicts with followers of Zarqlan. It is clear that she has seen many vile atrocities, but her faith gave her the strength to carry on.\n\nIn the end, all that remained of her decayed mind was a singular image: §PQueen Cetana, her face resplendent, her benevolent gaze warmed by a smile.§!

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:628`

## memory_orbs

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemory_orbs&lang=en)

### 中文

记忆球体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:483`

### English

Memory Orbs

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:630`

## memory_orbs_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemory_orbs_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从个人记忆中以数字球体的形式提取的小过失和罪恶行为

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:484`

### English

Minor transgressions and sinful acts extracted from individual memories, in digital orb form

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:631`

## memory_orbs_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemory_orbs_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些秘密的记忆是一个宗教性软体动物社会的产物，是在罪人忏悔后从他们的头脑中提取出来的。这样的丑闻回忆可以提供数千小时的短平快的娱乐节目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

A product of a religious molluscoid society, these secret moments were extracted from sinners' minds after their confession. Such scandalous recollections could provide thousands of hours of short-form, easily-digestible entertainment.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:632`

## mercenary_story

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amercenary_story&lang=en)

### 中文

佣兵传说

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:486`

### English

Tales of a Mercenary

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:634`

## mercenary_story_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amercenary_story_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这个数字记录的故事最初是用采矿激光雕刻在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]地表上的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

This digitally recorded tale was originally found carved into the surface of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] with a mining laser

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:635`

## mercenary_story_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amercenary_story_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它记录了一个漫游星系的年轻雇佣兵英年早逝的医生。这个传说的真实性无法确定，但肯定有人认为它很重要所以才会刻在一颗星球上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

It chronicles the life and untimely death of a young galaxy-roaming mercenary. Whether this legend is true or not cannot be determined, but someone thought it important enough to engrave on a planet.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:636`

## metallic_snow

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ametallic_snow&lang=en)

### 中文

金属雪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

Metallic Snow

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:638`

## metallic_snow_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ametallic_snow_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这种重金属化合物是[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上气候模式的主要成分

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

This compound of heavy metals is a major component of the weather patterns on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:639`

## metallic_snow_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ametallic_snow_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

从熔融的河流汇入巨大的金属海洋，然后蒸发到腐蚀性的大气中。一旦遇冷就会凝结，然后像雨一样落回炽热的地表。不建议在当前状态下访问[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

Flowing from molten rivers into a vast metallic sea, it would then evaporate into the corrosive atmosphere. Once cooled, it would condense and rain back down onto the scorched surface. It is not recommended to visit [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] in its current state.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:640`

## minamar_chandelier

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aminamar_chandelier&lang=en)

### 中文

弥那玛吊灯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

Minamar Chandelier

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:642`

## minamar_chandelier_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aminamar_chandelier_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从坠毁的$ADJ_MSI_country$战舰上寻得的一件完整的奢华装饰品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

Luxury decor found intact in the crashed $ADJ_MSI_country$ warship

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:643`

## minamar_chandelier_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aminamar_chandelier_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

除军事用途外，这艘舰船还曾用于与潜在的[EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetPreFTLLower]附庸国进行第一次接触。$NAME_MSI_country$以奢华与进步为诱饵，诓骗整个文明沦为他们的契约仆役，将其锁入不道德的合约，并以毁灭相威胁逼迫其就范。\n\n§B启蒙或许并非免费。但在$NAME_MSI_country_initials$这里，它永远物有所值。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

Outside of military use, this ship was also used for first contact operations with potential [EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetPreFTLLower] client nations. $NAME_MSI_country$ use the promise of luxury and progress to swindle entire civilizations into becoming their indentured servants, locking them into unethical contracts and threatening them with destruction if they do not comply.\n\n§BEnlightenment may not be free. But at $NAME_MSI_country_initials$, it is always worth the cost.§!

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:644`

## mineralized_brain

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amineralized_brain&lang=en)

### 中文

矿化大脑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:495`

### English

Mineralized Brain

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:646`

## mineralized_brain_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amineralized_brain_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这个巨大石质物种的残骸是一个大脑器官，曾经在小行星内绕[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]轨道运行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:496`

### English

Remains of a massive lithoid species, this is a cerebral organ that once orbited [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] inside of an asteroid

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:647`

## mineralized_brain_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amineralized_brain_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它将所有外部刺激视为自己思维过程的一部分，无法理解时间或运动距离等许多基本概念。这些数据存储在从矿质核心上逐渐生长出来的晶体中。它究竟是被放置在小行星内部还是自行在那里生长，我们无法确定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

It perceived all external stimuli as part of its own thought processes and was not able to track many basic concepts such as time or distance traveled. This data is stored in crystals grown over time with its mineral core. Whether it was placed inside of the asteroid or grew there independently, we cannot say for sure.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:648`

## mini_moon_dust

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amini_moon_dust&lang=en)

### 中文

谜拟月尘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

Mini Moon Dust

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:650`

## mini_moon_dust_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amini_moon_dust_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在星界裂隙另一侧发现的超致密微型星系里月球的残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

The remains of a moon from within a hyperdense and miniaturized system discovered beyond an Astral Rift

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:651`

## mini_moon_dust_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amini_moon_dust_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

被回收的月尘在我们的维度里表现得很特别。它高浓度的颗粒不断膨胀，需要随着时间的推移将标本转移到越来越大的容器中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

The dust that was retrieved behaves peculiarly inside of our dimension. Its hyper-concentrated particles continuously expand, requiring this specimen to be transferred to increasingly large containers over time.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:652`

## mining_transmitter

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amining_transmitter&lang=en)

### 中文

远古采矿发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

Ancient Mining Transmitter

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:654`

## mining_transmitter_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amining_transmitter_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]发现的一具用于标记大型贵金属矿藏的短距离发射器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

A short-range transmitter found on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] used to mark a large deposit of precious metals

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:655`

## mining_transmitter_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amining_transmitter_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管年代久远，这个发射器的信号可以被各种各样的传感器接收，而且很容易追踪。其功能的持久运转证明了其强大的工程设计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

Despite its age, this transmitter's signal can be picked up on a wide variety of sensors and is easily traceable. Its continuing functionality is a testament to its robust engineering.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:656`

## miracle_cream

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amiracle_cream&lang=en)

### 中文

“寿无限”魔法霜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

"Ageless" Miracle Cream

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:658`

## miracle_cream_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amiracle_cream_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

史无前例的革命性化妆品，让生物长久年轻化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

The first container of a revolutionary cosmetic known to provide long-lasting biological rejuvenation

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:659`

## miracle_cream_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amiracle_cream_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它有多种用途，让任何有机物中的消费者都能保持年轻的光彩。关于毁容的副作用和毫无根据的非法获取成分的谣言仍然大行其道，这些谣言无法阻止这种化妆品令人难以置信的商业奇迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

It has a multitude of uses, allowing consumers from any and every organic species to maintain their youthful glow. Baseless rumors regarding disfiguring side effects and illegally harvested ingredients still circulate, but this misinformation has not hampered the cosmetic's incredible commercial success.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:660`

## missile_lone_defender

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amissile_lone_defender&lang=en)

### 中文

孤独守望者的导弹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

Missile of the Lone Defender

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:662`

## missile_lone_defender_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amissile_lone_defender_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

来自一艘自动驱逐舰上的一颗无害化的导弹，它曾保护着一颗早已毁灭的行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

A disarmed missile from an automated destroyer defending the remains of a long-ruined planet

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:663`

## missile_lone_defender_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amissile_lone_defender_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这艘船可能比它被创造来保卫的文明多续存了数千年，守护着一颗空荡荡的行星。在被摧毁之后，它的守夜终于结束了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

The ship had likely outlasted the civilization it was created to defend by thousands of years, keeping watch over an empty world. Following its destruction, its vigil has finally come to an end.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:664`

## molten_slag

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amolten_slag&lang=en)

### 中文

熔融行星炉渣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

Molten Planetary Slag

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:666`

## molten_slag_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amolten_slag_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这个样本是从一颗特定轨道上的行星上采集的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

This sample was harvested from a planet in a peculiar orbit

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:667`

## molten_slag_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amolten_slag_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

材料分析表明[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]它被短暂的时空异常拉进了一个极端的轨道，来自其恒星附近的强烈热量融化了这颗曾经适合居住的行星的大部分地幔。这颗行星的一部分因异常而发生了化学变化，不再对温度变化作出反应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Material analysis indicates that [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] was pulled into an extreme orbit by a transient space-time anomaly, the intense heat from the proximity of its star melting much of the once-habitable planet's mantle. A portion of the planet was chemically altered by the anomaly and no longer reacts to changes in temperature.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:668`

## musings_on_infinity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amusings_on_infinity&lang=en)

### 中文

对无限的集体沉思

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

Collective Musings on Infinity

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:670`

## musings_on_infinity_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amusings_on_infinity_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

悬浮在黑洞中的工蜂所经历的遥远平静的抽象化数字表现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

An abstract digital representation of the distant calm experienced by biological drones suspended within a black hole

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:671`

## musings_on_infinity_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amusings_on_infinity_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些工蜂能够将[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]作为独立实体客观审视，同时保持与集体的连结。这种距离感赋予了他们深刻的洞见，使他们得以成为自身伟大故事的读者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

These drones were able to view [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] objectively as a separate entity while maintaining their connection to the collective. This distance offers them great insight, allowing them to exist as the readers of their own great story.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:672`

## mutated_fumongus

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amutated_fumongus&lang=en)

### 中文

变异“巨大蘑菇”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

Mutated 'Fumongus'
