Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:674`

## mutated_fumongus_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amutated_fumongus_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

增强版的“巨大蘑菇”，这些菌丝发射器可以以更高的速率收集和传输数据，从而提供更详细的监测报告

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

An enhanced version of the 'Fumongus,' these mycelial emitters can now collect and transmit data at a much higher rate, allowing for more detailed surveillance reports

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:675`

## mutated_fumongus_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amutated_fumongus_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种扩容同时带来的是单个蘑菇大小的增长，而且还形成了一个类似无线电广播天线的新集群。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

This increased capacity has caused the individual mushrooms to grow in size and create a new cluster formation that somewhat resembles a radio broadcast antenna.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:676`

## mutative_digestive_fluids

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amutative_digestive_fluids&lang=en)

### 中文

变异消化液

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

Mutative Digestive Fluids

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:678`

## mutative_digestive_fluids_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amutative_digestive_fluids_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从星界裂隙另一侧采集的一个部分机械的生物样本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

Biological samples taken from a partially mechanical being found beyond an Astral Rift

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:679`

## mutative_digestive_fluids_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amutative_digestive_fluids_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种生物拥有一种新颖的能力，可以在自己的消化系统中操纵其他生物的遗传结构。我们怀疑这一过程最初是作为对含有母星外生物质的行星的一种环境改造方法而发展起来的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

This creature possessed a novel ability to manipulate the genetic structure of other creatures from within its own digestive system. It is suspected that this process was initially developed as a method of terraforming planets containing biomass outside of their homeworld.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:680`

## namarian_colonist_standard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian_colonist_standard&lang=en)

### 中文

纳马里安殖民旗杆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

Namarian Colonist Standard

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:682`

## namarian_colonist_standard_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian_colonist_standard_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这面旗帜标志着[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]在[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]上的定居，由[EVENT_TARGET_2.GetName]慷慨相赠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

This flag marked the settlement of the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] on [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName], generously gifted to them by [EVENT_TARGET_2.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:683`

## namarian_colonist_standard_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian_colonist_standard_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

厌倦了四海为家的游牧民在那里定居下来，回归了祖先的生活方式。毫无疑问，这一决定让殖民者们他们离开了世世代代居住的船只，这一定在他们的脑海中产生了深远的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

Nomads who grew tired of their itinerant lifestyle settled there, returning to the ways of their ancestors. No doubt that this decision weighed heavily in the minds of the colonists as they left behind the ships that had housed their people for generations.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:684`

## namarian_sculpture

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian_sculpture&lang=en)

### 中文

纳马里安雕塑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

Namarian Sculpture

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:686`

## namarian_sculpture_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian_sculpture_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一座混合媒介雕像，是加入[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]的一群逐浪者游牧民为表感激而建造的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

A mixed-media statue built as a token of gratitude from a group of Pursuing Waters nomads joining the [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:687`

## namarian_sculpture_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian_sculpture_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

刚一在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]着陆，这些前游牧民就带来了他们杰出的科技专长。然而，他们不希望仅仅以修补匠和工程师的身份为人所知；这件作品证明了他们的艺术才华和穿越星系界的灵感。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

Upon landing on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], the former nomads brought their extensive technological expertise. However, they did not wish to be known solely as tinkerers and engineers; this piece is a testament to their artistry and the inspiration that comes with journeying across the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:688`

## namarian_vessel_heraldry

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian_vessel_heraldry&lang=en)

### 中文

纳马里安舰船纹章

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

Namarian Vessel Heraldry

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:690`

## namarian_vessel_heraldry_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian_vessel_heraldry_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这些冠饰发现于纳马里安游牧民所造的舰船中，代表了逐浪者舰队上的诸多派系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

Found within ships crafted by the Namarian Nomads, these crests represent the various factions within the Pursuing Waters fleet

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:691`

## namarian_vessel_heraldry_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anamarian_vessel_heraldry_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

平静的水池代表着居住舰，纳马里安人在这里休憩，种田，并进行大部分的家庭活动。浅水激流的造型标志着为工业和科学而设计的方舟舰，较老的中型舰船上配备了用于打捞和科学实验的各种器具。最后，汹涌的波峰代表着纳马里安的海军，训练有素的战士驾驶着坚固的船只，披坚执锐势不可挡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

The Calm Pools represent housing vessels, where Namarians sleep, grow food, and carry out the majority of their domestic activities. The emblem of the Shallow Rapids arkships designed for industrial and scientific use, older mid-size ships equipped with a variety of tools for everything from scavenging to scientific experimentation. Finally, the crest of the Raging Waters stands for the Namarian navy, trained combatants piloting sturdy vessels that can withstand significant firepower and dole it out just as well.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:692`

## nanite_sludge

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ananite_sludge&lang=en)

### 中文

纳米机器人泥

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

Nanite Sludge

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:694`

## nanite_sludge_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ananite_sludge_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

精心保存的由微观自复制纳米机械形成的复合物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

A carefully preserved compound formed of microscopic, self-replicating nano-machines

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:695`

## nanite_sludge_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ananite_sludge_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些纳米机器人的密度惊人，给它们所在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]增加了数十亿吨的质量。理论上讲，这些纳米机器人发生了故障，进入了一个无限制繁殖的阶段，完全吞没了它们的星球从而毁灭了之前的居民。这个特殊的标本已经完全无害化了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

The density of these nanites is astounding, adding billions of tons of mass to [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] where they were discovered. It is theorized that these nanites malfunctioned and entered a phase of unrestricted reproduction, engulfing their planet completely and thus dooming its previous inhabitants. This particular specimen has been completely neutralized.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:696`

## obsidian_obelisk_fragment

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aobsidian_obelisk_fragment&lang=en)

### 中文

黑曜石方尖碑碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

Obsidian Obelisk Fragment

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:698`

## obsidian_obelisk_fragment_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aobsidian_obelisk_fragment_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

曾伫立在星界裂隙另一侧的火山位面中心方尖碑的碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

A fragment of an obelisk that once stood at the center of a volcanic plane beyond an Astral Rift

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:699`

## obsidian_obelisk_fragment_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aobsidian_obelisk_fragment_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

在其流变的符号中编码着成千上万个个体的名字及相关的罪行。这个装置被应该是一个巨大次元监狱的主控面板。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

Encoded in its shifting symbols are the names of thousands of individuals and their associated crimes. This device is believed to have been used as the primary control panel for a massive dimensional prison.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:700`

## oiled_fabric

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoiled_fabric&lang=en)

### 中文

油绘织物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

Oiled Fabric

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:702`

## oiled_fabric_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoiled_fabric_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一幅完全由油基颜料绘制的宁静画面，上面有白雪皑皑的山脉，近处是寒带森林

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

A serene depiction of snow-capped mountains with a boreal forest shown in the foreground, composed entirely of oil-based pigments

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:703`

## oiled_fabric_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aoiled_fabric_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然这是大多数具有可抓握附肢的物种的标准艺术表达，但这位艺术家似乎特别熟练。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

While a standard expression of art in most species with prehensile appendages, this particular artist appears to have been especially skilled.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:704`

## organic_computer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorganic_computer&lang=en)

### 中文

有机计算机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

Organic Computer

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:706`

## organic_computer_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorganic_computer_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在巴奥[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]殖民地的残骸中找到的有机计算机化石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

A fossilized organic computer found within the ruins of the Baol colony on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:707`

## organic_computer_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorganic_computer_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这件装置是生物工程的一个了不起的壮举，用于教育整合进蜂巢之前的年轻树苗。恢复的文件详细介绍了创新的生态管理技术，这些技术只有通过这种特殊物种独特的生理功能才能实现。他们周围的星球不是建造出来的，而是栽培出来的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

Used to educate young saplings prior to hive integration, this device is a remarkable feat of bio-engineering. Recovered files detail innovative eco-management techniques made possible only by this particular species' unique biology. The worlds around them were not built, but cultivated.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:708`

## organic_machine_interface_sculpture

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorganic_machine_interface_sculpture&lang=en)

### 中文

有机—机械交口雕塑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Organic-Machine Interface Sculpture

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:710`

## organic_machine_interface_sculpture_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorganic_machine_interface_sculpture_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一座纪念雕像，为表彰[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesName]有机人口在[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]地磁太阳风暴期间所提供的服务而立

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

A commemorative statue in recognition of the service provided by [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetSpeciesName] bio-pops during a geomagnetic solar storm in [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:711`

## organic_machine_interface_sculpture_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorganic_machine_interface_sculpture_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

有机人口介入支持的机械仆人，代替故障的看护单元进行工作。这件作品是由有艺术倾向的有机体和专门的焊接无人机设计的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

The organic population stepped in to support their mechanical servitors, carrying out the work of malfunctioning caretaker units. This piece was designed by artistically inclined organics and specialized welding drones.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:712`

## orinthi_teeth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorinthi_teeth&lang=en)

### 中文

欧林提之牙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

Orinthi Teeth

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:714`

## orinthi_teeth_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorinthi_teeth_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

欧林提持续生长的牙齿

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

The still-growing teeth of an Orinthi

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:715`

## orinthi_teeth_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorinthi_teeth_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

一种小型类鼠生物，欧林提在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的地下钻出了一座横亘星球的巨大迷宫。他们的天然牙齿小且中空，但是一种角质真菌的伴生物种栖息在欧林提的牙齿上，形成锋利、易碎的尖牙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

Small rodent-like creatures, the Orinthi had carved world-spanning labyrinthine burrows throughout [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]. Their natural teeth are small and hollow, but a partner species of keratinous fungus inhabits each individual tooth and forms sharp, brittle fangs for the Orinthi to use.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:716`

## osseus_construction_material

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aosseus_construction_material&lang=en)

### 中文

奥修斯建材

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Osseus Construction Material

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:718`

## osseus_construction_material_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aosseus_construction_material_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上用作建筑资源的巨大骨骼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

These massive bones were used as a material resource on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:719`

## osseus_construction_material_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aosseus_construction_material_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

一种具有遗传性软组织骨化爬行动物被大规模饲养，用于获取建筑材料。分析表明，这种爬行动物有多种其他遗传缺陷，比如可能由于近亲繁殖而导致的无蹼脚。这些动物中的大多数在收获前都被人道地安乐死。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

A species of reptile with genetic soft-tissue ossification was bred extensively to be harvested for building supplies. Analysis shows that the reptile had a variety of other genetic deficiencies such as unwebbed feet potentially due to inbreeding. The majority of these creatures were humanely euthanized before harvesting took place.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:720`

## path_to_progress

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apath_to_progress&lang=en)

### 中文

进步之路

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

The Path to Progress

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:722`

## path_to_progress_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apath_to_progress_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetName]工业使命宣言的正式呈递本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

A formal presentation of the [EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetName] industrial mission statement

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:723`

## path_to_progress_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apath_to_progress_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

不惜一切代价追求增长与提升客户满意度，是[EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetAdjective]意识形态的核心信条。将行星变为辐射废土，是这场无尽进步追逐中的必要一步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

The desire for growth and improved customer satisfaction at any cost is a core tenet of [EVENT_TARGET_0.Owner.GetAdjective] ideology. Turning planets into irradiated wastelands is a necessary step in the endless pursuit of progress.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:724`

## peerless_atomic_clock

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apeerless_atomic_clock&lang=en)

### 中文

精准原子钟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:555`

### English

Peerless Atomic Clock

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:726`

## peerless_atomic_clock_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apeerless_atomic_clock_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一座极为精密的原子钟，发现于[event_target:atomic_clock_system.GetName]，出自无名工匠之手

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

A highly precise atomic clock found in [event_target:atomic_clock_system.GetName] crafted by unknown makers

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:727`

## peerless_atomic_clock_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apeerless_atomic_clock_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

当被发现时，它正倒数到42年零3天，但不知道在那之前它倒数了多久。在数到0的时候，它释放了一群带有神秘的命令的纳米机器人。尽管它的有效载荷和使命已经结束，但它的功能仍然完美。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

When discovered, it was counting down 42 years and 3 days, but there is no knowing how long it was counting before that. At the count of zero, it released a stream of stored nanites with a set of mysterious orders. It remains perfectly functional, though its payload and purpose is spent.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:728`

## perception_of_infinity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aperception_of_infinity&lang=en)

### 中文

无限知觉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

Perception of Infinity

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:730`

## perception_of_infinity_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aperception_of_infinity_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

悬浮在黑洞中的工蜂所遭受的暂时痛苦的抽象数字表现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

An abstract digital representation of the temporal anguish suffered by drones suspended within a black hole

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:731`

## perception_of_infinity_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aperception_of_infinity_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些工蜂长期处于一种似曾相识的状态；不安，渴望着一种早已被遗忘的感觉。这种不祥的预感至今仍隐约萦绕于[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetAdjective]思维之网中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

These drones existed in a perennial state of deja-vu; uneasy, yearning for a feeling that has been long-forgotten. This foreboding sensation is still loosely detected across the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetAdjective] mindweave.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:732`

## perfectly_faceted_carbon

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aperfectly_faceted_carbon&lang=en)

### 中文

完美切面碳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

Perfectly Faceted Carbon

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:734`

## perfectly_faceted_carbon_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aperfectly_faceted_carbon_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然晶体形式的碳在我们的星系中并不罕见，但特别值得注意的是这个标本完全没有任何内部或外部的包裹体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:562`

### English

While carbon in its crystalline form is not such a rare find in our galaxy, this specimen is especially notable for its complete lack of any internal or external inclusions

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:735`

## perfectly_faceted_carbon_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aperfectly_faceted_carbon_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

不管是谁之前拥有这件物品想必都花了很多的时间雕刻它，才能突出这些显著的特征。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

Whoever was previously in possession of this item took the time to carve it in such a way as to highlight these remarkable features.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:736`

## pet_collar

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apet_collar&lang=en)

### 中文

镶嵌宝石的宠物项圈

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

Bejeweled Pet Collar

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:738`

## pet_collar_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apet_collar_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种用来对驯养的小型哺乳动物表示所有权的项链

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

A necklace used to designate ownership over a small domesticated mammal

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:739`

## pet_collar_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apet_collar_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这件项圈最初由[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]使者[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]所使用，以防其爱宠走失。如今它已被更可靠的嵌入式微芯片取代。这只喜怒无常的小生物常常扰乱主人的工作站，每当[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]用[EVENT_TARGET_0.Species.GetHandName]抚摸它的头时，它都会发出满足的鸣颤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

This was initially used by [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName] envoy [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] in the event that their valued companion were to get lost. It has since been replaced with a more reliable embedded microchip. The temperamental creature could often be found disrupting their owner's workstation, and would release a satisfied tonal flutter whenever [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] touched its head with their [EVENT_TARGET_0.Species.GetHandName].

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:740`

## perikan_tapico_jewel

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aperikan_tapico_jewel&lang=en)

### 中文

佩里坎塔皮科珠宝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

Perikan Tapico Jewel

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:742`

## perikan_tapico_jewel_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aperikan_tapico_jewel_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

佩里坎联盟是一个能工巧匠社会，以制造极端奢华的物品闻名

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

The Perikan Union was a society of master craftsmen known for creating exquisite luxury goods

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:743`

## perikan_tapico_jewel_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aperikan_tapico_jewel_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗宝石是传奇珠宝商贝迪尔·塔皮科的杰作。塔皮科仅用简单的凿子就雕刻了这颗巨大的宝石，并使用炼金术液化了它的中心。漂浮在宝石内部的晶体是通过精密激光形成的，通过对矿物分子进行过加热，形成更小、更暗的宝石，同时保持外部完整。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:569`

### English

This gem was the masterpiece of the legendary jeweler known as Bedeers Tapico. Tapico faceted this enormous gem with a simple chisel over a period of years and used alchemical techniques to liquify its center. The crystals floating within the jewel were formed with precision lasers, superheating the mineral molecules to reform smaller, darker gems while leaving the exterior intact.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:744`

## piece_of_invocator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apiece_of_invocator&lang=en)

### 中文

召唤器碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:570`

### English

Piece of the Invocator

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:746`

## piece_of_invocator_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apiece_of_invocator_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

曾被称为“风暴召唤器”的一台装置被毁坏后的一片碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

A fragment of the destroyed device once known as the "Tempest Invocator"

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:747`

## piece_of_invocator_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apiece_of_invocator_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管只是一个破碎的碎片，它依然充满了恶毒的灵能。它被保存在一个屏蔽的环境中，以便安全观察。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:572`

### English

Despite being only a shattered piece, it pulses with malevolent psionic energy.  It is contained here in a shielded environment to allow for safe observation.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:748`

## pilgrimage_last_days

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apilgrimage_last_days&lang=en)

### 中文

末日朝圣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

The Pilgrimage of Last Days

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:750`

## pilgrimage_last_days_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apilgrimage_last_days_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一份描述了在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上被称为“揭纱秘仪”的神圣仪式的宗教文献

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

A religious document describing a holy ritual known as "The Rite of Lifted Veil" on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:751`

## pilgrimage_last_days_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apilgrimage_last_days_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

指示概述了一系列的要由一群人站在精心组织的多级队形中齐声重唱的圣歌。从理论上讲，这一过程能够在这种特殊的物种中建立一种新颖而有效的催眠状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:575`

### English

Instructions outline a series of chantings to be repeated in unison by a crowd standing in carefully organized hierarchical formations. In theory, this procedure is capable of establishing a novel and effective state of hypnosis in this particular species.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:752`

## planet_crystalose

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_crystalose&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]晶糖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Crystalose

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:754`

## planet_crystalose_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_crystalose_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这些蔗糖晶体能够通过吸收太阳辐射和某些小行星中存在的特定矿物质而繁殖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

These sucrose crystals are able to multiply by absorbing solar radiation and specific minerals present within certain asteroids

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:755`

## planet_crystalose_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_crystalose_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

由于非常易碎，它们相对容易培育和加工。其工业应用范围包括食品、特殊聚合物以及药物开发。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:578`

### English

Due to their extremely brittle nature, they are relatively easy to cultivate and process. Their industrial applications range from foodstuffs to the development of specialized polymers and medicine.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:756`

## planet_pushing_thruster

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_pushing_thruster&lang=en)

### 中文

行星发动机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:579`

### English

Planet-Pusher

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:758`

## planet_pushing_thruster_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_pushing_thruster_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这些巨大的推进装置串联在一起，理论上能够影响小型行星的轨道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:580`

### English

These massive propulsion devices, when connected in series, are theoretically capable of influencing a small planet's orbit

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:759`

## planet_pushing_thruster_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_pushing_thruster_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些推力阵列的安装系统被设计成在不影响行星自转的情况下推动行星。这种发动机技术的主要后勤问题是维持其使用所需的燃料数量过多，需要整个文明的协调才能维持它的运作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

The intended mounting system for these thrusting arrays is designed to push the planet without affecting its rotation. The main logistical concern with this type of engine technology would be the inordinate quantity of fuel required to sustain its use. It would take the coordination of an entire civilization to maintain its operation.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:760`

## planet_super_roots

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_super_roots&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]超级根茎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Super Roots

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:762`

## planet_super_roots_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_super_roots_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的森林中发现，这些天然的果肉根营养价值很高

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

Found in the forests of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], these naturally pulpy roots have been found to be highly nutritious

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:763`

## planet_super_roots_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_super_roots_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

通过精心的管理，只收获它们庞大网络中的一部分，就可以让寄主植物在支持殖民地发展的同时生存下来。我们已经开始试验一种简单的基因修饰，可以将这些根嫁接到现有的作物上，以提高产量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

With careful management, harvesting only a portion of their extensive networks allow the host plants to survive while supporting developing colonies. We have already begun experimenting with a simple genetic modification that will allow the grafting if these roots into existing crops for an extra yield to the harvests.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:764`

## planet_vaccine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_vaccine&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]疫苗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Vaccine

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:766`

## planet_vaccine_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_vaccine_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

为肆虐[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的突变疾病研发的特效药

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

The miracle cure developed for the mutating disease that plagued [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:767`

## planet_vaccine_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_vaccine_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这个处方是通过使用人工智能预测算法来绘制出疾病的所有可能变体，并确定整体的基本弱点而开发的。它的发明是医学科学领域的一个里程碑式的进步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:587`

### English

This formula was developed by using AI predictive algorithms to map out all of the possible variants of the disease and identify a fundamental weakness common to the whole. Its creation was a landmark advancement in the field of medical science.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:768`

## planetary_sculpture

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanetary_sculpture&lang=en)

### 中文

行星雕刻

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:588`

### English

Planetary Sculpture

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:770`

## planetary_sculpture_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanetary_sculpture_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个行星尺度的雕塑的例子，其原材料为[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的表面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:589`

### English

An example of sculpture on a planetary scale using the surface of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] as raw material

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:771`

## planetary_sculpture_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanetary_sculpture_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

配备强大激光的无人机被用来在整个大陆、完美的球形山脉和难以置信的复杂洞穴系统上雕刻精确的线条。上传到中央控制卫星上的设计示意图说明了为该项目开发的大规模后勤规划和艺术考量。不幸的是，它在真正完成之前就被遗弃了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:590`

### English

Drones armed with powerful lasers were used to carve precise lines through entire continents, perfectly spherical mountains, and impossibly intricate cavern systems. The design schematics uploaded on their central control satellite illustrate an extensive level of logistical planning and artistic consideration developed for this project. Unfortunately, it was abandoned before it was ever truly completed.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:772`

## primitive_mega_electromagnet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprimitive_mega_electromagnet&lang=en)

### 中文

原始巨型电磁铁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:591`

### English

Primitive Mega-Electromagnet

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:774`

## primitive_mega_electromagnet_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprimitive_mega_electromagnet_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一具强方向性电磁铁的一部分

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:592`

### English

A portion of a powerful directional electromagnet

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:775`

## primitive_mega_electromagnet_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprimitive_mega_electromagnet_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]星球上的古代居民愚蠢地用这个装置拉近了他们星球的卫星，以便更容易地收获珍贵的金属矿石。这种方法有效到充满金属的卫星直接撞向行星表面，并在由此产生的影响中灭绝了所有的居民。这个标本只是从行星尺度机器中取回的一小部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:593`

### English

The ancient inhabitants of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] foolishly used this device to bring their planet's moon closer in order to more easily harvest its valuable metal ores. This approach was too effective, bringing the metal-filled moon crashing into the planet's surface and exterminating every inhabitant in the resulting impact. This specimen is only a small fraction of what could be recovered from the planet-spanning machine.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:776`

## programmable_puzzle_cube

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogrammable_puzzle_cube&lang=en)

### 中文

可编程谜盒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:594`

### English

Programmable Puzzle Cube

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:778`

## programmable_puzzle_cube_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogrammable_puzzle_cube_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种高度工程设计的设备，能够记录复杂的图案，创造抽象的谜题

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:595`

### English

A highly engineered device capable of registering complicated patterns to create abstract puzzles

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:779`

## programmable_puzzle_cube_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprogrammable_puzzle_cube_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

最初的碎片是在一座巨大的人造结构中发现的，作为对探险家智力的测试。这个立方体已经被复制供消费者使用，允许个体为彼此编写自己的拼图，或者只是用来保护自己的财物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:596`

### English

The original piece was found within a vast artificial construct as a test of an explorer's intellect. This cube has since been replicated for consumer use, allowing individuals to program their own puzzles for one another or simply safeguard their belongings.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:780`

## psionic_entity_residue

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apsionic_entity_residue&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能实体残留

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:597`

### English

Psionic Entity Residue

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:782`

## psionic_entity_residue_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apsionic_entity_residue_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]内出现的纯灵能实体的残余

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:598`

### English

All that remains of an entity of pure psionic energy found within [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:783`

## psionic_entity_residue_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apsionic_entity_residue_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它是在一个时空裂隙中被发现的，这个时空裂缝让来自整个星系的能量汇聚于此。他是一种集中的恶意形式，可能被故意从超空间航道网络中隔离了亿万年，直到最近才被发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:599`

### English

It was discovered emerging from a temporal rift that allowed energy from across the galaxy to pool in a single location. Malevolence in concentrated form, it may have been purposely isolated from the hyperlane network for eons until its recent discovery.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:784`

## radiated_remains

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aradiated_remains&lang=en)

### 中文

受辐射残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:600`

### English

Radiated Remains

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:786`

## radiated_remains_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aradiated_remains_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一种使用强力放射性诱变剂进行仪式的物种的遗骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:601`

### English

The remains of a species that carried out ritualistic ceremonies involving powerful radioactive mutagens

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:787`

## radiated_remains_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aradiated_remains_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

每个突变都是一个神迹，被解读为个体命运的表现。随着时间的推移，这一过程慢慢破坏了物种的遗传结构，削弱了它们的免疫系统，使种群极易受到各种疾病的侵害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:602`

### English

Each mutation was a sacred event, interpreted as a manifestation of the individual's destiny. Over time, this procedure destabilized the genetic structure of the species and weakened their immune system, making the population extremely vulnerable to disease.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:788`

## rainaissance

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arainaissance&lang=en)

### 中文

紫雨复兴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:604`

### English

Purple Renaissance

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:791`

## rainaissance_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arainaissance_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的天气模式中发现的独特紫雨的艺术图像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:605`

### English

Artistic images taken of the uniquely purple rain found present in the weather patterns of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:792`

## rainaissance_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arainaissance_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

据说，观察这种激发想象的意象对缓解悲伤和痛苦的年头很有效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:606`

### English

It is said that observing this evocative imagery is effective in relieving thoughts of sorrow and pain.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:793`

## reactive_rock

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areactive_rock&lang=en)

### 中文

反应石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:607`

### English

Reactive Rock

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:795`

## reactive_rock_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areactive_rock_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这块不稳定的矿物在目前状况下没有贸易或工业的价值

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:608`

### English

This unstable mineral has no commercial or industrial use in its current state

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:796`

## reactive_rock_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areactive_rock_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

然而，由于其独特的反应性质，它有可能在特定条件下引发完全的交代变化。这个标本被包裹在一个含铅的观察窗口中，以限制放射性和电轰击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:609`

### English

However, due to its uniquely reactive properties, it is possible to trigger a complete metasomatic change under specific conditions. This specimen is encased in a lead-infused viewing window that limits radioactive and electrical bombardment.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:797`

## reparation_invoice

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areparation_invoice&lang=en)

### 中文

赔款发票

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:610`

### English

Reparation Invoice

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:799`

## reparation_invoice_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areparation_invoice_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_MSI_country$发来的战争赔款发票

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:611`

### English

An invoice for war reparations sent by $NAME_MSI_country$

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:800`

## reparation_invoice_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areparation_invoice_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

由星际信贷管理公司起草的这份清单，详列了[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]摆脱所谓“仁善公司”契约奴役时所损失的一切军事资产。这家寰宇企业期望这些损失获得赔付，甚至声称触碰[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetAdjective]领空内的被毁舰船也将构成进一步违约。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:612`

### English

Drafted by the Interstellar Credit Management Company, this list details every military asset lost when [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] broke free of their indentured servitude to the so-called "Benevolent Corporation". The MegaCorp expects to be reimbursed for these damages, and even claims that touching the destroyed ships in [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetAdjective] space would be a further breach of contract.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:801`

## repurposed_cleaning_drone

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arepurposed_cleaning_drone&lang=en)

### 中文

改造清洁无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:613`

### English

Repurposed Cleaning Drone

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:803`

## repurposed_cleaning_drone_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arepurposed_cleaning_drone_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这种无人机的原是星球卫生协议的一部分，无意中将其创造者的整颗母星变得完全无菌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:614`

### English

This drone design was part of a planet-wide sanitation protocol that inadvertently rendered its creators' homeworld completely sterile

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:804`

## repurposed_cleaning_drone_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arepurposed_cleaning_drone_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它用途极广，可凭借多向推进器系统完成复杂的空中机动。这台机器经逆向工程改造后，被用于识别和分类异星动植物，随后又被大量生产，以协助整个[EVENT_TARGET_0.explorer.GetName]的殖民工作。作为预防措施，其所有清洁子例程都已被抹除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:615`

### English

It is highly versatile and can perform complex aerial maneuvers with its system of multi-directional thrusters. This machine was reverse-engineered and repurposed to identify and categorize alien flora and fauna, and subsequently has been mass-produced to assist with colonization efforts across the [EVENT_TARGET_0.explorer.GetName]. As a precaution, all of its cleansing subroutines have been erased.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:805`

## resonant_diffusion_crystal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresonant_diffusion_crystal&lang=en)

### 中文

共振扩散晶体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:616`

### English

Resonant Diffusion Crystal

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:807`

## resonant_diffusion_crystal_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresonant_diffusion_crystal_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这种晶体能够吸收任何频率的太阳辐射并发生共振，在这个过程中产生万花筒一般的光效

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:617`

### English

This crystal is able to absorb and resonate any frequency of solar radiation, creating a kaleidoscopic display in the process

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:808`

## resonant_diffusion_crystal_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresonant_diffusion_crystal_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这不仅是视觉上的奇迹，而且可以帮助开发基于能源的武器或工具。这颗宝石也引起了星系珠宝行业的极大兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:618`

### English

Not only is this a visual marvel, but it could aid in the development of energy-based weapons or tools. This gem has also sparked great interest within the galactic jewelry industry.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:809`

## retarax_hunting_trophies

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aretarax_hunting_trophies&lang=en)

### 中文

雷塔拉克斯狩猎战利品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:619`

### English

Retarax Hunting Trophies

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:811`

## retarax_hunting_trophies_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aretarax_hunting_trophies_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

狩猎各种各样智慧物种所获得的战利品收藏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:620`

### English

A collection of hunting trophies harvested from a wide variety of sapient species

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:812`

## retarax_hunting_trophies_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aretarax_hunting_trophies_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些都是从马克维尔之收集来的，在那里，雷塔拉克斯人会猎杀从他们的许多附庸国虏来的公民取乐。所有的遗骸都保存完好，每个都有一个小匾，详细说明了战利品的信息：物种、名字、年龄和以前的职业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:621`

### English

These were collected from the Valley of Mak'vaehl, where the Retarax would hunt citizens pulled from their many vassal states for sport. All remains have been preserved in immaculate condition, each with a small plaque detailing the trophy's features: species, name, age, and former profession.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:813`

## robe_of_the_golden_broker

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arobe_of_the_golden_broker&lang=en)

### 中文

黄金掮客的长袍

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:622`

### English

Robe of the Golden Broker

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:815`

## robe_of_the_golden_broker_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arobe_of_the_golden_broker_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这是5000多年前一位很有影响力的商人的奢华服装

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:623`

### English

The luxurious attire of an influential merchant from over 5000 years ago

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:816`

## robe_of_the_golden_broker_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arobe_of_the_golden_broker_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这位“黄金掮客”带领绰尔的罗盘贸易站进入了一个无与伦比的繁荣时代。他戴着金色面具，穿着这身舒适奢华的长袍，谈着纵横星系的贸易协定。不幸的是，黄金掮客的统治在他的纯金星际飞船在一次劫机行动中毁于一旦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:624`

### English

The "Golden Broker" led the Chor's Compass merchant station into an era of unrivaled prosperity. Anonymous under a golden mask, they wore this comfortable and luxurious robe while negotiating galaxy-spanning trade agreements. The Golden Broker's reign was sadly cut short after their pure-gold starship was destroyed in a hijacking attempt gone awry.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:817`

## rock_jellies

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arock_jellies&lang=en)

### 中文

岩石水母

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

Rock Jellies

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:819`

## rock_jellies_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arock_jellies_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

大型胶状无脊椎动物的冷冻化石残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

Cryo-fossilized remains of large gelatinous invertebrates

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:820`

## rock_jellies_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arock_jellies_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

从表面上看，这些钙化的遗骸类似于岩层，但事实上，这些本土动物曾经是[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]星球上的主要生命形式。在该行星最近的一次冰河时代之后，这个物种几乎就灭绝了。它们的残骸在腐烂的过程中会逐渐凝固，最终形成巨大的、漂浮的伪岛屿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

On a superficial level, these calcified remains resemble rock formations, but in fact this native fauna were once the dominant lifeform on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]. Following the planet's most recent ice age, the species was rendered nearly extinct. Their remains would solidify as they decayed, eventually forming massive, floating pseudo-islands.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:821`

## rudari_death_laser_prototype

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arudari_death_laser_prototype&lang=en)

### 中文

鲁达里死亡激光原型机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

Rudari Death Laser Prototype

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:823`

## rudari_death_laser_prototype_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arudari_death_laser_prototype_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一把由早已消亡的鲁达里仓促拼装的武器化激光器，发现于[EVENT_TARGET_0.solar_system.GetName]的一座空间站上

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:629`

### English

A weaponized laser hastily assembled by the long-dead Rudari, found on a station in [EVENT_TARGET_0.solar_system.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:824`

## rudari_death_laser_prototype_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arudari_death_laser_prototype_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这个匆忙拼凑起来的大规模杀伤性武器是鲁达里种族对抗波格-莫里的最后一招。最终，激光在启动后就自毁了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:630`

### English

This hacked-together weapon of mass destruction was created as the final gambit for the Rudari species in their war against the Pogg-Mory. In the end, the laser simply self-destructed upon activation.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:825`

## saariah_guilt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asaariah_guilt&lang=en)

### 中文

萨阿里亚之愧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:631`

### English

Saariah's Guilt

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:827`

## saariah_guilt_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asaariah_guilt_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

萨阿里亚的记忆记录，他是眠梦仪潜意识系统的首席开发者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

The recorded memories of Saariah, the lead developer of the Memorex subconscious system

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:828`

## saariah_guilt_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asaariah_guilt_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

萨阿里亚的任务是消除集体记忆，以适应星球的领导层，但一次故障最终清除了人们的思想。由于无法恢复他们的记忆，萨阿里亚拆除了栖息地的生命维持设备，任其自行消亡。这些设备中的每一个都具有令人难以置信的原始计算能力，它们本身就是一项成就。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:633`

### English

Saariah was tasked with removing collective memories to suit the planet's leadership, but a malfunction eventually wiped the population's minds clean. Unable to recover their memories, Saariah removed the habitat's life support and left it to perish. Each of these devices are incredibly dense with raw computational power and are an achievement unto themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:829`

## scent_based_information_device

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascent_based_information_device&lang=en)

### 中文

嗅盘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:634`

### English

Olfactodrive

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:831`

## scent_based_information_device_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascent_based_information_device_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个以气味为媒介编码信息的存储设备

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:635`

### English

A cache of information encoded in the medium of scent

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:832`

## scent_based_information_device_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascent_based_information_device_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

里面的数据要么是有趣的叙述的集合，要么是对各种有机排泄物的细致记录。复制这种嗅觉数据存储技术是可行的，但除了新奇之外，它没有任何实用价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:636`

### English

The data within is either a collection of interesting narratives, or the meticulous recordings of various organic excretions in high detail. Replicating this olfactory data storage technique is feasible, but it offers zero practical utility outside of novelty alone.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:833`

## scientific_treatise_of_telisa

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascientific_treatise_of_telisa&lang=en)

### 中文

特丽莎科学专论

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:637`

### English

Scientific Treatise of Telisa

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:835`

## scientific_treatise_of_telisa_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascientific_treatise_of_telisa_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

说书人特丽莎的哲学思考

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:638`

### English

The philosophical musings of Telisa, Teller of Tales

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:836`

## scientific_treatise_of_telisa_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ascientific_treatise_of_telisa_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

她探讨了各种修辞主题以及科学方法的核心原则。这些文章挑战了客观真理的概念，鼓励了批判性思维。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:639`

### English

She explores a variety of rhetorical topics as well as the core principles of the scientific method. Inside these texts, the very concept of objective truth is challenged and critical thinking is encouraged.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:837`

## secrets_black_crown

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asecrets_black_crown&lang=en)

### 中文

黑王冠秘密

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

### English

Secrets of the Black Crown

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:839`

## secrets_black_crown_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asecrets_black_crown_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

被称为黑王冠的星系星门的详细示意图

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

A detailed schematic of the intergalactic gateway known as the Black Crown

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:840`

## secrets_black_crown_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asecrets_black_crown_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这份数据是星门神秘创造者送出的最后礼物，旨在保存他们不可思议的科技。这份托付交给了[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]，其星门技术的力量与威严至今无可匹敌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

This data was transmitted as a final gift from the gateway's enigmatic creators as a way to preserve their incredible technology. Entrusted to the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], the power and majesty of their gateway technology is unmatched to this day.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:841`

## sembelami_alms_basket

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asembelami_alms_basket&lang=en)

### 中文

森贝拉米受施碗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

Sembelami Alms Basket

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:843`

## sembelami_alms_basket_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asembelami_alms_basket_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

森贝拉米游牧民的成员端来接受施舍的碗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:644`

### English

A bowl carried by members of the Sembelami Nomads to accept charity

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:844`

## sembelami_alms_basket_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asembelami_alms_basket_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些苦行僧在他们的轨道寺庙被一个不断扩张的企业帝国摧毁后，开始进行一种“道德”的海盗行为。他们从富有的寰宇企业劫富济贫，资助难民哨站。他们则完全依靠难民回馈给他们的捐款生存下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:645`

### English

These ascetic travelers committed a form of 'ethical' piracy after their orbital temple was destroyed by an expanding corporate empire. They stole from wealthy mega-corporations and used their earnings to fund refugee outposts. They survived entirely off of the donations received from the refugees they supported.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:845`

## shallarian_data_core

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashallarian_data_core&lang=en)

### 中文

和乐族数据核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

Shallarian Data Core

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:847`

## shallarian_data_core_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashallarian_data_core_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在和乐族乌托邦的废墟中找到的环境改造数据库

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:647`

### English

A terraforming database found amongst the ruins of the Shallarian utopia

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:848`

## shallarian_data_core_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashallarian_data_core_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

在遭[event_target:fanatic_xenophobe_fe.GetSpeciesName]灭族之前，和乐族曾建造了一套利用六颗等距卫星的气候管理系统。在他们不幸陨落之前，和乐族还计划环境改造不宜居的行星，以安置和供养难民人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

Exterminated by the [event_target:fanatic_xenophobe_fe.GetSpeciesName], the Shallarians created a climate management system using six equidistant moons. Before their untimely demise, the Shallarians had planned to terraform uninhabited planets in order to house and support refugee populations.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:849`

## shipyard_schematics

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashipyard_schematics&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]船坞设计

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:649`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Shipyard Schematics

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:851`

## shipyard_schematics_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashipyard_schematics_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一份由[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]的[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]所控制的自动化船坞的蓝图

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:650`

### English

A blueprint of an automated shipyard controlled by the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] of [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:852`

## shipyard_schematics_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ashipyard_schematics_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

在这座被其创造者遗弃的外星船坞被发现后，该组织的总工程师对其进行了翻新，以便为他们误入歧途的生产军用舰队。这座造船坞里完成的最后一个记录在案的项目是一艘装备了先进战斗载荷的令人印象深刻的旗舰。这座建筑群能如此容易地就被重新启用，也证明了其初始设计师的聪明才智。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:651`

### English

Having found this shipyard abandoned by its alien creators, the organization's chief engineer refurbished it to produce military fleets for their misguided cause. The last recorded project to be completed at this shipyard was an impressive flagship vessel equipped with an advanced combat loadout. For this complex to be so easily repurposed is evidence of the genius of its original designers.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:853`

## sky_dragon

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asky_dragon&lang=en)

### 中文

天龙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:652`

### English

The Sky Dragon

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:855`

## sky_dragon_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asky_dragon_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这座庆祝雕像受托建造，以彰显[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]与长期环绕[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetHomeWorldName]运行的“天龙”之间的协作

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

This celebratory statue was commissioned to represent the collaboration between [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] and the "Sky Dragon" that has long orbited [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetHomeWorldName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:856`

## sky_dragon_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asky_dragon_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

认定[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetAdjective]社会配得上之后，这只生物宣誓以其自身及后代提供军事支援，以换取让幼崽在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetAdjective]领空中成长的许可。据说这些太空生物会等待千年寻找适合哺育幼崽的宿主，且只会选择一颗行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:654`

### English

Having deemed the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetAdjective] society worthy, the creature pledged itself and its offspring in military support in exchange for the opportunity to raise its young in [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetAdjective] space. It is said these spaceborne creatures can wait millennia to find a suitable host for their young, and only ever choose one planet.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:857`

## smeegibb_statue

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asmeegibb_statue&lang=en)

### 中文

斯密吉卜雕塑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:655`

### English

Statue of Smeegibb

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:859`

## smeegibb_statue_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asmeegibb_statue_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一尊斯密吉卜的雕塑，这是奈斯船长的蜂巢思维伙伴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:656`

### English

A statue of Smeegibb, the hive-minded companions of Captain Ness

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:860`

## smeegibb_statue_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asmeegibb_statue_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

斯密吉卜是忠诚的仆人，在这位传奇海盗的职业生涯后期担任着全部船员和跳帮战队的职位。这座仅存的雕像是在黑针军团袭击他们的小定居点后从残留的遗迹里修复出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:657`

### English

Smeegibb was a loyal servant, functioning as the legendary pirate's entire crew and boarding party in the latter stages of her career. This reconstructed statue was all that remained after the Black Needle's attack on their small settlement.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:861`

## sniran_spikes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asniran_spikes&lang=en)

### 中文

萨尼兰尖刺

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:658`

### English

Sniran Spikes

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:863`

## sniran_spikes_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asniran_spikes_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在一种被称为萨尼兰的生物背上取下的长刺

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Tall spikes found on the back of a creature known as a Sniran

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:864`

## sniran_spikes_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asniran_spikes_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

萨尼兰是一种大型伏击掠食者，它们藏身在原生沙丘下，当发现表面有动静时，就会伸出起致命的尖刺。尽管体型庞大，这些生物的身体却非常脆弱。它们细长的网状鳞片使它们能够在沙子下无声地滑行，但也很容易被刺穿。据推测，这也有助于它们的生殖过程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

Snirans are large ambush predators, lying beneath their native sand dunes and raising deadly spikes whenever they detect movement on the surface above. Despite their large size, the bodies of these creatures are surprisingly fragile. Their thin, reticulated scales allow them to glide silently under the sand, but can be pierced quite easily. It is speculated that this aids in their reproductive processes.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:865`

## sonic_reflective_prism

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asonic_reflective_prism&lang=en)

### 中文

声学反射棱镜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

Sonically Reflective Prism

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:867`

## sonic_reflective_prism_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asonic_reflective_prism_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个刻有特殊科学符号的多棱柱棱镜，这个物体只反射某一频率以下的能量波

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

A polygonal prism engraved with peculiar scientific notation, this object only reflects energy waves below a certain frequency

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:868`

## sonic_reflective_prism_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asonic_reflective_prism_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

也许起初是某个文明的测量仪器，它也能用于美学、音乐表演和声学。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

Perhaps originally a measuring device of some kind, it also can be utilized for aesthetic purposes in musical performance and acoustics.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:869`

## spaceborne_hunter_handbook

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspaceborne_hunter_handbook&lang=en)

### 中文

狩猎艺术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

Art of the Hunt

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:871`

## spaceborne_hunter_handbook_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspaceborne_hunter_handbook_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_PrimalHunter$撰写的关于太空兽群的现场指导

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

A field guide to spaceborne fauna written by $NAME_PrimalHunter$

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:872`

## spaceborne_hunter_handbook_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspaceborne_hunter_handbook_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

该书通过谚语和原始哲学来解释“狩猎的艺术”。其中详细描述太空生物的进食和迁徙模式，并介绍了如何绕过每种生物的天然防御，揭示了许多可以利用的弱点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

It explains the "Art of the Hunt" by way of proverbs and primal philosophies. Detailed insights into the feeding and migration patterns of spaceborne creatures are provided within.
