The text offers practical instructions on how to bypass each creature's natural defenses, revealing multiple weak points to be exploited.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:873`

## spaceborne_xeno_encyclopedia

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspaceborne_xeno_encyclopedia&lang=en)

### 中文

异种百科全书

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

Encyclopedia Xenologica

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:875`

## spaceborne_xeno_encyclopedia_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspaceborne_xeno_encyclopedia_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_PrimalRancher$撰写的异种学论文集

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:668`

### English

A collection of xenological theses written by $NAME_PrimalRancher$

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:876`

## spaceborne_xeno_encyclopedia_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspaceborne_xeno_encyclopedia_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这本研究巨著为太空生命的行为模式提供了深入的见解；包括交配仪式、迁徙路线、物种间交流等等。还有一节关于使用独特的物理特征和音调来识别单个标本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:669`

### English

This body of research offers in-depth insight into the behavioral patterns of spaceborne life; mating rituals, migratory routes, interspecies communication and more. There is also a section on identifying individual specimens using unique physical features and tonal recognition.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:877`

## spiraling_attraction

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspiraling_attraction&lang=en)

### 中文

螺旋吸引装置

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:670`

### English

Spiraling Attraction

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:879`

## spiraling_attraction_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspiraling_attraction_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种大型的复杂装置，理论上认为是为娱乐而设计的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:671`

### English

A large and complex contraption theorized to be designed for amusement

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:880`

## spiraling_attraction_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspiraling_attraction_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这个缠绕的装置被废弃在一个有许多不同颜色和材料的类似结构的区域。据说，在特定的重力水平下沿着它的螺旋路径下降能令人感到兴奋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

This winding installation was abandoned in an area housing many similar structures of various colors and material. Descending along its coiled path within a specific level of gravity is said to be thrilling.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:881`

## spirit_of_the_unshackled

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspirit_of_the_unshackled&lang=en)

挣脱桎梏之魂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

Spirit of the Unshackled

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:883`

## spirit_of_the_unshackled_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspirit_of_the_unshackled_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

为纪念[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的成立而制作的雕塑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:674`

### English

A sculpture made in commemoration of the formation of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:884`

## spirit_of_the_unshackled_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspirit_of_the_unshackled_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

挣脱了奴隶制的枷锁，苦难的过去将他们牢牢团结在一起，去创造一个更光明的未来。雕像上的每一个链环都刻着创始成员的名字。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:675`

### English

Broken free from the chains of slavery, their shared and troubled past unites them in creating a more promising future. Each link in the sculpture's chain has the name of a founding member engraved upon it.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:885`

## star_mall_map

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astar_mall_map&lang=en)

### 中文

星达广场免费地图

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:676`

### English

Star Mall Complimentary Map

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:887`

## star_mall_map_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astar_mall_map_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在过去会提供给每个游客的一张地图，这里曾被称为“星达广场”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:677`

### English

A map once provided to each visitor to a location known once known as "Star Mall"

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:888`

## star_mall_map_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astar_mall_map_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

作为星际消费商业的旧址，星达广场建有大量的便利设施，包括明亮的水疗中心，完美的营养庭院。这张地图提供了无数商家的信息，包括科瑞克运动商品、麦克斯金丝雀连锁店、建造鹦鹉工作室、种子蛋糕工坊等等。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

The former home of intergalactic consumer commerce, the Star Mall boasted a huge number of amenities from illuminating spas to succulent nutrition courts. This map provides information on a vast number of outlets including Koriik's Sporting Goods, T. Canary. Maxx, Build-A-Budgerigar, and Seedcake Factory.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:889`

## starmetal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astarmetal&lang=en)

### 中文

星铁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

Starmetal

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:891`

## starmetal_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astarmetal_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种在超新星爆发后短时间内收集到的高密度重金属

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

A condensed mass of heavy metals collected shortly after a supernova explosion

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:892`

## starmetal_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astarmetal_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

该样品究竟是如何采集的对我们来说是一个谜，但它在研究恒星演化和组成方面具有显著的科学价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

How this recovery was done is a mystery to us, but its scientific value in the studying of stellar evolution and composition are remarkable.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:893`

## striated_sand

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astriated_sand&lang=en)

### 中文

横纹沙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

Striated Sand

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:895`

## striated_sand_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astriated_sand_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一罐自组装沙子

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

A jar of self-sorting sand

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:896`

## striated_sand_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astriated_sand_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些微小的硅酸盐颗粒似乎展现出了一定程度的意识，会将相似的矿物质在看似随机的水平面上相互组合。尽管有这种能力，沙子却不具备任何其他生命的基本特征。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

These tiny silicate particles seem to exhibit a low level of sentience, with similar minerals grouping with one another across random horizontal planes. Despite this ability, the sand does not possess any of the other primary characteristics of life.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:897`

## sullebaster_control_column

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asullebaster_control_column&lang=en)

### 中文

舒勒巴斯特控制杆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:685`

### English

Sullebaster Control Column

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:899`

## sullebaster_control_column_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asullebaster_control_column_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

发生传奇事故的亚光速飞船的方向杠杆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

The directional lever from a sublight spacecraft involved in a legendary accident

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:900`

## sullebaster_control_column_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asullebaster_control_column_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

舒勒巴斯特号客船在奥德良王国的两个殖民地之间运载平民时，恒星耀斑爆发导致了飞船引擎的严重故障，这通常会导致灾难性后果。飞行员克服重重困难，手动控制匹配速度，成功地将飞船安全降落在一颗经过的小行星上。舒勒巴斯特号随后退役，并在公共场所进行展示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

The Sullebaster passenger ship was transporting civilians between two colonies of the Odelian Kingdom when a solar flare caused a critical malfunction in the vessel's engines that would usually guarantee disaster. Against all odds, the pilot managed to safely land the vessel on a passing asteroid by matching velocities using manual control. The Sullebaster was subsequently decommissioned and mounted for public display.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:901`

## symbiotic_fruit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asymbiotic_fruit&lang=en)

### 中文

共生菌果

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

Symbiotic Fruit

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:903`

## symbiotic_fruit_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asymbiotic_fruit_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

来自其他维度的一丛智慧真菌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

A cluster of intelligent fungus from another dimension

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:904`

## symbiotic_fruit_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asymbiotic_fruit_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种“果实”与一种高度危险的杂食性植物保持共生关系。每一簇菌落都是这对有机体的思想中心，协调着它们复杂的防御行为模式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

This 'fruit' maintains a symbiotic relationship with a species of highly dangerous omnivorous plant. Each colony of fungus functions as the thought center for the organism pair, orchestrating its complex patterns of defensive behavior.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:905`

## tarblac_shimmer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atarblac_shimmer&lang=en)

### 中文

塔布拉克闪耀膜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

Tarblac Shimmer

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:907`

## tarblac_shimmer_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atarblac_shimmer_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

塔布拉克的一种闪闪发光的薄纱状膜片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

A sparkling veil-like membrane of a Tarblac

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:908`

## tarblac_shimmer_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atarblac_shimmer_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种生物创新性地使用灵能来捕猎和自我保护。它们通过施加一种压倒一切的恐惧感使猎物无法动弹，或以此恐吓掠食者。这个标本被保存在一个罐子里，里面装着它的原生水体，它的心灵感应能力已经丧失了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:693`

### English

This creature makes novel use of psionics to hunt and protect itself, inflicting a sense of overwhelming dread to immobilize prey and intimidate predators. This specimen is preserved in a jar of its native waters and has been rendered telepathically inert.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:909`

## terraforming_case_study

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_case_study&lang=en)

环境改造案例研究

### English

Terraforming Case Study

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:911`

## terraforming_case_study_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_case_study_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个成功在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]实施的环境改造项目的比例模型，展现了对[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetSpeciesName]母星的近乎完美的再现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:695`

### English

A scale model of the successful terraforming project on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], representing a near perfect recreation of the [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetSpeciesName] homeworld

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:912`

## terraforming_case_study_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_case_study_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

该行星现在生长着完全相同的生物群系，哪怕下至微观层面，甚至连[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetHomeWorldName]最独特的夸群系生态系统行为都完美复刻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

The planet is now populated by identical fauna down to the microscopic level, and even the most specific cross-biome ecosystems behave just as they do on [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetHomeWorldName].

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:913`

## terraforming_machinery

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_machinery&lang=en)

### 中文

环境改造机械

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

Terraforming Machinery

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:922`

## terraforming_machinery_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_machinery_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种古老的环境改造科技中使用的微型装置

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

Tiny devices used as part of an ancient form of terraforming technology

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:923`

## terraforming_machinery_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_machinery_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些微小的机器是设计用于散布到整个行星的表面上、对现有的有机物和矿物质应运更理想气候进行改造。这组机器人用于操控生态群系内的气候模式，并复制新的动植物以管理新造的生态系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

These miniature machines were designed to spread out across a planet's surface and convert the existing organic and mineral matter to suit a more desirable climate. Teams of these robots were used to manipulate weather patterns within biomes and replicate new plants and animals to manage the newly-crafted ecosystems.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:924`

## terraforming_records

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_records&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]环境改造记录

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Terraforming Records

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:926`

## terraforming_records_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_records_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]进行环境改造的流程中，所有生物形式都是行为同步的，从而形成了一个物流天堂。在这个天堂里，每一种动植物都在其生态系统中有着自己的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:707`

### English

All lifeforms on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] were behaviorally synchronized during its terraforming process, resulting in a logistical paradise where each species of flora and fauna served a specific purpose within its ecosystem

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:927`

## terraforming_records_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_records_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然这种类型的共生经常在进化过程中自然出现，但从未观见过如此极端的程度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

While this type of symbiotic situation often emerges naturally in the process of evolution, never before has it been observed to this extreme degree.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:928`

## the_final_sacrifice

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_final_sacrifice&lang=en)

### 中文

终极献祭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

The Final Sacrifice

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:930`

## the_final_sacrifice_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_final_sacrifice_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从挖掘出的数据水晶推断，这是泽珞族内战最后时刻的图像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:710`

### English

An image of the final moment of the Zroni civil war, extrapolated from excavated data crystals

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:931`

## the_final_sacrifice_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_final_sacrifice_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

一位孤独的福音派用尽整个物种的灵能来消灭被称为升格派的敌对派系，消除他们消耗掉整个宇宙的风险。所有这些飞升物种的都化为了我们成为泽珞的小型资源矿藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:711`

### English

A lone Savior harnessed the psionic power of their entire species to exterminate the rival faction known as the Divine, preventing them from potentially consuming the entire universe. All that now remains of this ascended species are small deposits of the resource we know as Zro.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:932`

## the_mask_of_transformation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_mask_of_transformation&lang=en)

### 中文

造化面具

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:712`

### English

The Mask of Transformation

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:934`

## the_mask_of_transformation_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_mask_of_transformation_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]上的巨大晶柱中保存着的一个类人面具

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:713`

### English

A humanoid mask found preserved in a magnificent crystal pillar on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:935`

## the_mask_of_transformation_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_mask_of_transformation_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它低鸣着灵能之力，靠近者会在意识中感受到某种存在。这个面具似乎是[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的卫星的某种能量通路，具有独自转动潮汐、改变行星的力量。它被封印在这个晶柱当中的原因不得而知，但我们推测应该是为了能够安全地向公众展示而为之。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:714`

### English

It hums with psionic power and some who approach it feel a presence within their mind. The mask itself seems to be a conduit for the power of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]'s moon, with the potential to turn tides and transform planets with its strength alone. Why it was encased within crystal can only be speculated, but we are mostly certain it is safe enough for public display.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:936`

## the_planet_orchestra

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_planet_orchestra&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]管乐队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

The [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Orchestra

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:938`

## the_planet_orchestra_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_planet_orchestra_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]当地的集体生命演奏的旋律

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

A melody played by the collective native life on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:939`

## the_planet_orchestra_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_planet_orchestra_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

经过广泛的分析，我们认为这种和声是由一个未知的演奏家引入该动物群的，目的是编码一个隐藏的信息。我们不确定他们的动机是秘密交流或纯粹是某种情感表达。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

After extensive analysis, we believe this harmony was introduced to the fauna by an unknown actor in order to encode a hidden message. We are unsure whether their motive was covert communication or an act of sentimentality.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:940`

## the_writer_sphere

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_writer_sphere&lang=en)

### 中文

写作者球体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

The Writer's Sphere

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:942`

## the_writer_sphere_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_writer_sphere_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个我们只知道它是一位叫“写作者”的高维存在送给我们的金色球体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

A golden sphere gifted to us by an extra-dimensional being only known as the Writer

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:943`

## the_writer_sphere_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_writer_sphere_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这件装置收集了海量的在跨纬度过程中产生的随机信息。它是文字具有超越一切边界的力量的证明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:720`

### English

This device gathers an immense quantity of random data created across dimensions. It is proof the power of the written word can transcend any boundary.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:944`

## tiny_ancient_sarcophagus

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atiny_ancient_sarcophagus&lang=en)

### 中文

远古微型石棺

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

Tiny Ancient Sarcophagus

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:946`

## tiny_ancient_sarcophagus_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atiny_ancient_sarcophagus_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一位数百万年前的精神领袖安眠的棺材

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:722`

### English

The burial chamber of an ancient spiritual leader from millions of years ago

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:947`

## tiny_ancient_sarcophagus_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atiny_ancient_sarcophagus_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它的尺寸只有十厘米。尽管其中的残骸大部分已经化为尘埃，但一个纯金的微型面具仍在其中完好无损，从不会被氧化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:723`

### English

It measures only ten centimeters in length. Though most of its remains are now dust, a tiny mask of pure gold remains intact, having never oxidized.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:948`

## tiyanki_radioactive_sac

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atiyanki_radioactive_sac&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇放射囊

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:724`

### English

Tiyanki Radioactive Sac

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:950`

## tiyanki_radioactive_sac_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atiyanki_radioactive_sac_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个在缇扬奇的生物膜上发现的小型多孔器官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:725`

### English

A small porous organ found within the membranes of a Tiyanki

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:951`

## tiyanki_radioactive_sac_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atiyanki_radioactive_sac_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这个发现揭示了缇杨绮摄入放射性水分子和行星气体作为额外的营养来源。一旦装满，这个囊就会成为该生物在太空深处是的临时放射性热源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:726`

### English

Its discovery revealed that Tiyanki consume irradiated water molecules alongside planetary gases as an additional source of nutrition. Once full, this sac becomes a temporary radioactive heat source for the creatures in the depths of space.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:952`

## torn_solar_sail

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atorn_solar_sail&lang=en)

### 中文

破损恒星帆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:727`

### English

Torn Solar Sail

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:954`

## torn_solar_sail_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atorn_solar_sail_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的轨道上发现，这面破损的光帆连接着一艘令人耳目一新的亚光速飞船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:728`

### English

Found orbiting [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName], this torn sail was attached to an innovative sublight vessel

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:955`

## torn_solar_sail_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atorn_solar_sail_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

过去它被一个未知文明的成员用于穿越[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]星系。它使用的科技已经严重过时了，但建造中使用到的一些工程技术此前从未设想过。其主“帆”上的巨大裂缝可能导致了它的旅程提早结束了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:729`

### English

It was used to travel across the [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName] system by members of an unknown civilization. The technology itself has been long obsolete, but some of the engineering techniques used in its construction have been previously unconsidered. The massive gash in its main 'sail' likely ended its journey prematurely.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:956`

## toxic_bio_engine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxic_bio_engine&lang=en)

### 中文

剧毒生物引擎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

Toxic Bio-Engine

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:958`

## toxic_bio_engine_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxic_bio_engine_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

发现时这些半生物机械正在对[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]进行污染，使其对多数生命呈现毒性

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:731`

### English

These semi-biological machines were found rendering the atmosphere of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] toxic to most life

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:959`

## toxic_bio_engine_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxic_bio_engine_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它们能独立工作，使其有害气体的排放适用于远程工业运作。这些机器的加油机制非常有意思，它们可以回收附近的生物质，利用化学提取过程将其转化为可用的能源。目前尚不清楚它们的生物整合是否从最初的构建开始就存在，还是随着时间的推移逐渐发展而来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:732`

### English

Their ability to function independently makes their noxious emissions suitable for remote industrial work. The machines possess a clever refueling mechanism that recycles nearby biomass, using a chemical extraction process to convert it into usable energy. It is unclear if their biological integrations were present from their initial construction or developed over time.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:960`

## tree_of_life_sample

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atree_of_life_sample&lang=en)

### 中文

“生命树”标本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:733`

### English

"Tree of Life" Sample

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:962`

## tree_of_life_sample_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atree_of_life_sample_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这些枝条是来自一个被称为“生命树”的东西

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:734`

### English

These clippings are from what has been called a 'tree of life'

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:963`

## tree_of_life_sample_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atree_of_life_sample_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它的汁液奇异地漂浮在太空中，其恢复活力的能力也相当神奇。尽管我们尽了最大的努力，但还是没能移植活这段剪枝，也没能再生出一个新的稀有物种标本。不管最初的种植者是谁，他们一定拥有非常先进的园艺技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:735`

### English

Found floating anomalously through space, the rejuvenative properties of its sap are fairly miraculous. Despite our best efforts, we have not been able to graft this clipping or regrow a new specimen of the rare species. The original cultivators, whoever they were, must have been privy to some very advanced horticultural techniques.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:964`

## tricorne_of_aussan

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atricorne_of_aussan&lang=en)

### 中文

奥桑三角帽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:736`

### English

Tricorne of Aussan

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:966`

## tricorne_of_aussan_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atricorne_of_aussan_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个被遗忘的海盗共和国流行的三尖帽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:737`

### English

A tri-tipped hat made popular by a forgotten pirate republic

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:967`

## tricorne_of_aussan_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atricorne_of_aussan_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

奥桑是一颗居住着[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetPreFTLLower]文明的小星球，它在不自知的情况下为一支通缉海盗舰队“布尔决斗者”提供了避难所。为了躲避星系当局的追捕，“决斗者”建造了一座小型纺织厂，用当地的纤维植物制作奢华的织物和服装，以在藏匿期间维生。后来，“决斗者”们改名为“布尔设计师”，成为一群商业游牧民族，以其令人生畏的销售代表而闻名。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

Aussan was a small planet populated by a [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetPreFTLLower] civilization that unknowingly provided refuge to a wanted pirate fleet, the Bourre Brawlers. Lying low from the galactic authorities, the Brawlers built a small textile mill to fund their time in hiding, using native fibrous plants to create luxurious fabrics and clothing. The Brawlers later became known as the Bourre Couturiers, mercantile nomads known for their oddly intimidating sales representatives.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:968`

## triggerless_gun

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atriggerless_gun&lang=en)

### 中文

武器商的无扳机枪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:739`

### English

Arms Dealer's Triggerless Gun

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:970`

## triggerless_gun_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atriggerless_gun_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这种武器是在绰尔的罗盘的武器经销商短暂的第二次统治期间设计的

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:740`

### English

This weapon was designed during the short-lived second reign of the Arms Broker on Chor's Compass

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:971`

## triggerless_gun_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atriggerless_gun_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

在那时候，行商的擎天堡更像是一个武器交易论坛，而不是一个赌场，里面充斥着各种各样的武器贩子。那时候，为了防止小偷小摸的行为，这些武器会被卖给潜在的买家，但会将扳机去除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

At that time, the Caravaneer's Citadel was closer to a weapons trading forum than a casino, with blasters smithed in-house and home to a variety of resident arms dealers. At the time, weapons were presented to prospective buyers with the trigger removed in an attempt to discourage incidents of petty theft.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:972`

## uddloran_pelts

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auddloran_pelts&lang=en)

### 中文

乌杜兰毛皮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

Uddloran Pelts

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:975`

## uddloran_pelts_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auddloran_pelts_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的乌杜兰物种身上剥下的奢华毛皮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

Luxurious furs harvested from the Uddloran species of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:976`

## uddloran_pelts_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auddloran_pelts_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些灵活的杂食动物会把自己紧紧地抱成一颗球，成群结队地在这颗行星白雪皑皑的地表上滚动，看起来就像活的雪崩一般。这种策略既用于狩猎，也用于迁徙。一群乌杜兰通常分为两组：一组是在这片土地上摧枯拉朽开路的猎手，另一组是紧随其后的采集者，它们会停下来收集被压扁的猎物和连根拔起的植物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

These flexible omnivores wrap themselves into tight balls and roll across the planet's snowy surface in large groups. Akin to a living avalanche, this tactic is used for both hunting and migration. A pack of Uddlorans is typically divided into two groups: hunters who carve a destructive path through the landscape, and gatherers who follow behind, stopping to collect flattened prey and uprooted plants.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:977`

## underworld_cantina_sign

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunderworld_cantina_sign&lang=en)

### 中文

地下酒吧招牌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:746`

### English

Underworld Cantina Sign

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:979`

## underworld_cantina_sign_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunderworld_cantina_sign_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

还能工作的数字招牌，取自大约两百万年前走私者光顾的违法酒吧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:747`

### English

Still-functioning digital signage from an illicit cantina used by smugglers some two million years ago

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:980`

## underworld_cantina_sign_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunderworld_cantina_sign_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

在一颗被挖空的小行星上发现的，这个地方可能是第一联盟统治时期蓬勃发展的犯罪组织的常规藏身之处。这里展示的这块照片可能是某种暗号，表明这里是通缉犯的避风港，或者可能只是某种饮料的广告。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:748`

### English

Found within a hollowed-out asteroid, this establishment would have been a routine hideout for the criminal organizations that thrived during the rule of the First League. The symbols shown here are believed to be a coded marker that this establishment was a safe-haven for wanted individuals, or perhaps an advertisement for a particular drink.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:981`

## unfinished_manifesto

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunfinished_manifesto&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]未完宣言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:749`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Unfinished Manifesto

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:983`

## unfinished_manifesto_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunfinished_manifesto_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一份未完成的宣言，出自[EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]的[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]领袖之手

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:750`

### English

An incomplete manifesto written by the leader of the [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] of [EVENT_TARGET_1.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:984`

## unfinished_manifesto_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunfinished_manifesto_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种文风让人想起古代的宗教文本，使用复古的词汇和语法，试图让自己看起来更像一回事。经文中所宣扬的一个关键信息是面对失败时的骄傲，鼓励信徒在面对即将到来的俘虏时献出自己的生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:751`

### English

The style is reminiscent of ancient religious texts, using old-fashioned vocabulary and syntax in an attempt to seem more legitimate. A key message preached in the text is pride in the face of defeat, encouraging followers to martyr themselves when faced with imminent capture.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:985`

## uranium_glassware

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auranium_glassware&lang=en)

### 中文

铀玻璃器皿

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:752`

### English

Uranium Glassware

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:987`

## uranium_glassware_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auranium_glassware_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

用铀石英雕琢出的装饰品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

A decorative piece carved from uranium quartz

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:988`

## uranium_glassware_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auranium_glassware_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

装饰物诱人的外观与未提炼铀的危险形成鲜明对比。作为限量收藏的一部分，在工匠完成这件作品后，它的大部分放射性特性都被中和了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

The appetizing appearance of the ornament contrasts with the danger associated with unrefined uranium. Part of a limited collection, the trinket was neutralized of most of its radioactive properties after the artisan had finished the piece.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:989`

## vault_of_polaris

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avault_of_polaris&lang=en)

### 中文

勾陈一庇护所

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

Vault of Polaris

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:991`

## vault_of_polaris_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avault_of_polaris_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

勾陈一原生植物和细菌的详尽收集

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:756`

### English

An exhaustive collection of the native plants and bacteria of Polaris

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:992`

## vault_of_polaris_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avault_of_polaris_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

容器上的铭文表明，这个庇护所是为了保护那些可能在永冻土融化后无法在全球变暖中幸存下来的物种。这颗星球曾经被厚厚的积雪覆盖，气候寒冷，但现在只剩下一座冰山。它的创造者的远见卓识使我们现在可以把他们的努力展示给所有人学习。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:757`

### English

The container's inscription reveals that this vault was created to preserve the species that may not survive the predicted global warming after the melting of its permafrost. The planet was once covered in thick snow with a cold climate, but now all that remains is a sole iceberg. The foresight of its creators allows us now to place their efforts on display for all to study.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:993`

## veil_of_selene

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aveil_of_selene&lang=en)

### 中文

塞勒涅的面纱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:758`

### English

Veil of Selene

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:995`

## veil_of_selene_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aveil_of_selene_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一件令人兴奋和陶醉的面部饰品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:759`

### English

A stimulating and intoxicating mask

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:996`

## veil_of_selene_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aveil_of_selene_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这层面纱不可貌相，其中充满了独特的感官特性，使它能够轻轻地压倒视觉、嗅觉和听觉的生物受体。人们相信戴这种头饰可以迷惑对方，使他们更容易受到暗示。据传原来的主人有一定的灵能能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:760`

### English

More than it seems, this veil is imbued with unique sensory properties that allow it to gently overwhelm visual, olfactory, and acoustic biological receptors. It is believed that this headwear was worn to disorient those who encountered it and make them more susceptible to suggestion. It is rumored that the original owner had some amount of psionic capability.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:997`

## verdingo_tendril_hook

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Averdingo_tendril_hook&lang=en)

### 中文

佛丁戈卷须钩

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

Verdingo Tendril Hook

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:999`

## verdingo_tendril_hook_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Averdingo_tendril_hook_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

佛丁戈海盗船长海斯波利·华勒里安的假肢钩

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

The prosthetic hook of Verdingo pirate captain Hespori Valerian

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1000`

## verdingo_tendril_hook_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Averdingo_tendril_hook_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

佛丁戈是一群亦商亦盗的植物类，他们追债和掠夺货船的可能性一样大。海斯波利船长在受雇领导一次地面军事进攻时失去了他的惯用卷须。在胜利之后，船长用自己的功勋章锻造了这个钩子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

The Verdingos were a group of Plantoids that bridged the gap between piracy and mercenaries, as likely to collect debts as they were to capture cargo ships. Captain Hespori lost their dominant tendril while hired to lead a military ground assault. Following their victory, the Captain forged this hook from the very medals awarded for their role in the battle.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1001`

## vibrating_crystal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avibrating_crystal&lang=en)

### 中文

颤动水晶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:764`

### English

Vibrating Crystal

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1003`

## vibrating_crystal_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avibrating_crystal_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]地表四处可见的独特石英矿物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:765`

### English

A unique quartz mineral found distributed throughout the surface of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1004`

## vibrating_crystal_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avibrating_crystal_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它与宿主行星的潜在共振创造了一个场，可以抚慰有机物的心灵，甚至也可以吸引合成人。这种共振是由微放射性的细菌菌落产生的，晶体在菌落周围形成。这些磁性微生物产生一种内部电流，使晶体以一致的高频率振动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:766`

### English

Its latent resonance with its host planet creates a field that soothes the mind of organics and attracts synthetics alike. This resonance is created by mildly radioactive bacterial colonies around which the crystal has formed. These magnetic microorganisms generate an internal current that causes the crystal to vibrate at consistently high frequencies.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1005`

## voidworm_amniotics

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avoidworm_amniotics&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空虫羊膜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:767`

### English

Voidworm Amniotics

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1007`

## voidworm_amniotics_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avoidworm_amniotics_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一种发现于虚空三头虫中心的胶状物质

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

A gelatinous substance found at the center of a Voidworm troika

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1008`

## voidworm_amniotics_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avoidworm_amniotics_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这种粘稠的液体在三个个体之间形成保护屏障，让细胞得以交换从而进行繁殖和交流。行星轰击产生的激素被储存在这里，使这种雌雄同体的生物能安全地释放配子并产生后代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:769`

### English

This viscous slime forms a protective barrier between the three organisms, allowing for the exchange of cells for reproductive and communicative purposes. Hormones produced from the bombardment of planets are stored here, allowing the hermaphroditic creatures to safely release their gametes and produce offspring.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1009`

## voidworm_heart

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avoidworm_heart&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空虫心脏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

Voidworm Heart

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1011`

## voidworm_heart_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avoidworm_heart_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空虫的心脏分成三个主要心室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:771`

### English

Voidworm hearts are divided into three main chambers

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1012`

## voidworm_heart_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avoidworm_heart_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

每个心室都会依次提升循环系统的压力，维持液体在沸点以下以确保在真空中的生存。虚空虫的皮肤大部分是密封的，但随着时间的推移，各种消化和生殖孔会泄出一些宝贵的压力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:772`

### English

Each chamber increases the pressure of its circulatory system in sequence, maintaining its fluids below their boiling point to ensure survival in the vacuum of space. While the skin of a voidworm is for the most part hermetically sealed, various digestive and reproductive orifices surrender some amount of valuable pressure over time.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1013`

## vultaum_religious_pamphlet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_religious_pamphlet&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆传教手册

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:773`

### English

Vultaum Religious Pamphlet

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1015`

## vultaum_religious_pamphlet_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_religious_pamphlet_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆传教士分发的小册子，其中试图揭露虚拟现实设备是最大的罪恶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:774`

### English

Distributed by Vultaum missionaries, this pamphlet attempts to expose Virtual Reality devices as the greatest sin

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1016`

## vultaum_religious_pamphlet_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_religious_pamphlet_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它将“大谎言”作为一个关键概念，声称任何形式的沉浸式逃避主义都类似于摧毁自己现实的意志。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:775`

### English

It refers to "The Great Lie" as a key concept, painting any form of immersive escapism akin to a will to destroy one's own reality.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1017`

## vultaum_vr_video_game

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_vr_video_game&lang=en)

### 中文

沃陶姆虚拟现实视频游戏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:776`

### English

Vultaum VR Video Game

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1019`

## vultaum_vr_video_game_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_vr_video_game_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

曾经在久远时代被数十亿沃陶姆人玩过的多人虚拟现实视频游戏中使用的数字界面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:777`

### English

The digital interface used in a multi-user virtual reality video game once played by billions of Vultaum individuals in a bygone era

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1020`

## vultaum_vr_video_game_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avultaum_vr_video_game_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些控制器针对他们的生物特征进行了专门的研发，它们包含数千个振动感受器，能够检测到极其细微的运动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:778`

### English

Uniquely attuned to their biology, the controls consist of an array of thousands of vibrational receptors capable of detecting extremely subtle movements.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1021`

## warning_monolith

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awarning_monolith&lang=en)

### 中文

警示碑塔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:779`

### English

Warning Monolith

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1023`

## warning_monolith_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awarning_monolith_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一块由已灭绝物种最后成员留下的雕刻巨石，作为信息传递。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

A carved monolith left as a message by one of the last members of an extinct species

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1024`

## warning_monolith_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awarning_monolith_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它试图劝阻任何发现这颗星球遗迹的人不要寻找导致他们种族灭亡的终极武器。语气相当戏剧化，但考虑到当时的状况，是可以理解的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:781`

### English

It attempts to dissuade anyone who discovers the remains of this planet from seeking the ultimate weapon that had led to the death of their race. It is quite melodramatic in tone, but this can be excused given the circumstances.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1025`

## wormhole_anchor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awormhole_anchor&lang=en)

### 中文

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]虫洞锚

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:782`

### English

[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName] Wormhole Anchor

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1027`

## wormhole_anchor_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awormhole_anchor_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

用于从黑洞中收集暗物质的单向虫洞装置的锚点

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:783`

### English

The anchor point of a one-way wormhole device used to harvest Dark Matter from a black hole

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1028`

## wormhole_anchor_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awormhole_anchor_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它充其量只能说是拼凑而成，完全由来自多个文明的废弃物和残骸建造。该装置背后的科学远远超出了用于建造它的工程能力，利用了一种巧妙的技术来操纵负质量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:784`

### English

Its construction is haphazard at best, built entirely from salvage and scrap from several civilizations. The science behind the device goes far beyond the engineering ability used to construct it, utilizing an elegant technique for manipulating negative mass.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1029`

## wormhole_fur

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awormhole_fur&lang=en)

### 中文

虫洞毫毛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:785`

### English

Wormhole Fur

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1031`

## wormhole_fur_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awormhole_fur_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从星界裂隙另一侧的一种狐狸身上采集的生物样本

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:786`

### English

Biological samples taken from vulpines from beyond an Astral Rift

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1032`

## wormhole_fur_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awormhole_fur_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些哺乳动物有一种基因突变，使它们能够随意产生虫洞。据推测，这种能力是在无数次元间旅行后进化而来的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:787`

### English

These mammalians have a genetic mutation that allows them to generate wormholes at will. It is speculated that this ability evolved after countless millennia of travel between dimensions.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1033`

## ytterite_preserved_corpse

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aytterite_preserved_corpse&lang=en)

### 中文

保存的乙特人尸体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:788`

### English

Ytterite Preserved Corpse

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1035`

## ytterite_preserved_corpse_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aytterite_preserved_corpse_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

在他们的母星被劫持的地爆天星武器摧毁后，唯一幸存的前超光速乙特人物种样本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:789`

### English

The sole surviving specimen of the Pre-FTL Ytterite species after their homeworld was destroyed by a hijacked World Cracker

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1036`

## ytterite_preserved_corpse_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aytterite_preserved_corpse_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它们的消化系统将摄入的矿物质转化为光子，内部器官类似于围绕熔融核心排列的复杂折射晶体簇。这个生物的一条下肢上刻着一些形状的雕刻，作为某种文化意义上的身体改造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:790`

### English

Their digestive system converted consumed minerals into photons, with internal organs resembling complex clusters of refractive crystals arranged around a molten core. This particular being had a series of shaped carvings in one of its lower limbs as some form of culturally significant body modification.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1037`

## ytterite_kaleidoscopic_fascinator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aytterite_kaleidoscopic_fascinator&lang=en)

### 中文

乙特人万花筒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:791`

### English

Ytterite Kaleidoscopic Fascinator

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1039`

## ytterite_kaleidoscopic_fascinator_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aytterite_kaleidoscopic_fascinator_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

由乙特人创造的装饰性饰品，作为视觉刺激

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:792`

### English

A decorative ornament created by the Ytterite as a visual stimulus

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1040`

## ytterite_kaleidoscopic_fascinator_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aytterite_kaleidoscopic_fascinator_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些装饰品将在阳光充足的地方展示，可能包含数百甚至数千种独特的图案。它们由层层薄薄的雕刻水晶制成，一位训练有素的工匠可能需要花费数月时间才能制作出像这样复杂的装饰品。工匠们会在装饰品的关键顶点处附上他们自己脱落的甲壳雕刻而成的一块作为“签名”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:793`

### English

Fascinators would be displayed in sunlit areas and could contain hundreds or even thousands of unique patterns. Crafted from layers of thinly carved crystal, a trained artisan could spend months creating a fascinator as complex as this one. Artisans would 'sign' their work by attaching a carved piece of their own shedded mantle to the object at a key vertex.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1041`

## ytterite_royal_scepter

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aytterite_royal_scepter&lang=en)

### 中文

乙特人皇家节杖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:794`

### English

Ytterite Royal Scepter

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1043`

## ytterite_royal_scepter_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aytterite_royal_scepter_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这根权杖曾被格奈斯·达慕尔的皇家大臣使用，格奈斯·达慕尔是一座雕刻在山上的巨大雕像，乙特人认为它是一尊沉睡的神灵，也是他们唯一的真主。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:795`

### English

This scepter was wielded by the Royal Minister to the Gneiss Damur; an enormous statue carved into a mountain that the Ytterites believed was a dormant deity and their one true monarch

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1044`

## ytterite_royal_scepter_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aytterite_royal_scepter_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

他们在等待雕像苏醒的时刻，进行了选举，选出了代理领导人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:796`

### English

They carried out elections to select proxy leaders as they waited for the statue's great awakening.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1045`

## yuht_audio_console

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_audio_console&lang=en)

### 中文

尤特音频控制台

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:797`

### English

Yuht Audio Console

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1047`

## yuht_audio_console_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_audio_console_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个曾经控制由尤特人设计的庞大音频监控阵列的模块

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:798`

### English

A module that once controlled an enormous audio surveillance array designed by the Yuht

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1048`

## yuht_audio_console_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_audio_console_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这项技术是为了帮助尤特人寻找地外生命而开发的，希望能够接收来自遥远星系的外部信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:799`

### English

This technology was developed to facilitate the Yuht's search for extra-terrestrial life, hoping to pick up foreign transmissions from systems away.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1049`

## yuht_cryo_pod

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_cryo_pod&lang=en)

### 中文

尤特冷冻仓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:800`

### English

Yuht Cryo Pod

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1051`

## yuht_cryo_pod_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_cryo_pod_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这个古老的休眠舱长96米，里面保存着来自巨大尤特物种的个体遗骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:801`

### English

At ninety-six meters in length, this ancient stasis pod contains preserved remains of an individual from the massive Yuht species

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1052`

## yuht_cryo_pod_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_cryo_pod_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

尤特人拥有极长的寿命，并且可以通过冷冻休眠将寿命延长数倍。研究表明，这种节肢动物物种很可能是雌雄同体的，能够根据群体临时需求调整其生殖系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:802`

### English

Yuht had extremely long lifespans, and could extend it by several magnitudes with the use of cryostasis. It has been determined that this arthropoid species was likely hermaphroditic, capable of adapting their reproductive systems to match the temporary needs of the colony.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1053`

## yuht_crystal_skull

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_crystal_skull&lang=en)

### 中文

尤特水晶头骨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:803`

### English

Yuht Crystal Skull

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1055`

## yuht_crystal_skull_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_crystal_skull_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

古代尤特人曾一度认为这颗水晶头骨是真正的外星文物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:804`

### English

A crystal skull that the ancient Yuht once believed to be a genuine alien artifact

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1056`

## yuht_crystal_skull_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ayuht_crystal_skull_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然这件伪造物品的来源最终被揭穿，但在尤特新闻媒体热衷地报道并将其推向名声之前，它已经声名大噪。许多“发现”这类伪造文物的人成为了小有名气的名人，并成为了媒体人物，激励着其他人效仿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:805`

### English

The origin of this fraudulent item was eventually debunked, but not before the eager Yuht news media ran with its story and brought it fame. Many of those who 'discovered' false artifacts such as this one became minor celebrities and media personalities, incentivizing others to do the same.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1057`

## zombie_probe

僵尸探机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:806`

### English

Zombie Probe

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1059`

## zombie_probe_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azombie_probe_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

一个未知的外星文明获取并改造了一艘探测器的残骸，赋予了它意识

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:807`

### English

The remains of an exploratory probe taken and augmented by an unknown alien organization, granting it sentience

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1060`

## zombie_probe_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azombie_probe_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

它在萌芽的意识中攻击了它的创造者，并被理所应当地消灭了。它是否曾感受过痛苦或理解死亡的概念，尚不清楚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:808`

### English

In its budding consciousness, it attacked its creator and was duly dispatched. It is uncertain if it ever knew pain or understood the concept of death.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1061`

Zone A Sedative

## zone_a_sedative_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azone_a_sedative_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

从[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]标注为“A区”的区域取的一铲土

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:810`

### English

A scoop of earth taken from the area designated as "Zone A" on [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1064`

## zone_a_sedative_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azone_a_sedative_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这片占地169平方公里的区域，保存着来自未知来源的外星访客遗留的废弃设备和技术。其中一些设备极其危险，但土壤本身却含有化学镇静剂，这使得研究人员能够在几乎没有压力的情况下处理它们。无论在那里进行过什么操作，都早已被遗忘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:811`

### English

This 169 square kilometer division of land harbors abandoned gadgets and technology from previous alien visitors of unknown origin. Some of these devices are extremely dangerous, but the very soil itself is laced with a chemical sedative that would have allowed researchers to handle them with little stress. Whatever operation was undertaken there has long been forgotten. 

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1065`

## zroni_war_banners

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_war_banners&lang=en)

泽珞族战旗

### English

Zroni War Banners
