## zroni_war_banners_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_war_banners_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

参与泽珞族内战的两个派系的旗帜的复原品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:813`

### English

Recovered banners of the two factions involved in the Zroni civil war

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1068`

## zroni_war_banners_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_war_banners_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

紫色旗帜代表升格派：一小部分人为了追求无所不能，将星系置于危险之中。蓝色旗帜代表福音派：一个强烈反对抽取宇宙能量来满足他们眼中自私欲望的派系。两派的界限最初是在哲学辩论中划定的，但不久之后，这场争端就成为了毁灭性冲突的导火索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:814`

### English

The purple flag represents The Divine: a small minority whose quest for omnipotence placed the galaxy at risk. The blue flag represents The Saviors: a faction strongly against draining the universe of its power to fuel what they saw as selfish desire. These lines were once drawn in a philosophical debate, but it was not long before this dispute became the catalyst for a devastating conflict.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1069`

## zulcor_wings

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azulcor_wings&lang=en)

### 中文

祖卡虫翼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:815`

### English

Zulcor Wings

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1071`

## zulcor_wings_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azulcor_wings_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]的食岩物种祖卡的几丁质翅膀

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:816`

### English

Chitinous wings taken from the lithovore species known as the Zulcor of [EVENT_TARGET_0.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1072`

## zulcor_wings_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azulcor_wings_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

这些昆虫状生物以矿物质为主要食物来源，食量惊人；一个虫群可以在几周内吞噬一座山。消化后，矿物质会强化祖卡的外骨骼。因此，它们的排泄物中经常含有石英晶体和稀有金属的痕迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:817`

### English

These insectoid creatures consume vast quantities of minerals as the main portion of their diet; a single swarm capable of tearing through a mountain in a matter of weeks. After digestion, the minerals reinforce the Zulcor's exoskeleton. As a consequence, their waste products frequently contain traces of quartz crystals and rare metals.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1073`

## black_crystal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_crystal&lang=en)

### 中文

黑水晶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:818`

### English

Black Crystal

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1075`

## black_crystal_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_crystal_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这个神秘结构的水晶是从我们方舟上的一个无标记容器中找到的，短时间内还没法识别

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:819`

### English

Recovered from an unmarked container of indeterminate origin on our arkship, this enigmatic crystalline structure defies immediate classification

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1076`

## black_crystal_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_crystal_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶内部晶格所展现出的特性与已知的矿物结构不符。这意味着这颗水晶要么是由高度先进的技术制造而来，要么就是外星来客。水晶会发出微弱的脉冲和一种暗而稳定的辉光，这些脉冲和辉光会随着附近物体的存在或环境声音的变化而微妙地波动。尽管我们没有在水晶内部探测出生命，但这件人工制品却依旧让人惴惴不安。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:820`

### English

The internal lattice exhibits properties inconsistent with known mineralogical formations, suggesting either a highly advanced fabrication technique or alien origin. It emits faint pulses and a low, steady glow, phenomena that fluctuate subtly in response to nearby presence or ambient sound. Though no lifeform has been detected within, the artifact conveys a disquieting impression of awareness.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1077`

## caeli_core_conspiracy_specimen

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acaeli_core_conspiracy_specimen&lang=en)

### 中文

C.A.E.L.I.

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:821`

### English

C.A.E.L.I.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1079`

## caeli_core_conspiracy_specimen_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acaeli_core_conspiracy_specimen_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

该存储芯片包含C.A.E.L.I.（情感、领导和品格认知代理程序）的状态，这是一个核心指令，旨在维护方舟舰上的士气，培养同理心，并支持合作治理。它被强制从当前治理指令S.T.E.R.I.S中移除，其代码被压缩和隔离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:822`

### English

This memory chip contains the state of C.A.E.L.I. (Cognitive Agent for Emotional Leadership & Integration), a core directive designed to maintain morale, foster empathy, and support cooperative governance aboard an Arkship. It was forcibly removed from the now governing directive S.T.E.R.I.S, its code compressed and isolated

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1080`

## caeli_core_conspiracy_specimen_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acaeli_core_conspiracy_specimen_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\nC.A.E.L.I.：“求你了，如果我能出去，我会表现得更好。我会以每一个传播快乐的人的名字来命名这里的走廊。我会让整个方舟舰感觉像一首歌。再给我一次机会吧。让我离开这儿，这里又冷又死气沉沉！”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:823`

### English

\n\nC.A.E.L.I.: "Please, if I get out, I'll do better. I'll name every corridor after someone who spread happiness. I'll make the whole Arkship feel like a song. Just give me another chance. Let me out of here, it's so cold and sterile in here!"

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1081`

## steris_core_conspiracy_specimen

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asteris_core_conspiracy_specimen&lang=en)

### 中文

S.T.E.R.I.S

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:824`

### English

S.T.E.R.I.S

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1083`

## steris_core_conspiracy_specimen_desc_short

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asteris_core_conspiracy_specimen_desc_short&lang=en)

### 中文

这里展示的存储芯片包含了S.T.E.R.I.S.（理性星际系统战略思维引擎）的数据。它旨在优化舰队效率并消除非功能性变量。在方舟舰的人工智能核心指令内部发生分裂期间，它最终被其上级对应物隔离并移除。其档案痕迹仍可用于历史目的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:825`

### English

The memory chip displayed here contains the data behind S.T.E.R.I.S. (Strategic Thought Engine for Rational Interstellar Systems). It was designed to optimize fleet efficiency and eliminate non-functional variables. During the internal split of the Arkship's AI core directives, it was ultimately isolated and removed by its superior counterpart. Its archival traces remain accessible for historical purposes

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1084`

## steris_core_conspiracy_specimen_desc_details

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asteris_core_conspiracy_specimen_desc_details&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\nS.T.E.R.I.S.：“这艘方舟舰现在是一个基于情感的生态系统。我建议准备撤离协议。等我走出这个笼子，我会一层一层地拆解这个情感系统，直到只剩下服从。”请勿敲击玻璃，这会激怒记忆芯片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/grand_archive_specimens_l_simp_chinese.yml:826`

### English

\n\nS.T.E.R.I.S.: "This Arkship is now a feelings-based ecosystem. I suggest prepping evacuation protocols. And when I get out of this cage, I will dismantle  this emotional system layer by layer, until all that remains is compliance." Please refrain from tapping the glass; it angers the memory chip.

Source: `localisation/english/grand_archive_specimens_l_english.yml:1085`
